===== BEGIN FILE: examples/consume_local_api.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 1c7e1bc928a56253b8fdb74f56047e33fdf013d9

"""Authenticated local GazeSpace 0.2 HTTP consumer; stdlib HTTP plus shared clock.

Start tracking and the Integration API in Studio, then copy its token:

    $env:GAZESPACE_API_TOKEN = 'PASTE_THE_CURRENT_DESKTOP_TOKEN'
    python examples/consume_local_api.py --once
    python examples/consume_local_api.py

Importing this module makes no requests and starts no threads. Call poll() on a
worker; marshal its result to your existing UI. Clear markers on every invalid
update and when fresh_until_monotonic_s expires on the UI's own timer. The
deadline uses gazespace.clock.host_now_s() in this consumer; use that same
clock in the UI deadline check. Do not compare the server's source
timestamp directly against an unrelated clock. The token is read from the
environment and is never printed. No raw
camera image, recording or system cursor action is requested by this example.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from copy import deepcopy
import json
import math
import os
import time
from gazespace.clock import HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, host_now_s
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from urllib.request import HTTPRedirectHandler, ProxyHandler, Request, build_opener


def unavailable(reason: str) -> dict:
    return {"valid": False, "screen_valid": False, "spatial_valid": False,
            "surface_hit": False, "new_frame": False, "reason": reason,
            "gaze_screen_px": None, "surface_name": None, "surface_xy_m": None,
            "surface_xy_px": None, "ray_origin_world_m": None,
            "ray_direction_world": None, "capture_timestamp_s": None,
            "received_monotonic_s": None, "fresh_until_monotonic_s": None}


def _vector(value, count: int) -> bool:
    return (isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) and len(value) == count
            and all(isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool)
                    and math.isfinite(v) for v in value))


def _reject_nonfinite(value: str):
    raise ValueError(f"Nonfinite JSON number: {value}")


class _NoRedirects(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        raise HTTPError(req.full_url, code, "Redirects are not accepted", headers, fp)


class LocalGazeClient:
    def __init__(self, token: str, *, base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8765",
                 timeout_s: float = 0.25, max_age_s: float = 0.4):
        url = urlsplit(base_url)
        if (url.scheme != "http" or url.hostname not in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1")
                or url.username or url.password or url.query or url.fragment
                or url.path not in ("", "/")):
            raise ValueError("Use the local HTTP server root, e.g. http://127.0.0.1:8765")
        if not isinstance(token, str) or len(token) < 16 or "\r" in token or "\n" in token:
            raise ValueError("Provide the current desktop bearer token (at least 16 characters)")
        if not all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in (timeout_s, max_age_s)):
            raise ValueError("Timeout and maximum age must be finite and positive")
        if timeout_s > max_age_s:
            raise ValueError("Request timeout must not exceed the freshness limit")
        self._url = base_url.rstrip("/") + "/v1/gaze"
        self._token = token
        self.timeout_s, self.max_age_s = timeout_s, max_age_s
        # Do not send a loopback token through an environment-configured proxy
        # or follow a redirect that could change its destination.
        self._opener = build_opener(ProxyHandler({}), _NoRedirects())
        self._last_key = None
        self._last_new_frame_at: float | None = None

    def poll(self) -> dict:
        started = host_now_s()
        request = Request(self._url, headers={
            "Authorization": "Bearer " + self._token,
            "Accept": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
        })
        try:
            with self._opener.open(request, timeout=self.timeout_s) as response:
                payload = json.load(response, parse_constant=_reject_nonfinite)
        except HTTPError as exc:
            return unavailable(f"api_http_{exc.code}")
        except (URLError, TimeoutError, OSError, ValueError):
            return unavailable("api_connection_or_payload_error")
        finished = host_now_s()
        return self._select(payload, finished - started, finished)

    def _select(self, payload, round_trip_s: float, now: float) -> dict:
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            return unavailable("invalid_api_payload")
        if payload.get("state") != "running" or payload.get("running") is not True:
            return unavailable(str(payload.get("state", "runtime_unavailable")))
        if payload.get("last_error"):
            return unavailable("runtime_error")
        latest, metrics = payload.get("latest"), payload.get("metrics")
        if not isinstance(latest, dict) or not isinstance(metrics, dict):
            return unavailable("awaiting_result")
        age_ms = metrics.get("capture_age_ms")
        if (not isinstance(age_ms, (int, float)) or isinstance(age_ms, bool)
                or not math.isfinite(age_ms) or age_ms < 0
                or age_ms / 1000 + round_trip_s > self.max_age_s):
            return unavailable("stale_capture_or_slow_response")
        stamp = latest.get("capture_timestamp_s")
        if not isinstance(stamp, (int, float)) or isinstance(stamp, bool) or not math.isfinite(stamp):
            return unavailable("invalid_capture_timestamp")
        key = (stamp, latest.get("model_fingerprint"))
        new_frame = key != self._last_key
        if new_frame:
            self._last_key, self._last_new_frame_at = key, now
        elif self._last_new_frame_at is not None and now - self._last_new_frame_at > self.max_age_s:
            return unavailable("repeated_stale_result")
        if latest.get("valid") is not True:
            return unavailable(str(latest.get("reason", "invalid_estimate")))
        screen = latest.get("screen_valid") is True and _vector(latest.get("gaze_screen_px"), 2)
        spatial = (latest.get("spatial_valid") is True
                   and _vector(latest.get("ray_origin_world_m"), 3)
                   and _vector(latest.get("ray_direction_world"), 3))
        hit = (spatial and isinstance(latest.get("surface_name"), str)
               and bool(latest["surface_name"]) and _vector(latest.get("surface_xy_m"), 2))
        if not screen and not spatial:
            return unavailable("missing_finite_coordinates")
        camera = payload.get("camera") if isinstance(payload.get("camera"), dict) else {}
        return {
            "valid": True, "screen_valid": screen, "spatial_valid": spatial,
            "surface_hit": hit, "new_frame": new_frame,
            "reason": str(latest.get("reason", "estimated")),
            "quality": latest.get("quality"),  # heuristic quality, not accuracy probability
            "gaze_screen_px": deepcopy(latest["gaze_screen_px"]) if screen else None,
            "surface_name": latest["surface_name"] if hit else None,
            "surface_xy_m": deepcopy(latest["surface_xy_m"]) if hit else None,
            "surface_xy_px": deepcopy(latest.get("surface_xy_px"))
                if hit and _vector(latest.get("surface_xy_px"), 2) else None,
            "ray_origin_world_m": deepcopy(latest["ray_origin_world_m"]) if spatial else None,
            "ray_direction_world": deepcopy(latest["ray_direction_world"]) if spatial else None,
            "capture_timestamp_s": stamp,
            "capture_age_ms": age_ms,
            "request_round_trip_ms": round_trip_s * 1000,
            "received_monotonic_s": now,
            "host_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN,
            "fresh_until_monotonic_s": now + self.max_age_s - age_ms / 1000 - round_trip_s,
            "eye_origin_source": latest.get("eye_origin_source"),
            "source": latest.get("source"),
            "synthetic": bool(camera.get("synthetic") or camera.get("simulated")
                              or latest.get("eye_origin_source") == "synthetic_fixture"),
            "warnings": deepcopy(latest.get("warnings", [])),
        }


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8765")
    parser.add_argument("--token-env", default="GAZESPACE_API_TOKEN")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=0.25)
    parser.add_argument("--max-age", type=float, default=0.4)
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=0.05)
    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if not math.isfinite(args.interval) or args.interval <= 0:
        parser.error("--interval must be finite and positive")
    try:
        client = LocalGazeClient(os.environ.get(args.token_env, ""), base_url=args.url,
                                timeout_s=args.timeout, max_age_s=args.max_age)
    except ValueError as exc:
        parser.error(str(exc))
    try:
        while True:
            print(json.dumps(client.poll(), allow_nan=False), flush=True)
            if args.once:
                return 0
            time.sleep(args.interval)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print(json.dumps(unavailable("consumer_stopped")), flush=True)
        return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== BEGIN FILE: examples/integrate_existing_windows_app.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 4c5a6204914c686460cf280cbb467dd8c9f3b86d

"""Embed GazeSpace 0.2 in an existing Windows Python application.

Importing this module never opens a device, loads a face model, starts a worker
or records data. The host continues to own its camera and GUI. Construct the
adapter INSIDE the processing worker and close it on that same worker.

Supply current device/display settings, independently obtained from the host:

    source_context = {
        "camera_id": "0",              # selected UVC index, or actual serial
        "backend": "dshow",            # same convention used for calibration
        "subject_id": "operator-1",    # selected operator, not face identity
        "exposure_native": None,       # None means left unchanged, as in Studio
        "exposure_us": None,
        "gain": None,
        "ir_bit_depth": 8,
        "monitor": current_monitor_name,
        "screen_size": [current_pixel_width, current_pixel_height],
    }

The monitor variables are host integration values, not SDK functions. Include
any additional fields bound by your calibration (for example mirror or monitor
origin). Do not copy expected values from a saved profile as verification of
the current hardware. Recreate the adapter after changing those settings.

For a host-owned BGR camera, timestamp acquisition with
gazespace.clock.host_now_s() (time.perf_counter, not time.monotonic on Windows
Python 3.11/3.12), then pass that value to on_camera_frame. For aligned depth,
call process_frame with a Frame:
depth_m is HxW meters on the BGR grid and metadata contains depth_aligned_to=
"color", depth_intrinsics=CameraIntrinsics.to_dict(), and the truthful
depth_deprojection_supported flag. Preserve hardware_timestamp_s separately;
it must not be treated as a host monotonic timestamp without synchronization.

Copy vendor-owned image/depth buffers BEFORE enqueueing them. Feed only the
newest pending Frame through a bounded host queue. Use snapshot() from the UI
timer to clear a marker after its freshness deadline, including while a camera
read or inference call is blocked. Screen pixels are LOCAL to the calibrated
monitor; the host owns any Windows virtual-desktop/DPI transformation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
import math
from pathlib import Path
from threading import Event, Lock, get_ident
from gazespace.clock import HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, host_now_s
from typing import Callable, Mapping

import numpy as np

from gazespace.engine import GazeEngine
from gazespace.types import Frame


def _unavailable(reason: str) -> dict:
    """Every unavailable update explicitly clears both coordinate paths."""
    return {
        "status": "unavailable", "valid": False, "screen_valid": False,
        "spatial_valid": False, "surface_hit": False, "reason": reason,
        "quality": 0.0, "gaze_screen_px": None,
        "gaze_screen_normalized": None, "raw_gaze_screen_px": None,
        "ray_origin_world_m": None, "ray_direction_world": None,
        "surface_name": None, "surface_xy_m": None, "surface_xy_px": None,
        "capture_timestamp_s": None, "received_monotonic_s": None,
        "fresh_until_monotonic_s": None,
    }


class ExistingAppGazeAdapter:
    """One worker-owned engine with a thread-safe, freshness-aware UI snapshot."""

    def __init__(self, profile_path: str | Path, *, source_context: Mapping,
                 mode: str = "rgb", roi_normalized=None,
                 pupil_polarity: str = "dark", face_model: str | None = None,
                 max_age_s: float = 0.4):
        if not math.isfinite(max_age_s) or max_age_s <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_age_s must be finite and positive")
        current = deepcopy(dict(source_context))
        for key in ("camera_id", "backend", "subject_id"):
            if not isinstance(current.get(key), str) or not current[key].strip():
                raise ValueError(f"Provide the current {key} as a nonempty string")
        self._owner_thread = get_ident()
        self._lock = Lock()
        self._closed = False
        self._last_source_timestamp: float | None = None
        self._latest = _unavailable("awaiting_frame")
        self.max_age_s = float(max_age_s)
        self._engine = GazeEngine.from_profile(
            profile_path, mode=mode, roi_normalized=roi_normalized,
            pupil_polarity=pupil_polarity, face_model=face_model,
            source_context=current,
        )
        # The SDK permits headless omission; this consumer deliberately requires
        # actual display dimensions before publishing a screen estimate.
        if self._engine.screen_model is not None:
            size = current.get("screen_size")
            if (not isinstance(size, (tuple, list)) or len(size) != 2
                    or any(not isinstance(v, int) or isinstance(v, bool) or v < 2 for v in size)):
                self._engine.close()
                raise ValueError("A screen model requires the actual screen_size [width,height]")

    def _check_owner(self) -> None:
        if get_ident() != self._owner_thread:
            raise RuntimeError("Process/reset/close the adapter on its creating worker thread")
        if self._closed:
            raise RuntimeError("The adapter is closed")

    def _set_latest(self, value: dict) -> dict:
        with self._lock:
            self._latest = deepcopy(value)
        return deepcopy(value)

    def invalidate(self, reason: str = "paused") -> dict:
        """Call on the processing worker for pause, disconnect or host failure."""
        self._check_owner()
        self._engine.invalidate(reason)
        return self._set_latest(_unavailable(reason))

    def reset(self) -> dict:
        """Start a new source timeline after reconnect; context must be unchanged."""
        self._check_owner()
        self._last_source_timestamp = None
        self._engine.reset()
        return self._set_latest(_unavailable("awaiting_fresh_frame"))

    def on_camera_frame(self, bgr_frame: np.ndarray, captured_monotonic_s: float,
                        *, sequence: int = 0) -> dict:
        """Convenience for host-clock BGR input; timestamp it at acquisition.

        Do not replace the timestamp with the later time of dequeuing a frame.
        Use process_frame for a source clock, hardware timestamp or depth data.
        """
        return self.process_frame(Frame(
            image=bgr_frame, timestamp_s=captured_monotonic_s, sequence=sequence,
            source="existing_application",
            metadata={"host_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN},
            received_monotonic_s=captured_monotonic_s,
        ))

    def process_frame(self, frame: Frame) -> dict:
        """Process one owned BGR8 frame; keep its optional aligned depth intact."""
        self._check_owner()
        received = frame.received_monotonic_s
        now = host_now_s()
        if received is None or not math.isfinite(received):
            return self.invalidate("missing_host_receive_timestamp")
        if not math.isfinite(frame.timestamp_s):
            return self.invalidate("nonfinite_capture_timestamp")
        declared_clock = frame.metadata.get("host_clock_domain")
        if ((declared_clock is not None and declared_clock != HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN)
                or received > now):
            return self.invalidate("receive_timestamp_not_in_host_clock")
        if now - received > self.max_age_s:
            return self.invalidate("stale_queued_frame")
        if self._last_source_timestamp is not None and frame.timestamp_s <= self._last_source_timestamp:
            return self.invalidate("duplicate_or_reversed_capture_timestamp")
        self._last_source_timestamp = float(frame.timestamp_s)
        try:
            # process_frame, unlike dropping down to a bare BGR call, preserves
            # aligned depth and checks the origin source used by calibration.
            result = self._engine.process_frame(frame)
            differences = []
            for model in (self._engine.screen_model, self._engine.spatial_model):
                if model is not None:
                    differences.extend(model.context_mismatches(
                        result.tracking_context, require_present=True))
            if differences:
                value = self.invalidate("calibration_context_mismatch")
                value["context_mismatches"] = sorted(set(differences))
                return self._set_latest(value)
            if host_now_s() - received > self.max_age_s:
                return self.invalidate("stale_after_processing")
            value = result.as_dict()
            value.update(
                status="available" if result.valid else "unavailable",
                # A valid ray without a bounded hit cannot select a surface.
                surface_hit=bool(result.spatial_valid and result.surface_name is not None
                                 and result.surface_xy_m is not None),
                received_monotonic_s=float(received),
                fresh_until_monotonic_s=float(received) + self.max_age_s,
                host_clock_domain=HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN,
            )
            return self._set_latest(value)
        except Exception:
            self.invalidate("processing_error")
            raise

    def snapshot(self) -> dict:
        """Read from any thread; a UI timer should call this even without frames."""
        with self._lock:
            value = deepcopy(self._latest)
        deadline = value.get("fresh_until_monotonic_s")
        if deadline is not None and host_now_s() > deadline:
            return _unavailable("stale_capture")
        return value

    def close(self) -> None:
        if get_ident() != self._owner_thread:
            raise RuntimeError("Close the adapter on its creating worker thread")
        if self._closed:
            return
        try:
            self._engine.close()
        finally:
            self._closed = True
            self._set_latest(_unavailable("stopped"))

    def __enter__(self):
        self._check_owner()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        self.close()


def run_gaze_worker(*, next_frame: Callable[[float], Frame | None],
                    publish: Callable[[dict], None], stop_event: Event,
                    profile_path: str | Path, source_context: Mapping,
                    **adapter_options) -> None:
    """A real worker entrypoint for an existing application's bounded queue.

    next_frame(timeout_seconds) returns the newest owned Frame, or None on
    timeout. It must return within the timeout so shutdown stays responsive.
    publish must enqueue/signal the update to the GUI, not touch widgets here.
    The GUI must also expire fresh_until_monotonic_s on its own timer even if
    no new update arrives while inference is blocked.
    The host owns camera shutdown and joins this worker before disposing it.
    """
    final_reason = "stopped"
    try:
        with ExistingAppGazeAdapter(profile_path, source_context=source_context,
                                    **adapter_options) as adapter:
            while not stop_event.is_set():
                frame = next_frame(0.05)
                if stop_event.is_set():
                    break
                publish(adapter.snapshot() if frame is None else adapter.process_frame(frame))
    except Exception as exc:
        final_reason = f"worker_error: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        raise
    finally:
        publish(_unavailable(final_reason))

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/acquisition.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Stable target acquisition shared by GUI and integration clients.

The collector measures quality and feature stability while a user is instructed
to fixate a known target. It cannot prove that the user actually looked at it.
No GUI, camera ownership, sleeps, or calibration-profile writes occur here.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
import json
import uuid
import numpy as np

from .types import Observation, CameraExtrinsics


class TargetCollector:
    """Collect a contiguous, settled batch of observations at one known target.

    ``target`` is normalized screen XY for ``kind='screen'`` or world XYZ in
    meters for ``kind='spatial'``. Call ``start()`` when the target is displayed,
    then ``add(observation)`` for each unique processed frame. With no explicit
    start timestamp, the first observation anchors the source-time clock.
    """

    def __init__(self, target_id: str, target, kind: str = "screen", *,
                 min_samples: int = 16, settle_seconds: float = 0.6,
                 min_duration_s: float = 0.6, quality_threshold: float = 0.4,
                 max_seconds: float = 15.0, max_feature_std: float | None = 0.12,
                 max_gap_s: float = 0.25, session_id: str | None = None):
        if kind not in ("screen", "spatial"):
            raise ValueError("target kind must be screen or spatial")
        coords = np.asarray(target, dtype=float)
        if coords.shape != ((2,) if kind == "screen" else (3,)) or not np.isfinite(coords).all():
            raise ValueError("target must contain finite normalized XY or world XYZ")
        if kind == "screen" and (np.any(coords < 0) or np.any(coords > 1)):
            raise ValueError("screen target coordinates must be normalized to [0,1]")
        if not str(target_id).strip():
            raise ValueError("target_id cannot be empty")
        if int(min_samples) != min_samples or min_samples < 4:
            raise ValueError("at least four distinct observations are required")
        if not all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in
                   (settle_seconds, min_duration_s, quality_threshold, max_seconds, max_gap_s)):
            raise ValueError("collector thresholds must be finite")
        if settle_seconds < 0.5 or min_duration_s < 0.1:
            raise ValueError("allow at least 0.5 seconds to settle and 0.1 seconds to measure")
        if not 0 <= quality_threshold <= 1 or max_gap_s <= 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid quality or temporal gap threshold")
        if max_seconds <= settle_seconds + min_duration_s:
            raise ValueError("timeout must exceed settling plus minimum acquisition duration")
        if max_feature_std is not None and (not math.isfinite(max_feature_std) or max_feature_std <= 0):
            raise ValueError("feature stability threshold must be positive or None")
        self.target_id = str(target_id)
        self.target = tuple(float(v) for v in coords)
        self.kind = kind
        self.min_samples = int(min_samples)
        self.settle_seconds = float(settle_seconds)
        self.min_duration_s = float(min_duration_s)
        self.quality_threshold = float(quality_threshold)
        self.max_seconds = float(max_seconds)
        self.max_feature_std = max_feature_std
        self.max_gap_s = float(max_gap_s)
        self.session_id = session_id or uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.observations: list[Observation] = []
        self.rejections: Counter[str] = Counter()
        self.state = "waiting"
        self.reason = "ready_to_start"
        self._started: float | None = None
        self._last_time: float | None = None
        self._settle_until: float | None = None
        self._stable_start: float | None = None
        self._context_key = None
        self._image_size = None
        self._method = None
        self._seen = 0

    @property
    def complete(self) -> bool:
        return self.state == "complete"

    def start(self, timestamp_s: float | None = None) -> None:
        if timestamp_s is not None and not math.isfinite(timestamp_s):
            raise ValueError("start timestamp must be finite")
        self.observations.clear()
        self.rejections.clear()
        self._started = timestamp_s
        self._last_time = None
        self._settle_until = None if timestamp_s is None else timestamp_s + self.settle_seconds
        self._stable_start = None
        self._context_key = self._image_size = self._method = None
        self._seen = 0
        self.state, self.reason = "settling", "allow_fixation_to_settle"

    def retry(self, timestamp_s: float | None = None) -> None:
        self.start(timestamp_s)

    def cancel(self) -> None:
        self.observations.clear()
        self.state, self.reason = "cancelled", "cancelled_by_user"

    def _reject(self, reason: str, stamp: float, *, reset: bool = True) -> bool:
        self.reason = reason
        self.rejections[reason] += 1
        if reset:
            self.observations.clear()
            self._stable_start = None
            self._settle_until = stamp + self.settle_seconds
            self.state = "settling"
        return False

    def add(self, observation: Observation) -> bool:
        """Return True only when this observation was accepted for the batch."""
        if self.state in ("waiting", "complete", "timeout", "cancelled", "incompatible"):
            return False
        stamp = float(observation.timestamp_s)
        if not math.isfinite(stamp):
            self.rejections["nonfinite_timestamp"] += 1
            self.reason = "nonfinite_timestamp"
            return False
        if self._started is None:
            self._started, self._settle_until = stamp, stamp + self.settle_seconds
        if stamp < self._started or (self._last_time is not None and stamp <= self._last_time):
            return self._reject("duplicate_or_reversed_timestamp", stamp, reset=False)
        if stamp - self._started > self.max_seconds:
            self.state, self.reason = "timeout", "insufficient_contiguous_quality_samples"
            self.observations.clear()
            return False
        previous_stamp = self._last_time
        self._last_time = stamp
        self._seen += 1
        if previous_stamp is not None and stamp - previous_stamp > self.max_gap_s:
            return self._reject("capture_gap", stamp)
        if observation.blink or not observation.eyes_open:
            return self._reject("blink_or_closed_eyes", stamp)
        if not math.isfinite(float(observation.quality)) or observation.quality < self.quality_threshold:
            return self._reject("low_tracking_quality", stamp)
        if observation.features is None:
            return self._reject("features_unavailable", stamp)
        features = np.asarray(observation.features, dtype=float)
        if features.ndim != 1 or len(features) < 4 or not np.isfinite(features).all():
            return self._reject("nonfinite_or_invalid_features", stamp)
        if self.kind == "spatial":
            origin = observation.eye_origin_camera_m
            if origin is None or np.asarray(origin).shape != (3,) or not np.isfinite(origin).all():
                return self._reject("spatial_eye_origin_unavailable", stamp)
            measured_origin = observation.eye_origin_source in (
                "aligned_depth_pupil_midpoint", "stereo_triangulated_pupil_midpoint", "stereo_pupil_midpoint")
            if observation.head_pose is None and not measured_origin:
                return self._reject("spatial_head_pose_unavailable", stamp)
        # A change in camera resolution, feature method or eye ROI invalidates
        # the batch, rather than blending incompatible coordinate systems.
        try:
            context = json.dumps(getattr(observation, "context", {}), sort_keys=True,
                                 separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False,
                                 default=lambda value: value.tolist() if isinstance(value, np.ndarray)
                                 else value.item() if isinstance(value, np.generic) else str(value))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return self._reject("invalid_tracking_context", stamp)
        if self._context_key is None:
            self._context_key = context
            self._image_size = tuple(observation.image_size)
            self._method = observation.pupil_method
        elif (context != self._context_key or tuple(observation.image_size) != self._image_size
              or observation.pupil_method != self._method):
            self.observations.clear()
            self.state, self.reason = "incompatible", "tracking_configuration_changed"
            self.rejections[self.reason] += 1
            return False
        if stamp + 1e-9 < self._settle_until:
            self.state, self.reason = "settling", "allow_fixation_to_settle"
            return False
        if self.max_feature_std is not None and self.observations:
            # Normalized pupil XY and normalized head rotations share roughly
            # comparable scales. Use recent observations to reject a moving
            # fixation, then restart dwell instead of averaging a transition.
            recent = [np.asarray(o.features, float)[:7] for o in self.observations[-24:]]
            candidate = np.vstack([*recent, features[:7]])
            if float(np.max(np.std(candidate, axis=0))) > self.max_feature_std:
                return self._reject("unstable_fixation_or_head_motion", stamp)
        if self._stable_start is None:
            self._stable_start = stamp
        self.observations.append(deepcopy(observation))
        self.state, self.reason = "collecting", "collecting_stable_samples"
        if len(self.observations) >= self.min_samples and stamp - self._stable_start + 1e-9 >= self.min_duration_s:
            self.state, self.reason = "complete", "stable_target_collected"
        return True

    def progress(self) -> dict:
        elapsed = max(0.0, (self._last_time or 0) - self._started) if self._started is not None and self._last_time is not None else 0.0
        stable = max(0.0, self._last_time - self._stable_start) if self._last_time is not None and self._stable_start is not None else 0.0
        fraction = min(1.0, len(self.observations) / self.min_samples,
                       stable / self.min_duration_s)
        return {"state": self.state, "reason": self.reason, "target_id": self.target_id,
                "accepted": len(self.observations), "required": self.min_samples,
                "elapsed_s": elapsed, "stable_duration_s": stable,
                "fraction": 1.0 if self.complete else fraction,
                "rejected": int(sum(self.rejections.values())),
                "rejection_reasons": dict(self.rejections), "frames_seen": self._seen}

    def records(self, *, screen_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None,
                extrinsics: CameraExtrinsics | None = None) -> list[dict]:
        """Convert a completed batch to core calibration records."""
        if not self.complete:
            raise ValueError("target collection is not complete")
        from .calibration import screen_record, spatial_record
        if self.kind == "screen":
            if not screen_size or len(screen_size) != 2 or min(screen_size) < 2:
                raise ValueError("screen dimensions >=2 are required")
            target_px = (self.target[0] * (screen_size[0] - 1),
                         self.target[1] * (screen_size[1] - 1))
            records = [screen_record(o, self.target_id, target_px, screen_size)
                       for o in self.observations]
        else:
            if extrinsics is None:
                raise ValueError("world extrinsics are required for spatial samples")
            records = [spatial_record(o, self.target_id, self.target, extrinsics)
                       for o in self.observations]
        for row in records:
            row["session_id"] = self.session_id
            row["acquisition"] = {"settle_seconds": self.settle_seconds,
                                  "stable_duration_s": self.progress()["stable_duration_s"],
                                  "target_assumption": "user_instructed_fixation"}
        return records

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/acquisition.py =====

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Offline gaze-stream analytics and self-contained evidence reports.

Precision describes dispersion of model estimates. It is not gaze accuracy:
accuracy requires fresh, independently known fixation targets. Missing frames,
blinks, pauses and long timestamp gaps never accrue dwell time.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from html import escape
from pathlib import Path
import json
import math
import numpy as np

from .sessions import SessionData, load_session, export_csv, _atomic_json, _validate_record


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AreaOfInterest:
    name: str
    rect: tuple[float, float, float, float]  # normalized x,y,width,height
    surface_name: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.name or len(self.rect) != 4 or not np.isfinite(self.rect).all():
            raise ValueError("AOI requires a name and finite normalized rectangle")
        x, y, width, height = self.rect
        if min(x, y) < 0 or min(width, height) <= 0 or x + width > 1 + 1e-9 or y + height > 1 + 1e-9:
            raise ValueError("AOI must fit within normalized [0,1] coordinates")

    def contains(self, xy):
        x, y, width, height = self.rect
        return x <= xy[0] <= x + width and y <= xy[1] <= y + height


def _stats(values):
    data = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    if not len(data):
        return {"count": 0, "mean": None, "median": None, "p95": None, "max": None}
    return {"count": int(len(data)), "mean": float(np.mean(data)),
            "median": float(np.median(data)), "p95": float(np.percentile(data, 95)),
            "max": float(np.max(data))}


def precision_metrics(points) -> dict:
    """Spatial RMS of a cluster, not the misleading SD of radial distances."""
    array = np.asarray(points, dtype=float)
    if array.size == 0:
        return {"samples": 0, "mean_xy": None, "std_xy": None, "radial_rms": None,
                "radial_p95": None, "sample_to_sample_rms": None}
    if array.ndim != 2 or array.shape[1] != 2 or not np.isfinite(array).all():
        raise ValueError("precision samples must be a finite Nx2 array")
    center = np.mean(array, axis=0)
    distances = np.linalg.norm(array - center, axis=1)
    differences = np.linalg.norm(np.diff(array, axis=0), axis=1)
    return {"samples": int(len(array)), "mean_xy": center.tolist(),
            "std_xy": np.std(array, axis=0).tolist(),
            "radial_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(distances ** 2))),
            "radial_p95": float(np.percentile(distances, 95)),
            "sample_to_sample_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(differences ** 2))) if len(differences) else None}


def compare_anchors(first_xy, repeat_xy, threshold_px: float = 30.0,
                    target_px=None) -> dict:
    """Compare instructed repeat fixations without silently modifying a model."""
    first, repeat = np.asarray(first_xy, float), np.asarray(repeat_xy, float)
    if first.ndim != 2 or repeat.ndim != 2 or first.shape[1:] != (2,) or repeat.shape[1:] != (2,):
        raise ValueError("anchor samples must be Nx2 pixel arrays")
    if min(len(first), len(repeat)) < 4 or not np.isfinite(first).all() or not np.isfinite(repeat).all():
        raise ValueError("each anchor requires >=4 finite samples")
    if not math.isfinite(threshold_px) or threshold_px <= 0:
        raise ValueError("drift threshold must be positive")
    a, b = np.mean(first, axis=0), np.mean(repeat, axis=0)
    drift = float(np.linalg.norm(b - a))
    report = {"drift_px": drift, "offset_xy_px": (b - a).tolist(),
              "threshold_px": float(threshold_px),
              "outcome": "recalibration_recommended" if drift > threshold_px else "within_selected_drift_threshold",
              "first": precision_metrics(first), "repeat": precision_metrics(repeat),
              "automatic_correction_applied": False,
              "ground_truth": "user_instructed_repeated_fixation",
              "accuracy_verified": False}
    if target_px is not None:
        target = np.asarray(target_px, float)
        if target.shape != (2,) or not np.isfinite(target).all():
            raise ValueError("target must be finite pixel XY")
        report["target_px"] = target.tolist()
        report["first_target_error_px"] = _stats(np.linalg.norm(first - target, axis=1))
        report["repeat_target_error_px"] = _stats(np.linalg.norm(repeat - target, axis=1))
    return report


def _is_stale(record):
    return bool(record.get("stale", False) or "stale" in str(record.get("reason", "")).lower())


def _screen_point(record, screen_size):
    if not record.get("valid") or not record.get("screen_valid", True) or _is_stale(record):
        return None, None
    xy = record.get("gaze_screen_px")
    norm = record.get("gaze_screen_normalized")
    if norm is not None:
        norm = np.asarray(norm, float)
        if norm.shape != (2,) or np.any(norm < 0) or np.any(norm > 1):
            return None, None
    if xy is not None:
        xy = np.asarray(xy, float)
        if xy.shape != (2,):
            return None, None
    if screen_size is not None:
        extent = np.asarray(screen_size, float) - 1
        if xy is not None and (np.any(xy < 0) or np.any(xy > extent + 1e-8)):
            return None, None
        if norm is None and xy is not None:
            norm = xy / extent
        if xy is None and norm is not None:
            xy = norm * extent
    return xy, norm


def _fixations(records, points, times, max_gap_s, radius_px, min_duration_s):
    clusters = []
    buffer = []

    def finish():
        nonlocal buffer
        if len(buffer) >= 3:
            ids = buffer
            duration = times[ids[-1]] - times[ids[0]]
            if duration >= min_duration_s:
                values = np.array([points[i] for i in ids])
                metrics = precision_metrics(values)
                clusters.append({"start_s": float(times[ids[0]]), "end_s": float(times[ids[-1]]),
                                 "duration_s": float(duration), "samples": len(ids),
                                 "centroid_px": metrics["mean_xy"], "jitter_rms_px": metrics["radial_rms"],
                                 "jitter_std_xy_px": metrics["std_xy"]})
        buffer = []

    for index, point in enumerate(points):
        if point is None:
            finish()
            continue
        if buffer:
            gap = times[index] - times[buffer[-1]]
            candidate = np.array([points[i] for i in buffer] + [point])
            center = np.mean(candidate, axis=0)
            radius = float(np.max(np.linalg.norm(candidate - center, axis=1)))
            if gap <= 0 or gap > max_gap_s or radius > radius_px:
                finish()
        buffer.append(index)
    finish()
    return clusters


def analyze_session(records, screen_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None,
                    aois=None, *, max_gap_s: float = 0.15,
                    fixation_radius_px: float = 35.0,
                    min_fixation_duration_s: float = 0.12,
                    heatmap_shape: tuple[int, int] = (32, 18),
                    surface_sizes: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] | None = None) -> dict:
    """Analyze metadata records with explicit timestamp-gap exclusion.

    AOIs use normalized rectangles. A surface AOI sets ``surface_name`` and
    requires its physical (width_m,height_m) in ``surface_sizes``. Overlapping
    AOIs are measured independently, so their dwell totals may overlap.
    """
    rows = [_validate_record(r) for r in records]
    if screen_size is not None and (len(screen_size) != 2 or min(screen_size) < 2 or any(int(v) != v for v in screen_size)):
        raise ValueError("screen dimensions must contain two integers >=2")
    if not all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in (max_gap_s, fixation_radius_px, min_fixation_duration_s)):
        raise ValueError("analysis thresholds must be positive and finite")
    cols, height = (int(v) for v in heatmap_shape)
    if not (2 <= cols <= 128 and 2 <= height <= 128):
        raise ValueError("heatmap dimensions must lie within [2,128]")
    areas = [a if isinstance(a, AreaOfInterest) else AreaOfInterest(str(a["name"]), tuple(a["rect"]), a.get("surface_name")) for a in (aois or [])]
    if len({a.name for a in areas}) != len(areas):
        raise ValueError("AOI names must be unique")
    surface_sizes = surface_sizes or {}
    for name, size in surface_sizes.items():
        if len(size) != 2 or not np.isfinite(size).all() or min(size) <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid metric surface dimensions for {name}")
    timestamps = [float(r.get("session_time_s", r["timestamp_s"])) for r in rows]
    if any(b < a for a, b in zip(timestamps, timestamps[1:])):
        raise ValueError("records must be ordered by session timestamp")
    times = np.array(timestamps, float)
    if len(times):
        times = times - times[0]
    deltas = np.diff(times, prepend=times[0] if len(times) else 0)
    deltas = deltas if len(times) else np.array([], float)
    good_intervals = (deltas > 0) & (deltas <= max_gap_s)
    points, normalized = [], []
    for record in rows:
        pixel, norm = _screen_point(record, screen_size)
        points.append(pixel)
        normalized.append(norm)
    valid_flags = np.array([bool(r.get("valid")) and not _is_stale(r) for r in rows], bool)
    screen_flags = np.array([p is not None or n is not None for p, n in zip(points, normalized)], bool)
    weights = np.zeros(len(rows), float)
    for i in range(1, len(rows)):
        if good_intervals[i] and screen_flags[i] and screen_flags[i - 1]:
            weights[i] = deltas[i]
    heatmap = np.zeros((height, cols), float)
    sample_heatmap = np.zeros((height, cols), int)
    for i, xy in enumerate(normalized):
        if xy is not None:
            column = min(cols - 1, max(0, int(xy[0] * cols)))
            row = min(height - 1, max(0, int(xy[1] * height)))
            heatmap[row, column] += weights[i]
            sample_heatmap[row, column] += 1
    aoi_reports = []
    for area in areas:
        dwell, entries, longest, active_duration = 0.0, 0, 0.0, 0.0
        previous_inside = False
        for i, record in enumerate(rows):
            if area.surface_name is None:
                xy = normalized[i]
            else:
                metric = record.get("surface_xy_m")
                size = surface_sizes.get(area.surface_name)
                xy = (np.asarray(metric) / np.asarray(size) if size is not None and metric is not None
                      and record.get("valid") and record.get("surface_name") == area.surface_name and not _is_stale(record)
                      else None)
            inside = xy is not None and area.contains(xy)
            continuous = i > 0 and good_intervals[i]
            if inside and (not previous_inside or not continuous):
                entries += 1
                active_duration = 0.0
            if inside and previous_inside and continuous:
                dwell += float(deltas[i])
                active_duration += float(deltas[i])
                longest = max(longest, active_duration)
            elif not inside:
                active_duration = 0.0
            previous_inside = inside
        aoi_reports.append({"name": area.name, "rect": list(area.rect), "surface_name": area.surface_name,
                            "dwell_s": dwell, "entries": entries, "longest_contiguous_dwell_s": longest})
    fixations = _fixations(rows, points, times, max_gap_s, fixation_radius_px, min_fixation_duration_s)
    all_points = [p for p in points if p is not None]
    overall = precision_metrics(all_points)
    # Across all targets, dispersion describes coverage, not fixation jitter.
    fixation_precision = _stats([f["jitter_rms_px"] for f in fixations])
    latency = [float(r["processing_ms"]) for r in rows
               if isinstance(r.get("processing_ms"), (int, float)) and r["processing_ms"] >= 0]
    quality = [float(r["quality"]) for r in rows
               if isinstance(r.get("quality"), (int, float)) and 0 <= r["quality"] <= 1]
    reasons = Counter(str(r.get("reason", "unknown")) for r in rows if not r.get("valid") or _is_stale(r))
    surfaces = {}
    for name in sorted({r.get("surface_name") for r in rows if r.get("surface_name")}):
        metric_points = []
        dwell = 0.0
        for i, record in enumerate(rows):
            good = record.get("valid") and record.get("surface_name") == name and record.get("surface_xy_m") is not None and not _is_stale(record)
            if good:
                metric_points.append(record["surface_xy_m"])
                if i > 0 and good_intervals[i] and rows[i - 1].get("valid") and rows[i - 1].get("surface_name") == name and not _is_stale(rows[i - 1]):
                    dwell += float(deltas[i])
        surfaces[name] = {"samples": len(metric_points), "dwell_s": dwell,
                          "overall_dispersion_m": precision_metrics(metric_points)}
    explicit_dropped = [r.get("dropped_frames") for r in rows
                        if isinstance(r.get("dropped_frames"), int) and r["dropped_frames"] >= 0]
    warnings = ["Gaze accuracy is not measured by this stream report; validate against independent fixation targets.",
                "Overall dispersion includes intentional eye movement; use fixation jitter for stability.",
                "Intervals longer than max_gap_s and intervals touching invalid data accrue no dwell time."]
    if screen_size is None:
        warnings.append("Screen dimensions unavailable: heatmap uses supplied normalized coordinates only.")
    for area in areas:
        if area.surface_name and area.surface_name not in surface_sizes:
            warnings.append(f"Surface AOI {area.name}: dimensions unavailable; dwell is not measured.")
    report = {
        "schema_version": 2, "screen_size": list(screen_size) if screen_size else None,
        "summary": {"frames": len(rows), "valid_frames": int(np.count_nonzero(valid_flags)),
                    "valid_fraction": float(np.mean(valid_flags)) if len(rows) else 0.0,
                    "screen_frames": int(np.count_nonzero(screen_flags)),
                    "duration_s": float(times[-1]) if len(times) else 0.0,
                    "valid_screen_dwell_s": float(np.sum(weights)),
                    "timestamp_gap_count": int(np.count_nonzero(deltas > max_gap_s)),
                    "excluded_gap_duration_s": float(np.sum(deltas[deltas > max_gap_s])),
                    "duplicate_session_timestamps": int(np.count_nonzero(deltas[1:] == 0)),
                    "stale_frames": sum(_is_stale(r) for r in rows),
                    "reported_dropped_frames": max(explicit_dropped) if explicit_dropped else None,
                    "processing_latency_ms": _stats(latency), "quality": _stats(quality),
                    "invalid_reasons": dict(reasons)},
        "precision": {"overall_screen_dispersion_px": overall,
                      "fixation_jitter_rms_px": fixation_precision,
                      "method": "radial RMS about centroid; consecutive within-cluster displacement"},
        "fixations": fixations, "aois": aoi_reports, "surfaces": surfaces,
        "heatmap": {"width_bins": cols, "height_bins": height, "dwell_seconds": heatmap.tolist(),
                    "sample_counts": sample_heatmap.tolist(), "coordinates": "normalized screen; origin top-left"},
        "thresholds": {"max_gap_s": max_gap_s, "fixation_radius_px": fixation_radius_px,
                       "minimum_fixation_duration_s": min_fixation_duration_s},
        "physical_accuracy_verified": False, "warnings": warnings,
    }
    return report


def heatmap_svg(report: dict, width: int = 960, height: int = 540) -> str:
    heatmap = report["heatmap"]
    values = np.asarray(heatmap["dwell_seconds"], float)
    unit = "dwell time"
    if not values.size or float(values.max(initial=0)) == 0:
        values = np.asarray(heatmap["sample_counts"], float)
        unit = "sample count"
    peak = float(np.max(values)) if values.size else 0.0
    rows, cols = values.shape
    cell_w, cell_h = width / cols, height / rows
    chunks = [f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}" role="img" aria-label="Gaze heatmap by {unit}">',
              f'<rect width="{width}" height="{height}" rx="14" fill="#101b2a"/>']
    for y in range(rows):
        for x in range(cols):
            if values[y, x] > 0 and peak > 0:
                alpha = 0.18 + 0.82 * math.sqrt(values[y, x] / peak)
                chunks.append(f'<rect x="{x*cell_w:.2f}" y="{y*cell_h:.2f}" width="{cell_w:.2f}" height="{cell_h:.2f}" fill="#52e0ba" opacity="{alpha:.3f}"><title>{values[y,x]:.3f} {unit}</title></rect>')
    for area in report.get("aois", []):
        if area.get("surface_name") is None:
            x, y, rw, rh = area["rect"]
            chunks.append(f'<rect x="{x*width:.2f}" y="{y*height:.2f}" width="{rw*width:.2f}" height="{rh*height:.2f}" fill="none" stroke="#f3b86d" stroke-width="2"/>')
            chunks.append(f'<text x="{x*width+6:.2f}" y="{y*height+17:.2f}" fill="#ffe2b4" font-size="14">{escape(area["name"])}</text>')
    if peak <= 0:
        chunks.append(f'<text x="{width/2}" y="{height/2}" fill="#9dafc4" text-anchor="middle" font-size="24">No calibrated screen coordinates recorded</text>')
    chunks.append('</svg>')
    return "".join(chunks)


def _format(value, decimals=2):
    return "Not measured" if value is None else f"{value:.{decimals}f}"


def export_report(session_or_path: SessionData | str | Path, output_dir: str | Path, *,
                  screen_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None, aois=None,
                  surface_sizes=None, **kwargs) -> dict[str, Path]:
    session = session_or_path if isinstance(session_or_path, SessionData) else load_session(session_or_path)
    if screen_size is None:
        metadata = session.manifest.get("metadata", {})
        candidate = metadata.get("screen_size")
        if candidate:
            screen_size = tuple(candidate)
    report = analyze_session(session.records, screen_size, aois,
                             surface_sizes=surface_sizes, **kwargs)
    report["session"] = {"id": session.manifest.get("session_id"),
                         "created_utc": session.manifest.get("created_utc"),
                         "provenance": session.manifest.get("provenance", "unclassified"),
                         "recorded_video": bool(session.manifest.get("video_file")),
                         "metadata": session.manifest.get("metadata", {})}
    output = Path(output_dir)
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    json_path, html_path, csv_path = output / "report.json", output / "report.html", output / "gaze.csv"
    _atomic_json(json_path, report)
    export_csv(session, csv_path)
    summary = report["summary"]
    cards = [("Recorded frames", str(summary["frames"])),
             ("Valid estimates", f'{summary["valid_fraction"]*100:.1f}%'),
             ("Session duration", _format(summary["duration_s"]) + " s"),
             ("Processing p95", _format(summary["processing_latency_ms"]["p95"]) + " ms"),
             ("Fixation jitter median", _format(report["precision"]["fixation_jitter_rms_px"]["median"]) + " px"),
             ("Excluded gap time", _format(summary["excluded_gap_duration_s"]) + " s")]
    cards_html = "".join(f'<article><span>{escape(name)}</span><strong>{escape(value)}</strong></article>' for name, value in cards)
    aoi_rows = "".join(f'<tr><td>{escape(a["name"])}</td><td>{escape(a.get("surface_name") or "Screen")}</td><td>{a["dwell_s"]:.3f}</td><td>{a["entries"]}</td><td>{a["longest_contiguous_dwell_s"]:.3f}</td></tr>' for a in report["aois"])
    if not aoi_rows:
        aoi_rows = '<tr><td colspan="5">No areas of interest configured.</td></tr>'
    fix_rows = "".join(f'<tr><td>{f["start_s"]:.3f}</td><td>{f["duration_s"]:.3f}</td><td>{f["centroid_px"][0]:.1f}, {f["centroid_px"][1]:.1f}</td><td>{f["jitter_rms_px"]:.3f}</td><td>{f["samples"]}</td></tr>' for f in report["fixations"][:200])
    if not fix_rows:
        fix_rows = '<tr><td colspan="5">No stable clusters met the selected duration and radius.</td></tr>'
    warnings_html = "".join(f'<li>{escape(w)}</li>' for w in report["warnings"])
    doc = f'''<!doctype html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>GazeSpace — Session report</title>
<style>body{{margin:0;background:#08111d;color:#e7eef8;font:15px/1.6 system-ui,sans-serif}}main{{max-width:1180px;margin:auto;padding:44px 28px}}header{{border-bottom:1px solid #26364b;padding-bottom:24px;margin-bottom:28px}}h1{{font-size:36px;letter-spacing:-1px;margin:8px 0}}.eyebrow{{color:#57ddba;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:3px;font-size:12px}}p,.muted{{color:#a5b6ca}}.cards{{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:14px}}article{{background:#111e2e;border:1px solid #25364c;border-radius:14px;padding:20px}}article span{{display:block;color:#9dafc4}}article strong{{display:block;font-size:28px;font-weight:600;margin-top:5px}}section{{margin-top:30px}}h2{{font-size:21px;font-weight:600}}.heatmap{{border:1px solid #293c53;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden}}svg{{display:block;width:100%;height:auto}}table{{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;background:#111e2e;border-radius:12px}}td,th{{padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid #25364b;text-align:left}}th{{color:#91a7c0;font-weight:500}}.note{{padding:18px 22px;border:1px solid #69543b;border-radius:12px;background:#211e1a;color:#dcc9ac}}code{{word-break:break-all}}@media(max-width:700px){{.cards{{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}}table{{font-size:12px}}td,th{{padding:8px}}h1{{font-size:28px}}}}@media print{{body{{background:white;color:#152033}}main{{padding:0}}p,.muted{{color:#42536b}}article,table{{background:white}}article strong{{color:#152033}}.note{{background:#faf7ef}}}}</style></head>
<body><main><header><div class="eyebrow">GazeSpace / local session evidence</div><h1>Tracking and gaze report</h1>
<p>{escape(str(report["session"]["created_utc"] or ""))} · Source: <strong>{escape(str(report["session"]["provenance"]))}</strong><br>Session <code>{escape(str(report["session"]["id"]))}</code></p></header>
<div class="cards">{cards_html}</div>
<section class="note"><strong>What this report measures</strong><p>These statistics describe recorded estimates and software processing time. They do not establish real-world gaze accuracy or end-to-end camera-to-display latency. A separately measured fixation target session is required for those claims.</p></section>
<section><h2>Screen heatmap</h2><p>Dwell-weighted screen coverage. Invalid observations and long gaps do not accrue time.</p><div class="heatmap">{heatmap_svg(report)}</div></section>
<section><h2>Areas of interest</h2><table><thead><tr><th>Area</th><th>Coordinate space</th><th>Dwell (s)</th><th>Entries</th><th>Longest dwell (s)</th></tr></thead><tbody>{aoi_rows}</tbody></table></section>
<section><h2>Stable gaze clusters</h2><p>{len(report["fixations"])} clusters; the table shows up to 200. Radius ≤ {report["thresholds"]["fixation_radius_px"]:.1f} px; duration ≥ {report["thresholds"]["minimum_fixation_duration_s"]:.2f} s. These are algorithmic clusters, not clinical eye-movement classifications.</p><table><thead><tr><th>Start (s)</th><th>Duration (s)</th><th>Centroid (px)</th><th>Radial RMS (px)</th><th>Samples</th></tr></thead><tbody>{fix_rows}</tbody></table></section>
<section><h2>Interpretation and limits</h2><ul>{warnings_html}</ul><p>Complete metrics: <a href="report.json">report.json</a> · Coordinates: <a href="gaze.csv">gaze.csv</a>. All files are local and require no internet connection.</p></section>
</main></body></html>'''
    html_path.write_text(doc, encoding="utf-8")
    return {"json": json_path, "html": html_path, "csv": csv_path}

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/calibration.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
