def json_print(obj):
    print(json.dumps(obj, indent=2, allow_nan=False))


def parse_roi(value: str):
    data=tuple(float(x) for x in value.split(","))
    if len(data)!=4 or not np.all(np.isfinite(data)):
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("ROI must be normalized x,y,w,h")
    x,y,w,h=data
    if x<0 or y<0 or w<=0 or h<=0 or x+w>1 or y+h>1:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("ROI must fit in normalized [0,1] frame")
    return data


def add_camera_args(p):
    p.add_argument("--source",default="0",help="USB index (e.g. 0), video file, or Basler serial")
    p.add_argument("--backend",choices=("auto","dshow","msmf","v4l2","ffmpeg","pylon","realsense","synthetic"),default="auto")
    p.add_argument("--width",type=int,default=1280)
    p.add_argument("--height",type=int,default=720)
    p.add_argument("--fps",type=int,default=30)
    p.add_argument("--exposure-native",type=float,help="DirectShow/MSMF-specific exposure value, not microseconds")
    p.add_argument("--exposure-us",type=float,help="Basler/pypylon ExposureTime in microseconds")
    p.add_argument("--gain",type=float)
    p.add_argument("--ir-bit-depth",type=int,choices=(8,10,12,14,16))


def add_tracking_args(p):
    add_camera_args(p)
    p.add_argument("--profile",help="JSON calibration profile")
    p.add_argument("--subject-id",default=None,help="Explicit current operator identity for profile checks")
    p.add_argument("--mode",choices=("rgb","ir","ir-roi","preview"),default="rgb")
    p.add_argument("--face-model",help="Optional MediaPipe Tasks face_landmarker.task model")
    p.add_argument("--roi-right",type=parse_roi)
    p.add_argument("--roi-left",type=parse_roi)
    p.add_argument("--roi-file",help="JSON with normalized right/left eye ROIs from select-rois")
    p.add_argument("--pupil-polarity",choices=("dark","bright"),default="dark")


def make_source(args):
    return create_source(args.source,backend=args.backend,width=args.width,height=args.height,
                         fps=args.fps,exposure_native=args.exposure_native,
                         exposure_us=args.exposure_us,gain_native=args.gain,
                         gain=args.gain,ir_bit_depth=args.ir_bit_depth,pupil_polarity=getattr(args,"pupil_polarity","dark"))


def make_rois(args):
    rois={}
    if args.roi_file:
        raw=json.loads(Path(args.roi_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        rois={k:parse_roi(",".join(str(v) for v in raw[k])) for k in ("right","left")}
    rois.update({side:value for side,value in (("right",args.roi_right),("left",args.roi_left)) if value})
    return rois


def verify_camera_profile_source(args):
    if not args.profile or not Path(args.profile).exists():
        return
    p=load_profile(args.profile)
    # Host apps using GazeEngine.from_profile are responsible for this hardware binding.
    if p.get("camera_id") and str(args.source) != str(p["camera_id"]):
        raise ValueError(f"profile bound to camera/source {p['camera_id']!r}, not {args.source!r}; recalibrate")


def source_context(args):
    context={"camera_id":str(args.source),"backend":args.backend,
             "exposure_native":args.exposure_native,"exposure_us":args.exposure_us,
             "gain":args.gain,"ir_bit_depth":args.ir_bit_depth or 8}
    if getattr(args,"subject_id",None):context["subject_id"]=args.subject_id
    return context


def make_tracker(args):
    if args.mode=="preview":
        from .preview import PreviewTracker
        return PreviewTracker()
    intr=None
    if getattr(args,"profile",None) and Path(args.profile).exists():
        p=load_profile(args.profile)
        if p.get("intrinsics"):
            intr=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(p["intrinsics"])
    return GazeTracker(intr,mode=args.mode,face_model=args.face_model,
                       roi_normalized=make_rois(args) or None,pupil_polarity=args.pupil_polarity)


def cmd_doctor(args):
    import os
    payload={"name":"GazeSpace","version":__version__,"os":platform.platform(),
             "python":sys.version.split()[0], "opencv":cv2.__version__,
             "libraries":{k:bool(importlib.util.find_spec(k)) for k in
                          ("numpy","cv2","mediapipe","scipy","PIL","pypylon","pyrealsense2","PySide6","fastapi","uvicorn")},
             "python_supported":(3,11)<=sys.version_info[:2]<(3,13),
             "physical_camera_tested":False,
             "security":"camera data stays local; recording is explicit opt-in"}
    json_print(payload)


def cmd_cameras(args):
    json_print({"uvc":list_cameras(args.max_index,args.backend),"basler":list_basler_cameras()})


def cmd_track(args):
    verify_camera_profile_source(args)
    engine=(GazeEngine.from_profile(args.profile,mode=args.mode,face_model=args.face_model,
             roi_normalized=make_rois(args) or None,pupil_polarity=args.pupil_polarity,source_context=source_context(args))
            if args.profile and Path(args.profile).exists() and args.mode!="preview"
            else GazeEngine(make_tracker(args)))
    stream=None
    count=0
    started=host_now_s()
    try:
        if args.record:
            dest=Path(args.record)
            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            stream=dest.open("a",encoding="utf-8")
        with make_source(args) as camera:
            if hasattr(camera,"capabilities"):
                json_print({"camera_report":camera.capabilities()})
            print("Press Q or ESC to quit; raw camera frames are not saved.")
            while True:
                frame=camera.read()
                if frame is None:
                    print("End of video or camera frame unavailable")
                    break
                result=engine.process_frame(frame)
                count+=1
                if stream:
                    stream.write(json.dumps(result.as_dict(),allow_nan=False)+"\n")
                    stream.flush()
                if args.no_preview:
                    if count%max(1,args.print_every)==0:
                        json_print(result.as_dict())
                    if args.max_frames and count>=args.max_frames:
                        break
                else:
                    fps=count/max(1e-4,host_now_s()-started)
                    rendered=draw_diagnostics(frame.image,result,engine.last_observation,fps)
                    cv2.imshow("GazeSpace | calibration-aware tracking",rendered)
                    if cv2.waitKey(1)&0xff in (27,ord("q")):
                        break
                    if args.max_frames and count>=args.max_frames:
                        break
    finally:
        if stream:
            stream.close()
        if not args.no_preview:
            cv2.destroyAllWindows()
        json_print({"summary":engine.metrics()})
        engine.close()


def cmd_api(args):
    verify_camera_profile_source(args)
    if args.host not in ("127.0.0.1","localhost","::1"):
        raise ValueError("API only binds to loopback; use an authenticated gateway for remote deployment")
    import uvicorn
    from .service import GazeService, create_api
    def factory():
        if args.profile and args.mode!="preview":
            return GazeEngine.from_profile(args.profile,mode=args.mode,face_model=args.face_model,
                    roi_normalized=make_rois(args) or None,pupil_polarity=args.pupil_polarity,
                    source_context=source_context(args))
        return GazeEngine(make_tracker(args))
    service=GazeService(None,lambda:make_source(args),args.record,engine_factory=factory)
    app=create_api(service,token=args.token,manage_lifecycle=True)
    uvicorn.run(app,host=args.host,port=args.port,log_level="info")


def cmd_screen(args):
    if args.mode=="preview":raise ValueError("Select eye tracking before gaze calibration")
    if args.backend in ("pylon","realsense") and str(args.source) in ("0","first"):
        raise ValueError("Use an explicit device serial for calibrated industrial-camera profiles")
    if args.command!="calibrate-screen":
        verify_camera_profile_source(args)
    with make_source(args) as source:
        tracker=make_tracker(args)
        session=ScreenSession(source,tracker,args.profile,args.samples,args.grid,
                              validate=args.command in ("validate-screen","drift-check"),camera_id=str(args.source),
                              drift=args.command=="drift-check")
        try:
            report=session.run()
        finally:
            tracker.close()
    if report is None:
        print("Calibration cancelled; previous profile unchanged")
    else:
        json_print(report)


def cmd_spatial(args):
    if args.mode=="preview":raise ValueError("Select eye tracking before gaze calibration")
    if args.backend in ("pylon","realsense") and str(args.source) in ("0","first"):
        raise ValueError("Use an explicit device serial for calibrated industrial-camera profiles")
    verify_camera_profile_source(args)
    with make_source(args) as source:
        tracker=make_tracker(args)
        try:
            report=spatial_session(source,tracker,args.profile,args.targets,args.samples,
                                   validate=args.command=="validate-spatial",
                                   require_multidepth=not args.allow_single_depth)
        finally:
            tracker.close()
    json_print(report)


def cmd_select_rois(args):
    with make_source(args) as source:
        frame=source.read()
        if frame is None:
            raise OSError("cannot read a frame for eye ROI selection")
        im=frame.image
        h,w=im.shape[:2]
        rois={}
        try:
            for side in ("right","left"):
                title=f"Select {side} eye | ENTER/SPACE confirm | C cancel"
                x,y,rw,rh=cv2.selectROI(title,im,showCrosshair=True,fromCenter=False)
                cv2.destroyWindow(title)
                if rw<12 or rh<12:
                    raise ValueError(f"ROI for {side} cancelled/too small")
                rois[side]=[x/w,y/h,rw/w,rh/h]
        finally:
            cv2.destroyAllWindows()
    save_json(args.output,rois)
    json_print({"saved":args.output,"normalized_rois":rois,
                "warning":"ROIs are static; moving heads require reliable facial landmarks or a vendor eye tracker"})


def cmd_capture_board(args):
    dst=Path(args.output)
    dst.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    print("Frame preview: SPACE saves board image; Q/ESC exits; vary tilt/position/distance")
    n=0
    with make_source(args) as source:
        while True:
            frame=source.read()
            if frame is None:
                break
            preview=frame.image.copy()
            cv2.putText(preview,"SPACE capture board | Q quit",(20,30),
                        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,.7,(0,255,170),2)
            cv2.imshow("GazeSpace checkerboard capture",preview)
            key=cv2.waitKey(1)&0xff
            if key in (ord("q"),27):
                break
            if key==32:
                filepath=dst/f"board_{int(time.time()*1000)}_{n:03d}.png"
                if not cv2.imwrite(str(filepath),frame.image):
                    raise OSError("unable to save checkerboard frame")
                print(f"Saved {filepath}")
                n+=1
                if args.limit and n>=args.limit:
                    break
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()
    json_print({"saved":n,"path":str(dst)})


def load_images(path):
    root=Path(path)
    files=sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if p.suffix.lower() in (".png",".jpg",".jpeg",".bmp",".tif",".tiff"))
    if not files:
        raise ValueError(f"no images in {root}")
    pairs=[(str(p),cv2.imread(str(p),cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)) for p in files]
    for name,img in pairs:
        if img is None:
            raise ValueError(f"unreadable image {name}")
    return [im for _,im in pairs]


def cmd_calibrate_camera(args):
    images=load_images(args.images)
    intr, report=calibrate_intrinsics(images,args.cols,args.rows,args.square_mm,min_views=args.min_views)
    from .validation import camera_training_evidence
    evidence=camera_training_evidence(images,cols=args.cols,rows=args.rows,square_mm=args.square_mm)
    update_profile(args.profile,intrinsics=intr.to_dict(),extrinsics=None,
                   screen_model=None,spatial_model=None,surfaces=[],camera_calibration_evidence=evidence)
    json_print({"intrinsics":intr.to_dict(),"report":report,
                "invalidated":"old gaze models and world calibration because camera intrinsics changed"})


def cmd_calibrate_world(args):
    profile=load_profile(args.profile)
    if not profile.get("intrinsics"):
        raise ValueError("calibrate intrinsics first")
    intr=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(profile["intrinsics"])
    image=cv2.imread(args.image)
    if image is None:
        raise ValueError("cannot read board image")
    ext=solve_world_board_extrinsics(image,intr,args.cols,args.rows,args.square_mm)
    update_profile(args.profile,extrinsics=ext.to_dict(),screen_model=None,spatial_model=None,surfaces=[])
    json_print({"extrinsics":ext.to_dict(),
                "world_axes":"board first internal corner=origin, X across board, Y down board, Z right-handed",
                "invalidated":"old screen/spatial models and surfaces; camera geometry may have changed"})


def cmd_calibrate_stereo(args):
    left_p=load_profile(args.left_profile);right_p=load_profile(args.right_profile)
    left=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(left_p["intrinsics"])
    right=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(right_p["intrinsics"])
    ldir,rdir=Path(args.left_images),Path(args.right_images)
    allowed=(".png",".jpg",".jpeg",".bmp",".tif",".tiff")
    names=sorted({p.name for p in ldir.iterdir() if p.suffix.lower() in allowed} &
                 {p.name for p in rdir.iterdir() if p.suffix.lower() in allowed})
    if len(names)<args.min_pairs:
        raise ValueError("left/right image folders need >= min-pairs identical filenames")
    l=[cv2.imread(str(ldir/n)) for n in names]
    r=[cv2.imread(str(rdir/n)) for n in names]
    report=calibrate_stereo(l,r,left,right,args.cols,args.rows,args.square_mm,args.min_pairs)
    save_json(args.output,report)
    json_print(report)


def cmd_surfaces(args):
    file_data=json.loads(Path(args.surfaces).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if not isinstance(file_data,list):
        raise ValueError("surfaces file should be JSON array")
    from .geometry import ray_surface_intersection
    surfaces=[Surface.from_dict(x) for x in file_data]
    names=set()
    for surf in surfaces:
        if surf.name in names:
            raise ValueError("duplicate surface name")
        names.add(surf.name)
        # geometrically validate axes and dimensions without needing actual ray hit
        u,v=np.array(surf.u_vector),np.array(surf.v_vector)
        if not np.isfinite(u).all() or not np.isfinite(v).all() or np.linalg.norm(np.cross(u,v))<1e-7:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid surface axes: {surf.name}")
        if surf.width_m<=0 or surf.height_m<=0:
            raise ValueError("surface dimensions must be positive")
    update_profile(args.profile,surfaces=[s.to_dict() for s in surfaces])
    json_print({"surfaces_saved":len(surfaces),"names":list(names)})


def cmd_demo(args):
    """Offline math/regression demo: synthetic, never described as real eye test."""
    rng=np.random.default_rng(28)
    samples=[]
    target_xy=[(x,y) for y in np.linspace(.1,.9,4) for x in np.linspace(.1,.9,4)]
    for i,(x,y) in enumerate(target_xy):
        for _ in range(20):
            noise=rng.normal(0,.015,4)
            feat=np.array([x,y,x,y,0,0,0,.5,.5,.6])
            feat[:4]+=noise
            samples.append({"features":feat.tolist(),"target":[float(x),float(y)],"target_id":str(i)})
    model=fit_gaze_model(samples,screen_size=(1920,1080))
    report={"demo":"SYNTHETIC math-only calibration (NOT a real accuracy claim)",
            "profile_schema":2,"model_report":model.report,
            "example_prediction_normalized":model.predict(np.array([.5,.5,.5,.5,0,0,0,.5,.5,.6])).tolist()}
    if args.output:
        save_json(args.output,report)
    json_print(report)


def build_parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="gazespace",description="Windows eye/pupil tracking and calibrated workspace gaze")
    p.add_argument("--version",action="version",version=__version__)
    sp=p.add_subparsers(dest="command",required=True)
    s=sp.add_parser("doctor",help="Report dependency and platform readiness");s.set_defaults(func=cmd_doctor)
    s=sp.add_parser("cameras",help="Probe local UVC/USB and Basler cameras")
    s.add_argument("--max-index",type=int,default=6)
    s.add_argument("--backend",choices=("auto","dshow","msmf","v4l2"),default="auto")
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_cameras)
    s=sp.add_parser("track",help="Run live calibrated gaze display or headless capture")
    add_tracking_args(s)
    s.add_argument("--record",help="Optional gaze JSONL log (no raw images)")
    s.add_argument("--no-preview",action="store_true")
    s.add_argument("--max-frames",type=int)
    s.add_argument("--print-every",type=int,default=30)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_track)
    s=sp.add_parser("api",help="Local-only gaze telemetry API and WebSocket")
    add_tracking_args(s)
    s.add_argument("--host",default="127.0.0.1")
    s.add_argument("--port",type=int,default=8765)
    s.add_argument("--record",help="Optional gaze metadata JSONL log")
    s.add_argument("--token",help="Optional bearer token of >=16 characters; keep it secret")
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_api)
    for name in ("calibrate-screen","validate-screen","drift-check"):
        s=sp.add_parser(name,help="Fullscreen guided screen gaze session")
        add_tracking_args(s)
        s.get_default("profile")
        for action in s._actions:
            if action.dest == "profile": action.required = True
        s.add_argument("--grid",type=int,default=4)
        s.add_argument("--samples",type=int,default=16)
        s.set_defaults(func=cmd_screen)
    for name in ("calibrate-spatial","validate-spatial"):
        s=sp.add_parser(name,help="Physical 3D target ray-model session")
        add_tracking_args(s)
        for action in s._actions:
            if action.dest == "profile": action.required = True
        s.add_argument("--targets",required=True)
        s.add_argument("--samples",type=int,default=16)
        s.add_argument("--allow-single-depth",action="store_true",help="Only for planar experiments; not volumetric 3D")
        s.set_defaults(func=cmd_spatial)
    s=sp.add_parser("select-rois",help="Select two infrared eye ROIs with the mouse")
    add_camera_args(s)
    s.add_argument("--output",required=True)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_select_rois)
    s=sp.add_parser("capture-board",help="Capture images of chessboard at diverse positions")
    add_camera_args(s)
    s.add_argument("--output",required=True)
    s.add_argument("--limit",type=int,default=25)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_capture_board)
    s=sp.add_parser("calibrate-camera",help="Intrinsic camera calibration from chessboard images")
    s.add_argument("--images",required=True)
    s.add_argument("--profile",required=True)
    s.add_argument("--cols",type=int,default=9)
    s.add_argument("--rows",type=int,default=6)
    s.add_argument("--square-mm",type=float,default=24)
    s.add_argument("--min-views",type=int,default=8)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_calibrate_camera)
    s=sp.add_parser("calibrate-world",help="Attach world frame to a measured reference board")
    s.add_argument("--image",required=True)
    s.add_argument("--profile",required=True)
    s.add_argument("--cols",type=int,default=9)
    s.add_argument("--rows",type=int,default=6)
    s.add_argument("--square-mm",type=float,default=24)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_calibrate_world)
    s=sp.add_parser("calibrate-stereo",help="Paired left/right chessboard multi-camera calibration")
    for key in ("left-profile","right-profile","left-images","right-images","output"):
        s.add_argument("--"+key,required=True)
    s.add_argument("--cols",type=int,default=9)
    s.add_argument("--rows",type=int,default=6)
    s.add_argument("--square-mm",type=float,default=24)
    s.add_argument("--min-pairs",type=int,default=8)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_calibrate_stereo)
    s=sp.add_parser("set-surfaces",help="Install measured bounded workspace surfaces")
    s.add_argument("--profile",required=True)
    s.add_argument("--surfaces",required=True)
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_surfaces)
    s=sp.add_parser("demo",help="Run deterministic SYNTHETIC calibration math demo")
    s.add_argument("--output")
    s.set_defaults(func=cmd_demo)
    from .commands import add_commands
    add_commands(sp)
    return p


def main(argv=None):
    arguments = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
    if not arguments:arguments = ["desktop"]
    args=build_parser().parse_args(arguments)
    try:
        return args.func(args) or 0
    except (OSError,RuntimeError,ValueError,ImportError,KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        print(f"GazeSpace error: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

if __name__=="__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: ab13ba9bf06243b13b23ccf33f2ab8042481c6d6

"""One high-resolution monotonic host clock for acquisition and freshness.

On Windows Python 3.11/3.12 ``time.monotonic`` uses a lower-resolution clock
than ``perf_counter``. Using the latter consistently prevents rapid, distinct
camera frames from inheriting the same coarse host tick. Source/video PTS and
device hardware clocks remain separate; only host arrival times and deadlines
use this clock. Its epoch is unspecified and is never a wall-clock timestamp.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time

HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN = "gazespace.perf_counter"


def host_now_s() -> float:
    """Return high-resolution monotonic host seconds (Windows QPC)."""
    return time.perf_counter()


def host_clock_info() -> dict:
    """Describe the actual host clock used by this process for diagnostics."""
    info = time.get_clock_info("perf_counter")
    return {"domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, "implementation": info.implementation,
            "resolution_s": info.resolution, "monotonic": info.monotonic,
            "adjustable": info.adjustable}

"""Additional command workflows used by packaging and reproducible verification."""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import json
import time


def command_desktop(args):
    from .desktop import launch
    return launch(args.workspace,args.profile,args.demo)


def command_simulate(args):
    from .demo import build_demo_profile, DEMO_SOURCE_CONTEXT, SyntheticDemoEngine
    from .capture import SyntheticEyeSource
    from .engine import GazeEngine
    from .sessions import SessionRecorder
    from .analytics import export_report
    if args.seconds<=0 or args.seconds>3600:raise ValueError('Simulation duration must be in (0,3600] seconds')
    output=Path(args.output).resolve();output.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    profile_path=output/'synthetic_profile.json'
    profile=build_demo_profile(profile_path)
    source=SyntheticEyeSource(fps=30,duration_s=args.seconds,realtime=False)
    engine=SyntheticDemoEngine.from_profile(profile_path,mode='ir-roi',roi_normalized=source.roi_normalized,
                                   source_context=DEMO_SOURCE_CONTEXT)
    start=0.0;elapsed=[0.0]
    recorder=SessionRecorder(output/'sessions',metadata={'provenance':'synthetic','synthetic':True,
                 'screen_size':[1920,1080],'purpose':'rendered pupil image -> detection -> calibrated coordinates -> replay/report'},
                 record_video=args.record_video,clock=lambda:elapsed[0])
    last=None
    try:
        while True:
            frame=source.read()
            if frame is None:break
            elapsed[0]=(frame.sequence-1)/30
            last=engine.process_frame(frame);recorder.append(last,frame.image)
        path=recorder.close()
        paths=export_report(path,output/'report')
        report={'synthetic':True,'human_gaze_accuracy_measured':False,'session':str(path),
                'profile':str(profile_path),'measurements':profile['screen_validation'],
                'runtime':engine.metrics(),'exports':{k:str(v) for k,v in paths.items()}}
        (output/'simulation_summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
        print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))
    finally:
        recorder.close();engine.close();source.close()
    return 0


def command_report(args):
    from .analytics import export_report
    aois=json.loads(Path(args.aois).read_text()) if args.aois else None
    paths=export_report(args.session,args.output,aois=aois)
    print(json.dumps({k:str(v) for k,v in paths.items()},indent=2));return 0


def command_replay(args):
    from .sessions import load_session,SessionReplay
    session=load_session(args.session)
    if args.no_preview:
        from .analytics import analyze_session
        report=analyze_session(session.records,screen_size=session.manifest.get('metadata',{}).get('screen_size'))
        print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!='heatmap'},indent=2));return 0
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    from .ui.main_window import MainWindow
    app=QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    window=MainWindow(workspace=args.workspace)
    window.load_session_path(args.session);window.set_page(4);window.show()
    return app.exec()


def command_board(args):
    from .validation import write_checkerboard_svg
    path=write_checkerboard_svg(args.output,args.cols,args.rows,args.square_mm)
    print(json.dumps({'output':str(path),'print_scale':'100%; measure actual square size after printing'}));return 0


def command_validate_camera(args):
    from .calibration import load_profile,update_profile
    from .types import CameraIntrinsics
    from .validation import validate_camera_images
    from .cli import load_images
    profile=load_profile(args.profile)
    intr=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(profile['intrinsics'])
    train=profile.get('camera_calibration_evidence',{})
    report=validate_camera_images(load_images(args.images),intr,cols=args.cols,rows=args.rows,square_mm=args.square_mm,
                                  training_hashes=train.get('image_sha256'),min_views=3)
    update_profile(args.profile,camera_validation=report)
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2));return 0


def command_triangulate(args):
    import numpy as np
    from .calibration import load_profile,load_json,save_json
    from .types import CameraIntrinsics
    from .stereo import triangulate_points_checked
    left=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(load_profile(args.left_profile)['intrinsics'])
    right=CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(load_profile(args.right_profile)['intrinsics'])
    rig=load_json(args.rig);points=load_json(args.observations)
    result=triangulate_points_checked(np.asarray(points['left_px']),np.asarray(points['right_px']),left,right,
                                     np.asarray(rig['R_right_from_left']),np.asarray(rig['T_right_from_left_m']))
    payload=result.to_dict();save_json(args.output,payload);print(json.dumps(payload,indent=2));return 0


def add_commands(subparsers):
    s=subparsers.add_parser('desktop',help='Open the complete native desktop workspace')
    s.add_argument('--workspace');s.add_argument('--profile');s.add_argument('--demo',action='store_true');s.set_defaults(func=command_desktop)
    s=subparsers.add_parser('simulate',help='Run generated pupil images through calibration, tracking, recording and reporting')
    s.add_argument('--output',required=True);s.add_argument('--seconds',type=float,default=5);s.add_argument('--record-video',action='store_true');s.set_defaults(func=command_simulate)
    s=subparsers.add_parser('report',help='Export an offline session HTML/JSON/CSV report')
    s.add_argument('--session',required=True);s.add_argument('--output',required=True);s.add_argument('--aois');s.set_defaults(func=command_report)
    s=subparsers.add_parser('replay',help='Replay and analyze a recorded local session')
    s.add_argument('--session',required=True);s.add_argument('--no-preview',action='store_true');s.add_argument('--workspace');s.set_defaults(func=command_replay)
    s=subparsers.add_parser('generate-board',help='Create an exact-size printable checkerboard SVG')
    s.add_argument('--output',required=True);s.add_argument('--cols',type=int,default=9);s.add_argument('--rows',type=int,default=6);s.add_argument('--square-mm',type=float,default=24);s.set_defaults(func=command_board)
    s=subparsers.add_parser('validate-camera',help='Evaluate fixed intrinsics on fresh held-out board images')
    s.add_argument('--images',required=True);s.add_argument('--profile',required=True);s.add_argument('--cols',type=int,default=9);s.add_argument('--rows',type=int,default=6);s.add_argument('--square-mm',type=float,default=24);s.set_defaults(func=command_validate_camera)
    s=subparsers.add_parser('triangulate',help='Reconstruct matched image points using a measured stereo rig')
    for key in ('left-profile','right-profile','rig','observations','output'):s.add_argument('--'+key,required=True)
    s.set_defaults(func=command_triangulate)

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/demo.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: f8d4c0df4b3ab02e796f35effddcf907b88efdd0

"""Reproducible synthetic end-to-end demo using the real pupil detector.

The camera image is generated, so errors here test software relationships only.
They do not measure human gaze accuracy, IR hardware, or clinical performance.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from .capture import SyntheticEyeSource
from .vision import GazeTracker
from .calibration import fit_gaze_model, screen_record, spatial_record, new_profile, load_profile, save_json, GazeModel, _error_metrics
from .engine import GazeEngine
from .types import CameraExtrinsics, Surface

DEMO_SOURCE_CONTEXT = {'camera_id':'synthetic-v2','backend':'synthetic','subject_id':'synthetic-fixture'}
VIRTUAL_EYE_ORIGIN = np.array([0.0,0.0,0.65])
VIRTUAL_EXTRINSICS = CameraExtrinsics(np.eye(3),np.zeros(3))


def apply_virtual_origin(observation):
    if observation.features is None:return
    observation.eye_origin_camera_m=VIRTUAL_EYE_ORIGIN.copy()
    observation.eye_origin_source='synthetic_fixture'
    observation.context['eye_origin_source']='synthetic_fixture'
    observation.features=observation.features.copy();observation.features[-1]=VIRTUAL_EYE_ORIGIN[2]
    observation.warnings.append('synthetic_origin_and_surfaces_are_known_virtual_geometry')


class SyntheticDemoEngine(GazeEngine):
    """Explicit software fixture; never guesses geometry for a real camera."""
    @classmethod
    def from_profile(cls,path,*,source_context=None,**_):
        profile=load_profile(path)
        if not profile.get('synthetic') or profile.get('camera_id')!='synthetic-v2':
            raise ValueError('The synthetic engine accepts only its clearly labeled fixture profile')
        sm=GazeModel.from_dict(profile['screen_model'])
        sp=GazeModel.from_dict(profile['spatial_model']) if profile.get('spatial_model') else None
        actual={**DEMO_SOURCE_CONTEXT,**(source_context or {})}
        for model in (sm,sp):
            if model is not None:
                mismatch=model.context_mismatches(actual)
                if mismatch:raise ValueError('Synthetic profile context mismatch: '+', '.join(mismatch))
        tracker=GazeTracker(mode='ir-roi',roi_normalized=profile['context']['roi_normalized'])
        return cls(tracker,sm,sp,VIRTUAL_EXTRINSICS,[Surface.from_dict(v) for v in profile['surfaces']],source_context=actual)

    def process_frame(self,frame):
        if not (frame.metadata or {}).get('synthetic'):
            raise ValueError('A real camera frame cannot use the virtual-eye simulation engine')
        return super().process_frame(frame)

    def _apply_depth(self,frame,observation):
        apply_virtual_origin(observation)


def build_demo_profile(path: str | Path, screen_size=(1920,1080), width=640, height=360) -> dict:
    source = SyntheticEyeSource(width=width,height=height,realtime=False)
    tracker = GazeTracker(mode='ir-roi',roi_normalized=source.roi_normalized)
    records = []
    spatial_records = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(412)
    timestamp = 10.0
    context = {}
    try:
        for i,y in enumerate(np.linspace(.08,.92,5)):
            for j,x in enumerate(np.linspace(.08,.92,5)):
                for sample in range(12):
                    # A small controlled image perturbation prevents claiming
                    # perfection from numerically identical feature copies.
                    nx,ny=np.asarray([x,y])+rng.normal(0,.002,2)
                    ob = tracker.process(source.render_target(float(nx),float(ny)),timestamp)
                    timestamp += 1/30
                    apply_virtual_origin(ob)
                    if ob.features is None or ob.quality < .35:
                        raise RuntimeError(f'Synthetic pupil detection failed at target {i}-{j}: {ob.message}')
                    context = dict(getattr(ob,'context',{}))
                    records.append(screen_record(ob,f'{i}-{j}',(x*(screen_size[0]-1),y*(screen_size[1]-1)),screen_size))
                    depth=0.0 if sample%2==0 else -0.25
                    scale=(VIRTUAL_EYE_ORIGIN[2]-depth)/VIRTUAL_EYE_ORIGIN[2]
                    point=((x-.5)*.60*scale,(y-.5)*.34*scale,depth)
                    spatial_records.append(spatial_record(ob,f'{i}-{j}',point,VIRTUAL_EXTRINSICS))
        context.update(DEMO_SOURCE_CONTEXT)
        model=fit_gaze_model(records,screen_size=screen_size,context=context,session_id='synthetic-training-v2')
        model.metadata.update({'synthetic':True,'provenance':'generated pupil images; software demo only',
                               'camera_id':'synthetic-v2'})
        spatial_model=fit_gaze_model(spatial_records,'spatial',context=context,session_id='synthetic-spatial-training-v2')
        spatial_model.metadata.update({'synthetic':True,'virtual_target_depths_m':[0.0,-.25],
                                       'provenance':'known virtual eye origin; rendered pupil observations'})
        independent=[]
        target_values=[]
        for x,y in ((.2,.25),(.5,.2),(.8,.25),(.22,.5),(.5,.5),(.78,.5),(.2,.75),(.5,.8),(.8,.75)):
            ob=tracker.process(source.render_target(x,y),timestamp);timestamp+=1/30;apply_virtual_origin(ob)
            independent.append(ob.features);target_values.append([x,y])
        report=_error_metrics(model.predict(np.asarray(independent)),np.asarray(target_values),'screen',
                              screen_size)
        profile=new_profile(camera_id='synthetic-v2',context=context,subject_id='synthetic-fixture')
        profile['screen_model']=model.to_dict()
        profile['spatial_model']=spatial_model.to_dict()
        profile['extrinsics']=VIRTUAL_EXTRINSICS.to_dict()
        profile['surfaces']=[Surface('Virtual display',(-.30,-.17,0.0),(1.,0.,0.),(0.,1.,0.),.60,.34,screen_size).to_dict(),
                             Surface('Virtual workbench',(-.30,.30,-.10),(1.,0.,0.),(0.,0.,1.),.60,.38,None).to_dict()]
        profile['simulation_geometry']={'eye_origin_camera_m':VIRTUAL_EYE_ORIGIN.tolist(),
                                        'source':'explicit simulation fixture; no physical measurements'}
        profile['synthetic']=True
        profile['screen_validation']={'synthetic':True,'independent_targets':True,'screen_error':report,
                                      'note':'Software fixture; does not establish real-person gaze accuracy'}
        save_json(path,profile)
        return profile
    finally:
        tracker.close();source.close()

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/demo.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/depth.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 8f22bfa6db5f8618d363e12e3919fb08a7422ea1

"""Optional RealSense acquisition and measured depth deprojection.

The SDK is imported only for an actual selected RealSense device. Depth must be
aligned to the color image used by the eye detector; arbitrary depth/IR image
coordinates are never mixed. A missing/invalid depth returns no metric point.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from threading import Event

import cv2
import numpy as np

from .capture import FrameSource, to_bgr
from .clock import HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, host_now_s
from .types import CameraIntrinsics, Frame


def sample_depth_point(depth_m: np.ndarray, xy_px: tuple[float, float],
                       intrinsics: CameraIntrinsics, *, radius: int = 2,
                       min_depth_m: float = .10, max_depth_m: float = 3.0,
                       max_patch_spread_m: float = .03) -> np.ndarray | None:
    """Robust small-patch median followed by calibrated pinhole deprojection."""
    if (depth_m.ndim != 2 or depth_m.shape != (intrinsics.height, intrinsics.width) or
            intrinsics.source not in ("measured", "factory")):
        raise ValueError("Aligned depth must match measured intrinsics and image resolution")
    if radius < 0 or min_depth_m <= 0 or max_depth_m <= min_depth_m:
        raise ValueError("Invalid depth sampling limits")
    if not np.isfinite(xy_px).all():
        return None
    x, y = map(float, xy_px)
    px, py = int(round(x)), int(round(y))
    if not (0 <= px < intrinsics.width and 0 <= py < intrinsics.height):
        return None
    patch = np.asarray(depth_m[max(0, py-radius):min(intrinsics.height, py+radius+1),
                               max(0, px-radius):min(intrinsics.width, px+radius+1)], float)
    values = patch[np.isfinite(patch) & (patch >= min_depth_m) & (patch <= max_depth_m)]
    if len(values) < max(1, int(patch.size * .4)):
        return None
    if float(np.percentile(values, 90) - np.percentile(values, 10)) > max_patch_spread_m:
        return None
    z = float(np.median(values))
    uv = cv2.undistortPoints(np.array([[[x, y]]], dtype=float), intrinsics.camera_matrix,
                              intrinsics.distortion).reshape(2)
    return np.array([uv[0] * z, uv[1] * z, z], dtype=float)


def eye_origin_from_depth(depth_m: np.ndarray, pupils_px: dict,
                          intrinsics: CameraIntrinsics) -> np.ndarray | None:
    """Approximate eye-surface midpoint from two valid aligned depth patches.

    This is a measured surface point, not the optical eyeball rotation center.
    Both samples must exist and agree in depth; a single patch is insufficient.
    """
    if not all(side in pupils_px for side in ("right", "left")):
        return None
    points = [sample_depth_point(depth_m, pupils_px[side], intrinsics)
              for side in ("right", "left")]
    if any(point is None for point in points):
        return None
    right, left = points
    if abs(right[2] - left[2]) > .08:
        return None
    separation = float(np.linalg.norm(right - left))
    if not .025 <= separation <= .10:
        return None
    return (right + left) / 2


class RealSenseSource(FrameSource):
    is_live = True

    def __init__(self, serial: str | None = None, width: int = 640, height: int = 480,
                 fps: int = 30, timeout_ms: int = 500):
        if width <= 0 or height <= 0 or fps <= 0 or not 1 <= timeout_ms <= 10000:
            raise ValueError("Invalid RealSense stream settings")
        try:
            import pyrealsense2 as rs
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError("RealSense mode requires pyrealsense2 and compatible camera firmware/drivers") from exc
        self.rs, self.timeout_ms = rs, int(timeout_ms)
        self._closed, self._started = False, False
        self._stop, self.seq = Event(), 0
        self.pipeline = rs.pipeline()
        config = rs.config()
        if serial:
            config.enable_device(serial)
        config.enable_stream(rs.stream.color, width, height, rs.format.bgr8, fps)
        config.enable_stream(rs.stream.depth, width, height, rs.format.z16, fps)
        try:
            profile = self.pipeline.start(config)
            self._started = True
            self.align = rs.align(rs.stream.color)
            device = profile.get_device()
            self.depth_scale = float(device.first_depth_sensor().get_depth_scale())
            actual_serial = device.get_info(rs.camera_info.serial_number)
            color = profile.get_stream(rs.stream.color).as_video_stream_profile().get_intrinsics()
            # RealSense inverse Brown-Conrady coefficients are not equivalent to
            # OpenCV's forward distortion. Only known forward/no-distortion models
            # are safely accepted here; other models require SDK deprojection.
            valid_models = (rs.distortion.none, rs.distortion.brown_conrady)
            self._cv_depth_supported = color.model in valid_models
            distortion = np.zeros(5) if color.model == rs.distortion.none else np.asarray(color.coeffs, float)
            self.intrinsics = CameraIntrinsics(color.width, color.height,
                np.array([[color.fx, 0, color.ppx], [0, color.fy, color.ppy], [0, 0, 1]], dtype=float),
                distortion, source="factory")
            self.info = {"camera_id": f"realsense:{actual_serial}", "serial": actual_serial,
                         "model": device.get_info(rs.camera_info.name), "backend": "realsense",
                         "identity_strength": "serial", "width": color.width, "height": color.height,
                         "fps_reported": fps, "is_live": True, "depth_aligned_to": "color",
                         "host_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN,
                         "intrinsics": self.intrinsics.to_dict(), "depth_scale_m": self.depth_scale,
                         "depth_deprojection_supported": self._cv_depth_supported,
                         "global_shutter_verified": False}
        except Exception:
            self.close()
            raise

    def read(self) -> Frame | None:
        if self._closed or self._stop.is_set():
            return None
        frames = self.pipeline.wait_for_frames(self.timeout_ms)
        aligned = self.align.process(frames)
        color, depth = aligned.get_color_frame(), aligned.get_depth_frame()
        if not color or not depth:
            raise OSError("RealSense frame missing aligned color/depth")
        received = host_now_s()
        image = to_bgr(np.asanyarray(color.get_data()))
        metric_depth = np.asanyarray(depth.get_data()).astype(np.float32) * self.depth_scale
        self.seq += 1
        return Frame(image, received, self.seq, self.info["serial"],
                     metadata={"simulated": False, "depth_aligned_to": "color",
                               "host_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN,
                               "depth_intrinsics": self.intrinsics.to_dict(),
                               "depth_deprojection_supported": self._cv_depth_supported,
                               "hardware_clock_synchronized": False},
                     received_monotonic_s=received,
                     hardware_timestamp_s=float(color.get_timestamp()) / 1000.0,
                     depth_m=metric_depth.copy() if self._cv_depth_supported else None)

    def capabilities(self) -> dict:
        return dict(self.info)

    def request_stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        if not self._closed:
            self._closed = True
            if self._started:
                self.pipeline.stop()
                self._started = False

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Native Windows desktop launcher. Qt is an optional install extra."""
from __future__ import annotations
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys


def launch(workspace=None, profile=None, demo=False):
    try:
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
        from PySide6.QtCore import QTimer
        from .ui.main_window import MainWindow
        from .ui.fonts import configure_app_fonts
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("Desktop needs python -m pip install 'gazespace[desktop,face,api]'") from exc
    app=QApplication.instance() or QApplication(sys.argv[:1])
    configure_app_fonts(app)
    app.setApplicationName('GazeSpace Studio')
    app.setOrganizationName('GazeSpace')
    window=MainWindow(workspace=workspace,profile=profile)
    window.show()
    if demo:QTimer.singleShot(100,window.open_demo)
    return app.exec()


def main():
    import argparse
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description='GazeSpace native desktop')
    parser.add_argument('--workspace',type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--profile',type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--demo',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    return launch(args.workspace,args.profile,args.demo)


if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/engine.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 01d35c8d30c5067bbdde0b9a872d724098b3cf4e

"""End-to-end gaze engine; dependency-injectable into an existing Windows app."""
from __future__ import annotations
from collections import deque
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import cv2
from .types import Observation, GazeResult, CameraIntrinsics, CameraExtrinsics, Surface, Frame
from .clock import host_now_s
from .filtering import OneEuro
from .geometry import camera_to_world_point, camera_to_world_direction, first_surface_hit, unit_vector
from .calibration import GazeModel, load_profile
from .vision import GazeTracker, FEATURE_VERSION, FEATURE_NAMES
from .lighting import frame_quality

class GazeEngine:
    def __init__(self, tracker: GazeTracker,
                 screen_model: GazeModel | None = None,
                 spatial_model: GazeModel | None = None,
                 extrinsics: CameraExtrinsics | None = None,
                 surfaces: list[Surface] | None = None,
                 min_quality: float = 0.35, filter_min_cutoff: float = 1.2,
                 filter_beta: float = 0.015, source_context: dict | None = None):
        if not np.isfinite(min_quality) or not 0 <= min_quality <= 1:
            raise ValueError("minimum quality must be in [0,1]")
        self.tracker = tracker
        self.screen_model, self.spatial_model = screen_model, spatial_model
        self.extrinsics, self.surfaces = extrinsics, surfaces or []
        self.min_quality = min_quality
        self.source_context = dict(source_context or {})
        if extrinsics is not None:
            extrinsics.validate()
        for surface in self.surfaces:
            surface.validate()
        self.last_capture_timestamp = None
        self.screen_filter = OneEuro(min_cutoff=filter_min_cutoff, beta=filter_beta)
        self.direction_filter = OneEuro(min_cutoff=filter_min_cutoff, beta=filter_beta)
        self.recent_times = deque(maxlen=250)
        self.total, self.valid, self.rejected, self.blinks = 0, 0, 0, 0
        self.last_result: GazeResult | None = None
        self.last_observation: Observation | None = None
        self.last_lighting: dict | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_profile(cls, path: str | Path, *, mode: str = "rgb",
                     roi_normalized=None, face_model: str | None = None,
                     pupil_polarity: str = "dark", source_context: dict | None = None, **kwargs):
        profile = load_profile(path)
        intr = CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(profile["intrinsics"]) if profile.get("intrinsics") else None
        ext = CameraExtrinsics.from_dict(profile["extrinsics"]) if profile.get("extrinsics") else None
        sm = GazeModel.from_dict(profile["screen_model"]) if profile.get("screen_model") else None
        sp = GazeModel.from_dict(profile["spatial_model"]) if profile.get("spatial_model") else None
        for model in (sm, sp):
            if model and model.metadata.get("tracking_mode") and model.metadata["tracking_mode"] != mode:
                raise ValueError("model was calibrated for a different tracking mode; recalibrate")
            if model and model.metadata.get("pupil_polarity") and model.metadata["pupil_polarity"] != pupil_polarity:
                raise ValueError("model was calibrated for different IR pupil polarity; recalibrate")
            if model and source_context:
                mismatch = model.context_mismatches(source_context)
                if mismatch:
                    raise ValueError("profile source context mismatch: " + ", ".join(mismatch))
        if sp and (not intr or intr.source not in ("measured", "factory") or ext is None):
            raise ValueError("spatial model requires measured camera intrinsics + world extrinsics")
        tracker = GazeTracker(intr, mode, face_model, roi_normalized, pupil_polarity)
        for model in (sm, sp):
            if model:
                mismatch = model.context_mismatches(tracker.context())
                if mismatch:
                    tracker.close()
                    raise ValueError("profile tracking context mismatch: " + ", ".join(mismatch))
        return cls(tracker, sm, sp, ext,
                   [Surface.from_dict(s) for s in profile.get("surfaces",[])],
                   source_context=source_context, **kwargs)

    def process(self, image_bgr: np.ndarray, timestamp_s: float | None = None) -> GazeResult:
        return self.process_frame(Frame(image_bgr, host_now_s() if timestamp_s is None else timestamp_s))

    def process_frame(self, frame: Frame) -> GazeResult:
        started = host_now_s()
        image_bgr, timestamp_s = frame.image, frame.timestamp_s
        ob = self.tracker.process(image_bgr, timestamp_s)
        self._apply_depth(frame, ob)
        result = self.process_observation(ob)
        if self.total == 1 or self.total % 20 == 0:
            self.last_lighting = frame_quality(image_bgr)
        if self.last_lighting:
            result.warnings.extend(self.last_lighting["warnings"])
        result.processing_ms = (host_now_s() - started)*1000
        self.recent_times.append(result.processing_ms)
        self.last_observation = ob
        return result

    def _apply_depth(self, frame: Frame, ob: Observation) -> None:
        """Use calibrated aligned depth as a visible-eye surface origin estimate.

        The result is explicitly not the optical eyeball center. Once trained with
        this origin source, missing depth invalidates the corresponding fit.
        """
        ob.context["eye_origin_source"] = ob.eye_origin_source
        if frame.depth_m is None:
            return
        if ob.features is None or ob.blink:
            return
        metadata = frame.metadata or {}
        raw_intrinsics = metadata.get("depth_intrinsics")
        if metadata.get("depth_aligned_to") != "color" or not metadata.get("depth_deprojection_supported", True) or not raw_intrinsics:
            ob.warnings.append("depth_alignment_or_intrinsics_unavailable")
            ob.eye_origin_camera_m = None
            ob.eye_origin_source = "depth_unavailable"
        else:
            from .depth import eye_origin_from_depth
            intrinsics = CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(raw_intrinsics)
            point = eye_origin_from_depth(frame.depth_m, ob.pupils_px, intrinsics)
            if point is None:
                ob.eye_origin_camera_m = None
                ob.eye_origin_source = "depth_unavailable"
                ob.warnings.append("invalid_depth_at_pupils")
            else:
                ob.eye_origin_camera_m = point
                ob.eye_origin_source = "aligned_depth_pupil_midpoint"
                ob.features = ob.features.copy()
                ob.features[-1] = float(point[2])
                ob.warnings = [w for w in ob.warnings if w != "eye_origin_uses_generic_anatomy"]
                ob.warnings.append("depth_origin_is_visible_eye_surface_not_optical_center")
        ob.context["eye_origin_source"] = ob.eye_origin_source

    def invalidate(self, reason: str, timestamp_s: float | None = None) -> GazeResult:
        self.screen_filter.reset()
        self.direction_filter.reset()
        self.last_observation = None
        self.last_capture_timestamp = None
        stamp = host_now_s() if timestamp_s is None else float(timestamp_s)
        self.last_result = GazeResult(stamp, False, reason, "unavailable", 0.0,
                                      capture_timestamp_s=stamp)
        return self.last_result

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Clear all temporal prediction state after source restart or seek."""
        self.invalidate("tracking_reset")

    def process_observation(self, ob: Observation) -> GazeResult:
        if not np.isfinite(ob.timestamp_s):
            raise ValueError("observation timestamp must be finite")
        self.total += 1
        quality = float(np.clip(ob.quality, 0, 1)) if np.isfinite(ob.quality) else 0.0
        pupil_points = {side:tuple(float(v) for v in xy) for side,xy in ob.pupils_px.items()
                        if len(xy) == 2 and np.isfinite(xy).all()}
        context = {**self.source_context, **ob.context, "image_size": list(ob.image_size)}
        result = GazeResult(ob.timestamp_s, False, ob.message, ob.pupil_method,
                            quality, pupils_px=pupil_points, capture_timestamp_s=ob.timestamp_s,
                            warnings=list(ob.warnings), tracking_context=context,
                            eye_origin_source=ob.eye_origin_source)
        if ob.head_pose is not None:
            angles = (ob.head_pose.yaw_deg, ob.head_pose.pitch_deg, ob.head_pose.roll_deg)
            if np.isfinite(angles).all():
                result.yaw_pitch_roll_deg = tuple(float(v) for v in angles)
            else:
                result.warnings.append("nonfinite_head_pose")
        self.last_result = result
        if ob.blink:
            self.blinks += 1
        if self.last_capture_timestamp is not None and ob.timestamp_s <= self.last_capture_timestamp:
            result.reason = "duplicate_or_reversed_capture_timestamp"
            self.screen_filter.reset()
            self.direction_filter.reset()
            self.rejected += 1
            return result
        self.last_capture_timestamp = ob.timestamp_s
        features_valid = ob.features is not None and np.asarray(ob.features).shape == (len(FEATURE_NAMES),) and np.isfinite(ob.features).all()
        if not features_valid or quality < self.min_quality or ob.blink or not ob.eyes_open:
            if ob.features is not None and not features_valid:
                result.reason = "nonfinite_or_invalid_features"
            elif not ob.eyes_open:
                result.reason = ob.message if ob.blink else "eyes_not_confirmed_open"
            self.screen_filter.reset()
            self.direction_filter.reset()
            self.rejected += 1
            return result

        def model_ready(model) -> bool:
            if model is None:
                return False
            mismatch = model.context_mismatches(context) if hasattr(model, "context_mismatches") else []
            outside = model.outside_envelope(ob.features) if hasattr(model, "outside_envelope") else []
            if mismatch:
                if not result.valid:
                    result.reason = "calibration_context_mismatch"
                result.warnings.append("Changed calibration context: " + ", ".join(mismatch))
                return False
            if outside:
                if not result.valid:
                    result.reason = "outside_calibrated_operating_envelope"
                result.warnings.append("Outside learned range: " + ", ".join(outside))
                return False
            if hasattr(model, "fingerprint"):
                result.model_fingerprint = model.fingerprint
            return True

        if model_ready(self.screen_model):
            try:
                screen = self.screen_model.predict(ob.features)[0]
            except (ValueError, FloatingPointError, np.linalg.LinAlgError) as exc:
                screen = np.array([np.nan, np.nan])
                result.warnings.append(str(exc))
            # Do not silently clamp off-screen data into plausible in-screen coordinates.
            if np.all(np.isfinite(screen)) and np.all(screen >= 0) and np.all(screen <= 1):
                filtered = self.screen_filter.update(screen, ob.timestamp_s)
                size = self.screen_model.metadata["screen_size"]
                result.gaze_screen_normalized = tuple(float(x) for x in filtered)
                scale = np.asarray(size, dtype=float) - 1
                result.raw_gaze_screen_px = tuple(float(v) for v in screen * scale)
                result.gaze_screen_px = tuple(float(v) for v in filtered * scale)
                result.valid = result.screen_valid = True
                result.reason = "screen_gaze_estimated"
            else:
                self.screen_filter.reset()
                result.reason = "screen_prediction_out_of_bounds"
        elif self.screen_model is not None:
            self.screen_filter.reset()
        if model_ready(self.spatial_model):
            if self.extrinsics is None or ob.eye_origin_camera_m is None:
                result.warnings.append("world_geometry_unavailable_for_this_frame")
            else:
                try:
                    predicted_cam = self.spatial_model.predict(ob.features)[0]
                    origin = camera_to_world_point(ob.eye_origin_camera_m, self.extrinsics)
                    direction = camera_to_world_direction(predicted_cam, self.extrinsics)
                    direction = self.direction_filter.update(direction, ob.timestamp_s)
                    direction = unit_vector(direction)
                    result.ray_origin_world_m = tuple(float(x) for x in origin)
                    result.ray_direction_world = tuple(float(x) for x in direction)
                    hit = first_surface_hit(origin, direction, self.surfaces)
                    if hit:
                        surface, coord = hit
                        result.surface_name = surface.name
                        result.surface_xy_m = coord["xy_m"]
                        result.surface_xy_px = coord["xy_px"]
                        result.valid = result.spatial_valid = True
                        result.reason = "spatial_surface_hit"
                    else:
                        result.spatial_valid = True
                        if not result.valid:
                            result.valid = True
                            result.reason = "spatial_ray_only_no_surface_hit"
                except (ValueError, FloatingPointError, np.linalg.LinAlgError) as e:
                    self.direction_filter.reset()
                    result.warnings.append(f"invalid_spatial_ray: {e}")
        elif self.spatial_model is not None:
            self.direction_filter.reset()
        if self.screen_model is None and self.spatial_model is None:
            result.reason = "tracking_only_uncalibrated"
        if result.valid:
            self.valid += 1
        else:
            self.rejected += 1
        return result

    def metrics(self) -> dict:
        latencies = np.asarray(tuple(self.recent_times),float)
        return {"frames":self.total, "gaze_valid":self.valid,
                "rejected":self.rejected, "blink_frames":self.blinks,
                "processing_median_ms":float(np.median(latencies)) if len(latencies) else None,
                "processing_p95_ms":float(np.percentile(latencies,95)) if len(latencies) else None,
                "calibrated_screen":self.screen_model is not None,
                "calibrated_spatial":self.spatial_model is not None,
                "physical_accuracy_verified":False,
                "lighting":self.last_lighting}

    def close(self):
        self.tracker.close()


def draw_diagnostics(image_bgr: np.ndarray, result: GazeResult,
                     ob: Observation | None = None, fps: float | None = None) -> np.ndarray:
    frame = image_bgr.copy()
    h,w = frame.shape[:2]
    for side, pupil in result.pupils_px.items():
        cv2.circle(frame, (round(pupil[0]), round(pupil[1])), 5, (0,255,170), 2)
    if ob and ob.landmarks_px:
        for i in (1,152,33,263,61,291):
            if i in ob.landmarks_px:
                x,y = ob.landmarks_px[i]
                cv2.circle(frame,(round(x),round(y)),3,(255,125,40),-1)
    cv2.rectangle(frame, (6,6), (min(w-6,600),min(h-6,115)),(18,24,34),-1)
    lines = [f"GAZESPACE  {'VALID' if result.valid else 'NO GAZE'}  | {result.reason}",
             f"quality {result.quality:.2f} | {result.source} | processing {result.processing_ms:.1f}ms",
             f"FPS {fps:.1f}" if fps is not None else "FPS sampling...",
             "Q quit | gaze requires calibration, not landmarks alone"]
    for i,line in enumerate(lines):
        cv2.putText(frame,line[:85],(14,27+i*24),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.52,
                    (120,235,190) if i==0 else (210,218,224),1,cv2.LINE_AA)
    if result.gaze_screen_normalized:
        # visualizations are camera overlays, NOT actual physical on-screen markers
        view_w,view_h = min(300,w-24), min(170,h-140)
        if view_w>50 and view_h>50:
            x0,y0 = w-view_w-12,h-view_h-12
            cv2.rectangle(frame,(x0,y0),(x0+view_w,y0+view_h),(40,48,65),-1)
            cv2.rectangle(frame,(x0,y0),(x0+view_w,y0+view_h),(170,170,170),1)
            gx,gy = result.gaze_screen_normalized
            cv2.drawMarker(frame,(int(x0+gx*view_w),int(y0+gy*view_h)),
                           (0,215,255),cv2.MARKER_CROSS,18,2)
            cv2.putText(frame,"SCREEN GAZE (INSET)",(x0+7,y0+18),
                        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,0.4,(220,220,220),1)
    return frame

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/engine.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/filtering.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 39720a794a1e51d8d635e65dcfb84d7cc5ca1f0a

"""Timestamp-aware One Euro smoothing; rejected observations reset stale output."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import numpy as np

class OneEuro:
    def __init__(self, min_cutoff=1.1, beta=0.014, d_cutoff=1.0,
                 max_gap_s=0.35):
        if not np.isfinite([min_cutoff, beta, d_cutoff, max_gap_s]).all() or min_cutoff <= 0 or d_cutoff <= 0 or beta < 0 or max_gap_s <= 0:
            raise ValueError("filter parameters must be finite with positive cutoffs/gap and nonnegative beta")
        self.min_cutoff, self.beta = float(min_cutoff), float(beta)
        self.d_cutoff, self.max_gap_s = float(d_cutoff), float(max_gap_s)
        self.reset()

    def reset(self):
        self.x = self.raw_x = self.dx = self.timestamp = None

    @staticmethod
    def alpha(cutoff, dt):
        tau = 1 / (2 * math.pi * np.asarray(cutoff))
        return 1 / (1 + tau / dt)

    def update(self, x, timestamp_s):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or not np.isfinite(timestamp_s):
            raise ValueError("nonfinite filter input")
        if self.timestamp is None or timestamp_s <= self.timestamp or timestamp_s - self.timestamp > self.max_gap_s:
            self.x = x.copy()
            self.raw_x = x.copy()
            self.dx = np.zeros_like(x)
            self.timestamp = timestamp_s
            return x.copy()
        dt = max(1e-4, timestamp_s - self.timestamp)
        if x.shape != self.x.shape:
            raise ValueError("filter input shape changed")
        derivative = (x - self.raw_x) / dt
        self.raw_x = x.copy()
        a_d = self.alpha(self.d_cutoff, dt)
        self.dx = a_d * derivative + (1 - a_d) * self.dx
        cutoff = self.min_cutoff + self.beta * np.abs(self.dx)
        a = self.alpha(cutoff, dt)
        self.x = a * x + (1 - a) * self.x
        self.timestamp = timestamp_s
        return self.x.copy()

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/filtering.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/geometry.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Camera intrinsics, head pose, 3D rigid transforms and bounded ray/surface mapping."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import numpy as np
import cv2
from .types import CameraIntrinsics, CameraExtrinsics, HeadPose, Surface

# Neutral head axes match the OpenCV camera: X right, Y down, Z away.
# Looking straight into the camera is identity pose and gaze direction (0,0,-1).
# This is generic anatomy, not a measurement of the person's eye origin.
FACE_INDICES = (1, 152, 33, 263, 61, 291)
FACE_TEMPLATE_M = np.array([
    (0.000, 0.000, 0.000),       # nose
    (0.000, 0.0636, 0.0125),    # chin, below and behind nose
    (-0.0433, -0.0327, 0.0260), # subject's right / image-left outer eye corner
    (0.0433, -0.0327, 0.0260),  # subject's left / image-right outer eye corner
    (-0.0289, 0.0289, 0.0241),
    (0.0289, 0.0289, 0.0241),
], dtype=np.float64)
EYE_MIDPOINT_HEAD_M = np.array([0.0, -0.031, 0.018], dtype=np.float64)
HEAD_MODEL_VERSION = "generic-camera-facing-v2"


def finite_vector(value, name="vector") -> np.ndarray:
    vector = np.asarray(value, dtype=float)
    if vector.shape != (3,) or not np.isfinite(vector).all():
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a finite three-vector")
    return vector


def unit_vector(value, name="direction") -> np.ndarray:
    vector = finite_vector(value, name)
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vector))
    if norm < 1e-9:
        raise ValueError(f"zero {name}")
    return vector / norm


def approximate_intrinsics(width: int, height: int) -> CameraIntrinsics:
    # Only for rendering/relative head orientation. NOT a calibrated 3D geometry source.
    if min(width, height) < 2:
        raise ValueError("image dimensions must be >= 2")
    fx = float(max(width, height))
    return CameraIntrinsics(width, height,
                            np.array([[fx, 0, width / 2], [0, fx, height / 2],
                                      [0, 0, 1]], dtype=float),
                            np.zeros((5, 1), dtype=float), source="approximate")


def estimate_head_pose(points: dict[int, tuple[float, float]],
                       intr: CameraIntrinsics) -> HeadPose | None:
    intr.validate()
    if any(i not in points for i in FACE_INDICES):
        return None
    img = np.array([points[i] for i in FACE_INDICES], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(img)):
        return None
    # Refuse insufficient spatial extent and mirrored/unordered eye corners.
    if img[2, 0] >= img[3, 0] or np.linalg.norm(img[3] - img[2]) < 12:
        return None
    try:
        ok, rvec, tvec = cv2.solvePnP(FACE_TEMPLATE_M, img, intr.camera_matrix,
                                    intr.distortion, flags=cv2.SOLVEPNP_ITERATIVE)
    except cv2.error:
        return None
    if not ok:
        return None
    rotation, _ = cv2.Rodrigues(rvec)
    projected, _ = cv2.projectPoints(FACE_TEMPLATE_M, rvec, tvec,
                                     intr.camera_matrix, intr.distortion)
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.sum((projected.reshape(-1, 2) - img) ** 2, axis=1))))
    if not np.isfinite(rms) or not np.isfinite(tvec).all() or not 0.10 < tvec[2, 0] < 5.0:
        return None
    angles, *_ = cv2.RQDecomp3x3(rotation)
    pitch, yaw, roll = (float(v) for v in angles)
    return HeadPose(rotation, tvec.reshape(3), yaw, pitch, roll, rms)


def eye_origin_from_pose(pose: HeadPose) -> np.ndarray:
    return finite_vector(pose.rotation @ EYE_MIDPOINT_HEAD_M + pose.translation_m, "eye origin")


def camera_to_world_point(point_camera: np.ndarray, ext: CameraExtrinsics) -> np.ndarray:
    ext.validate()
    return ext.rotation_world_to_camera.T @ (finite_vector(point_camera, "camera point") - ext.translation_world_to_camera_m)


def world_to_camera_point(point_world: np.ndarray, ext: CameraExtrinsics) -> np.ndarray:
    ext.validate()
    return ext.rotation_world_to_camera @ finite_vector(point_world, "world point") + ext.translation_world_to_camera_m


def camera_to_world_direction(dir_camera: np.ndarray, ext: CameraExtrinsics) -> np.ndarray:
    ext.validate()
    return unit_vector(ext.rotation_world_to_camera.T @ unit_vector(dir_camera))


def project_world_point(point_world: np.ndarray, intr: CameraIntrinsics, ext: CameraExtrinsics) -> np.ndarray:
    rvec, _ = cv2.Rodrigues(ext.rotation_world_to_camera)
    xy, _ = cv2.projectPoints(np.asarray(point_world, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3),
                              rvec, ext.translation_world_to_camera_m, intr.camera_matrix, intr.distortion)
    return xy.reshape(-1, 2)


def ray_surface_intersection(origin: np.ndarray, direction: np.ndarray,
                             surface: Surface, min_t_m: float = 0.02):
    """Returns dict containing distance, metric uv and pixel uv, or None.

    Rectangular axes must be orthogonal; they are normalized before mapping.
    Surface.two_sided=False accepts only rays arriving against cross(u,v).
    Pixel centers span 0..width-1 and 0..height-1, including boundary hits.
    """
    surface.validate()
    if not np.isfinite(min_t_m) or min_t_m < 0:
        raise ValueError("minimum ray distance must be finite and nonnegative")
    p0 = np.asarray(surface.origin_world_m, dtype=float)
    u, v = np.asarray(surface.u_vector, dtype=float), np.asarray(surface.v_vector, dtype=float)
    u_norm, v_norm = np.linalg.norm(u), np.linalg.norm(v)
    if u_norm < 1e-10 or v_norm < 1e-10:
        raise ValueError("invalid surface axes")
    u, v = u / u_norm, v / v_norm
    n = np.cross(u, v)
    if np.linalg.norm(n) < 1e-8:
        raise ValueError("surface axes are parallel")
    n /= np.linalg.norm(n)
    origin, d = finite_vector(origin, "ray origin"), unit_vector(direction, "gaze direction")
    denom = float(np.dot(n, d))
    if abs(denom) < 1e-8:
        return None
    if not surface.two_sided and denom >= 0:
        return None
    t = float(np.dot(n, p0 - origin) / denom)
    if t < min_t_m:
        return None
    p = origin + t * d
    uv = np.linalg.lstsq(np.column_stack((u, v)), p - p0, rcond=None)[0]
    x, y = float(uv[0]), float(uv[1])
    if not (-1e-6 <= x <= surface.width_m + 1e-6 and -1e-6 <= y <= surface.height_m + 1e-6):
        return None
    x, y = float(np.clip(x, 0, surface.width_m)), float(np.clip(y, 0, surface.height_m))
    pixels = None
    if surface.pixel_size:
        pixels = (x / surface.width_m * (surface.pixel_size[0] - 1),
                  y / surface.height_m * (surface.pixel_size[1] - 1))
    return {"distance_m": t, "xy_m": (x, y), "xy_px": pixels, "point_world_m": tuple(float(z) for z in p)}


def first_surface_hit(origin: np.ndarray, direction: np.ndarray, surfaces: list[Surface]):
    candidates = []
    for surf in surfaces:
        hit = ray_surface_intersection(origin, direction, surf)
        if hit is not None:
            candidates.append((hit["distance_m"], surf, hit))
    return min(candidates, key=lambda x: x[0])[1:] if candidates else None


def angular_error_deg(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    a, b = unit_vector(a), unit_vector(b)
    return float(math.degrees(math.acos(float(np.clip(np.dot(a, b), -1, 1)))))

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/geometry.py =====

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Basic exposure/blur/dynamic-range warnings for changing RGB/IR illumination.

These heuristics are diagnostics, not calibrated physical measurements.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
import cv2


def frame_quality(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    if image_bgr.ndim==3:
        g=cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr,cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    else:
        g=image_bgr
    sample=g[::4,::4]
    p05,p50,p95=np.percentile(sample,(5,50,95))
    dark_fraction=float(np.mean(sample<12))
    bright_fraction=float(np.mean(sample>245))
    contrast=float(p95-p05)
    # Stable downscaled Laplacian to keep CPU impact small.
    blur_variance=float(cv2.Laplacian(cv2.resize(g,(320,180)),cv2.CV_64F).var())
    alerts=[]
    if p50<35:
        alerts.append("underexposure_or_weak_illumination")
    if p50>225:
        alerts.append("overexposure_or_strong_illumination")
    if dark_fraction>.65:
        alerts.append("substantial_dark_clipping")
    if bright_fraction>.65:
        alerts.append("substantial_bright_clipping")
    if contrast<25:
        alerts.append("low_dynamic_range")
    if blur_variance<10:
        alerts.append("possible_defocus_or_motion_blur")
    return {"p05":float(p05),"median":float(p50),"p95":float(p95),
            "dark_fraction":dark_fraction,"bright_fraction":bright_fraction,
            "contrast":contrast,"sharpness_laplacian":blur_variance,"warnings":alerts}

"""Camera-only preview for inspection and selecting infrared eye regions."""
from .types import Observation


class PreviewTracker:
    """Expose frames without loading a face model or producing gaze coordinates."""

    mode = "preview"
    pupil_polarity = "dark"
    intrinsics = None
    roi_normalized = None

    def context(self, image_size=None):
        value = {"tracking_mode": "preview", "feature_method": "none"}
        if image_size is not None:
            value["image_size"] = list(image_size)
        return value

    def process(self, image_bgr, timestamp_s):
        size = (image_bgr.shape[1], image_bgr.shape[0])
        return Observation(timestamp_s, size, message="preview_only_no_tracking",
                           context=self.context(size))

    def close(self):
        pass
