===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/pupil.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: b6368926dd4197a0973a1f39d5ab0d245ca01a4a

"""IR-compatible dark/bright pupil detection from eye ROI, with glint diagnostics."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import cv2
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class Pupil:
    xy: tuple[float, float]
    radius_px: float
    confidence: float
    ellipse_axes: tuple[float, float]
    glint_xy: tuple[float, float] | None = None


def preprocess_ir(image: np.ndarray, clip_limit: float = 2.0) -> np.ndarray:
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if image.ndim == 3 else image
    if gray.dtype != np.uint8:
        raise ValueError("preprocess_ir expects uint8")
    return cv2.createCLAHE(clipLimit=clip_limit, tileGridSize=(8, 8)).apply(gray)


def detect_pupil(image: np.ndarray, roi: tuple[int, int, int, int],
                 polarity: str = "dark", min_radius: float = 3.0,
                 max_radius: float = 40.0) -> Pupil | None:
    """Detect pupil by thresholded near-round contour; emits no detection on failure.

    roi is x,y,width,height in image coordinates. Not a biometric guarantee.
    Can process grayscale active-IR (dark or bright pupils) or RGB inputs.
    """
    if polarity not in ("dark", "bright"):
        raise ValueError("polarity must be 'dark' or 'bright'")
    if not np.isfinite([min_radius, max_radius]).all() or min_radius <= 0 or max_radius <= min_radius:
        raise ValueError("pupil radius limits must be finite, positive and ordered")
    if image is None or image.dtype != np.uint8 or image.ndim not in (2, 3):
        raise ValueError("pupil detection expects uint8 grayscale or BGR image")
    if image.ndim == 3:
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    else:
        gray = image
    height, width = gray.shape[:2]
    x, y, w, h = (int(v) for v in roi)
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = max(x, 0), max(y, 0), min(width, x + w), min(height, y + h)
    if x1-x0 < 12 or y1-y0 < 12:
        return None
    cropped = gray[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    if float(np.percentile(cropped, 95) - np.percentile(cropped, 5)) < 8:
        return None
    filtered = cv2.GaussianBlur(cropped, (5, 5), 0)
    flag = cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV if polarity == "dark" else cv2.THRESH_BINARY
    _, mask = cv2.threshold(filtered, 0, 255, flag | cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    mask = cv2.morphologyEx(mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8))
    # Ignore border-connected dark shadows and lens edge artifacts.
    # Pupils can be nested inside a bright sclera surrounded by dark eyelids.
    # RETR_EXTERNAL drops that inner component; contour/contrast checks below
    # select real candidates without assuming the pupil is an outer component.
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(mask, cv2.RETR_LIST, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    center_x, center_y = (x1 - x0) / 2, (y1 - y0) / 2
    candidates = []
    for c in contours:
        area = cv2.contourArea(c)
        if area < np.pi * min_radius**2 * 0.45 or area > np.pi * max_radius**2 * 1.8:
            continue
        bx, by, bw, bh = cv2.boundingRect(c)
        if bx <= 0 or by <= 0 or bx + bw >= x1 - x0 - 1 or by + bh >= y1 - y0 - 1:
            continue
        if len(c) < 5:
            continue
        (cx, cy), axes, _ = cv2.fitEllipse(c)
        major, minor = max(axes), min(axes)
        if major <= 1 or minor / major < 0.40:
            continue
        radius = (major + minor) / 4
        if not (min_radius <= radius <= max_radius):
            continue
        # A near-round contour is insufficient: require optical contrast against
        # its immediate surroundings (also rejects near-uniform compression noise).
        yy, xx = np.ogrid[:cropped.shape[0], :cropped.shape[1]]
        distance2 = (xx - cx)**2 + (yy - cy)**2
        core = cropped[distance2 < (max(2, radius * .65))**2]
        ring = cropped[(distance2 > (radius * 1.2)**2) & (distance2 < (radius * 1.7)**2)]
        if not len(core) or not len(ring):
            continue
        contrast = float(np.median(ring) - np.median(core))
        if polarity == "bright":
            contrast *= -1
        if contrast < 8:
            continue
        perimeter = max(1.0, cv2.arcLength(c, True))
        circularity = 4 * np.pi * area / perimeter**2
        relative_center = np.hypot((cx-center_x)/max(1,w), (cy-center_y)/max(1,h))
        score = float(np.clip(np.clip(circularity, 0, 1) * (minor/major)**0.5 *
                      max(0, 1 - relative_center) * min(1., contrast / 35.), 0, 1))
        if score >= 0.15:
            candidates.append((score, cx, cy, radius, (float(axes[0]), float(axes[1]))))
    if not candidates:
        return None
    score, cx, cy, r, axes = max(candidates)
    # Glint is an optional optical quality diagnostic, not a true 3D PCCR model.
    local = cropped.copy()
    glare_threshold = np.percentile(local, 99.7)
    glare = (local >= max(glare_threshold, 245)).astype(np.uint8)
    glint = None
    n, labels, stats, centers = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(glare, 8)
    best = []
    for i in range(1, n):
        if 1 <= stats[i, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA] <= 30:
            gx, gy = centers[i]
            if np.hypot(gx - cx, gy - cy) <= max(15, r * 2):
                best.append((np.hypot(gx - cx, gy - cy), gx, gy))
    if best:
        _, gx, gy = min(best)
        glint = (float(x0 + gx), float(y0 + gy))
    return Pupil((float(x0 + cx), float(y0 + cy)), float(r), score, axes, glint)


def ratio_xy(point: tuple[float, float], roi: tuple[int, int, int, int]) -> tuple[float, float]:
    x,y,w,h = roi
    return ((point[0] - x)/w, (point[1] - y)/h)

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/pupil.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gazespace/runtime.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 734bc8957c17f874e72987377af1cdef9da673a2

"""Thread-owned acquisition and tracking with a bounded latest-frame mailbox.

The GUI polls snapshot(); neither camera IO nor MediaPipe runs on the GUI
thread. Invalid state transitions immediately remove active gaze coordinates.
No source, engine or SDK object is closed from a thread currently using it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import deque
from dataclasses import replace
import copy
import math
from threading import Condition, Event, Lock, Thread, current_thread
from typing import Callable, Any

from .types import Frame, GazeResult, Observation
from .clock import HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, host_now_s


class TrackingRuntime:
    def __init__(self, source_factory: Callable, engine_factory: Callable, *,
                 max_age_s: float = .4, reconnect_attempts: int = 5,
                 reconnect_delay_s: float = .25, on_result: Callable | None = None):
        if max_age_s <= 0 or reconnect_attempts < 0 or reconnect_delay_s < 0:
            raise ValueError("Invalid runtime age/reconnect settings")
        self.source_factory, self.engine_factory = source_factory, engine_factory
        self.max_age_s, self.reconnect_attempts = float(max_age_s), int(reconnect_attempts)
        self.reconnect_delay_s, self.on_result = float(reconnect_delay_s), on_result
        self._lock = Lock()
        self._condition = Condition(self._lock)
        self._stop, self._capture_done = Event(), Event()
        self._capture_thread: Thread | None = None
        self._process_thread: Thread | None = None
        self._source = None
        self._engine = None
        self._state = "idle"
        self._last_error: str | None = None
        self._paused = False
        self._generation = 0
        self._pending: tuple[int, Frame, float] | None = None
        self._frame: Frame | None = None
        self._observation: Observation | None = None
        self._result: GazeResult | None = None
        self._last_received: float | None = None
        self._camera: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._engine_metrics = {}
        self._capture_times = deque(maxlen=120)
        self._process_times = deque(maxlen=120)
        self._counters = {"received_frames": 0, "processed_frames": 0,
                          "dropped_frames": 0, "stale_frames": 0, "reconnections": 0}
        self._callback_error = None

    @property
    def engine(self):
        """Compatibility read access; do not process/close it outside its worker."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._engine

    def start(self) -> None:
        with self._condition:
            if self._alive_locked():
                raise RuntimeError("Runtime already active or previous camera read has not stopped")
            self._stop.clear()
            self._capture_done.clear()
            self._state, self._last_error, self._paused = "starting", None, False
            self._generation += 1
            self._pending = self._frame = self._observation = self._result = None
            self._last_received = None
            self._capture_times.clear()
            self._process_times.clear()
            self._counters = {key: 0 for key in self._counters}
            self._engine_metrics = {}
            self._callback_error = None
            self._capture_thread = Thread(target=self._capture_loop,
                                          name="gazespace-acquisition", daemon=True)
            self._process_thread = Thread(target=self._process_loop,
                                          name="gazespace-processing", daemon=True)
            self._process_thread.start()
            self._capture_thread.start()

    def pause(self) -> None:
        with self._condition:
            if self._state not in ("starting", "running", "reconnecting", "paused"):
                return
            self._paused = True
            self._state = "paused"
            self._generation += 1
            self._pending = None
            self._invalidate_locked("paused")
            self._condition.notify_all()

    def resume(self) -> None:
        with self._condition:
            if not self._paused or not self._alive_locked():
                return
            self._paused = False
            self._state = "starting"
            self._generation += 1
            self._pending = None
            self._invalidate_locked("awaiting_fresh_frame")
            self._condition.notify_all()

    def stop(self, timeout: float = 3.0) -> bool:
        """Request shutdown; return False when an OS/driver call remains blocked.

        A failed stop remains visibly stopped-for-output, cannot be restarted,
        and is never disguised by concurrently releasing an active camera.
        """
        if timeout < 0:
            raise ValueError("timeout must be nonnegative")
        deadline = host_now_s() + timeout
        self._stop.set()
        with self._condition:
            self._generation += 1
            self._pending = None
            self._state = "stopping"
            self._invalidate_locked("stopped")
            source = self._source
            self._condition.notify_all()
        if source is not None:
            try:
                source.request_stop()
            except Exception:
                pass  # A request is optional; never cross-thread close the source.
        for worker in (self._capture_thread, self._process_thread):
            if worker is not None and worker is not current_thread():
                worker.join(max(0.0, deadline - host_now_s()))
        with self._condition:
            completed = not self._alive_locked()
            self._state = "stopped" if completed else "stopping_blocked"
            if not completed:
                self._last_error = "Camera/processing call has not returned; output disabled, restart prevented"
            self._condition.notify_all()
        return completed

    def _alive_locked(self) -> bool:
        return any(t is not None and t.is_alive() for t in (self._capture_thread, self._process_thread))

    @staticmethod
    def _invalid(result: GazeResult, reason: str) -> GazeResult:
        invalid = copy.deepcopy(result)
        invalid.valid = False
        invalid.reason = reason
        for field in ("gaze_screen_px", "gaze_screen_normalized", "raw_gaze_screen_px",
                      "ray_origin_world_m", "ray_direction_world", "surface_name",
                      "surface_xy_m", "surface_xy_px"):
            if hasattr(invalid, field):
                setattr(invalid, field, None)
        for field in ("screen_valid", "spatial_valid"):
            if hasattr(invalid, field):
                setattr(invalid, field, False)
        return invalid

    def _invalidate_locked(self, reason: str) -> None:
        if self._result is not None:
            self._result = self._invalid(self._result, reason)
        self._observation = None

    def _capture_loop(self) -> None:
        consecutive_reconnects = 0
        try:
            while not self._stop.is_set():
                source = None
                try:
                    source = self.source_factory()
                    with self._condition:
                        self._source = source
                        self._camera = copy.deepcopy(source.capabilities()) if hasattr(source, "capabilities") else {}
                    while not self._stop.is_set():
                        # Live devices keep draining during pause so resuming
                        # cannot replay buffered pre-pause camera frames. Files
                        # and generated demos pause their presentation instead.
                        if not getattr(source, "is_live", True):
                            with self._condition:
                                self._condition.wait_for(lambda: not self._paused or self._stop.is_set(), timeout=.2)
                                if self._paused:
                                    continue
                            if self._stop.is_set():
                                break
                        frame = source.read()
                        acquired_at = host_now_s()
                        received = acquired_at
                        if self._stop.is_set():
                            break
                        if frame is None:
                            if getattr(source, "eof", False) or not getattr(source, "is_live", True):
                                with self._condition:
                                    if self._state != "fault":
                                        self._state = "exhausted"
                                    self._generation += 1
                                    self._pending = None
                                    self._invalidate_locked("source_exhausted")
                                return
                            raise OSError("Camera returned no frame")
                        frame_received = getattr(frame, "received_monotonic_s", None)
                        if frame_received is not None:
                            received = float(frame_received)
                        declared_clock = frame.metadata.get("host_clock_domain")
                        if ((declared_clock is not None and declared_clock != HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN)
                                or not math.isfinite(received) or received > acquired_at):
                            raise ValueError("Frame received_monotonic_s must use gazespace.clock.host_now_s()")
                        consecutive_reconnects = 0
                        with self._condition:
                            self._counters["received_frames"] += 1
                            self._capture_times.append(host_now_s())
                            if self._paused:
                                continue
                            if self._pending is not None:
                                self._counters["dropped_frames"] += 1
                            self._pending = (self._generation, frame, received)
                            self._condition.notify_all()
                except Exception as exc:
                    if self._stop.is_set():
                        break
                    consecutive_reconnects += 1
                    retry = (source is None or getattr(source, "is_live", True)) and consecutive_reconnects <= self.reconnect_attempts
                    with self._condition:
                        self._last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                        self._state = "reconnecting" if retry else "fault"
                        self._generation += 1
                        self._pending = None
                        self._invalidate_locked("camera_disconnected" if retry else "camera_fault")
                        if retry:
                            self._counters["reconnections"] += 1
                        self._condition.notify_all()
                    if not retry:
                        return
                finally:
                    if source is not None:
                        try:
                            source.close()
                        except Exception as exc:
                            with self._condition:
                                self._last_error = f"Camera close: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                    with self._condition:
                        if self._source is source:
                            self._source = None
                if self._stop.wait(min(2.0, self.reconnect_delay_s * (2 ** min(consecutive_reconnects - 1, 4)))):
                    break
        finally:
            self._capture_done.set()
            with self._condition:
                self._condition.notify_all()

    def _process_loop(self) -> None:
        engine = None
        previous_generation = None
        try:
            engine = self.engine_factory()
            with self._condition:
                self._engine = engine
            while not self._stop.is_set():
                with self._condition:
                    self._condition.wait_for(lambda: self._stop.is_set() or self._pending is not None or
                                             self._capture_done.is_set(), timeout=.2)
                    if self._stop.is_set():
                        break
                    if self._pending is None:
                        if self._capture_done.is_set():
                            break
                        continue
                    generation, frame, received = self._pending
                    self._pending = None
                    paused = self._paused
                if paused:
                    continue
                if previous_generation is not None and previous_generation != generation:
                    if hasattr(engine, "reset"):
                        engine.reset()
                    else:
                        for name in ("screen_filter", "direction_filter"):
                            filter_obj = getattr(engine, name, None)
                            if filter_obj is not None:
                                filter_obj.reset()
                previous_generation = generation
                # Drop over-age frames before expensive inference.
                age = host_now_s() - received
                if not math.isfinite(age) or age < 0 or age > self.max_age_s:
                    with self._condition:
                        self._counters["stale_frames"] += 1
                        self._invalidate_locked("invalid_host_clock" if not math.isfinite(age) or age < 0
                                                else "stale_capture")
                    continue
                result = (engine.process_frame(frame) if hasattr(engine, "process_frame")
                          else engine.process(frame.image, frame.timestamp_s))
                observation = getattr(engine, "last_observation", None)
                metrics = engine.metrics() if hasattr(engine, "metrics") else {}
                with self._condition:
                    if generation != self._generation or self._paused or self._stop.is_set():
                        continue
                    age = host_now_s() - received
                    if not math.isfinite(age) or age < 0 or age > self.max_age_s:
                        result = self._invalid(result, "invalid_host_clock" if not math.isfinite(age) or age < 0
                                               else "stale_processing_result")
                        self._counters["stale_frames"] += 1
                        observation = None
                    self._frame, self._result, self._observation = frame, result, observation
                    self._last_received = received
                    self._engine_metrics = metrics
                    self._counters["processed_frames"] += 1
                    self._process_times.append(host_now_s())
                    self._state, self._last_error = "running", None
                if self.on_result is not None:
                    try:
                        self.on_result(frame, observation, result)
                    except Exception as exc:
                        with self._condition:
                            self._callback_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        except Exception as exc:
            with self._condition:
                self._last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                self._state = "fault"
                self._generation += 1
                self._pending = None
                self._invalidate_locked("processing_fault")
                source = self._source
            self._stop.set()
            if source is not None:
                try:
                    source.request_stop()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        finally:
            if engine is not None:
                try:
                    engine.close()
                except Exception as exc:
                    with self._condition:
                        self._last_error = f"Engine close: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            with self._condition:
                self._engine = None
                self._condition.notify_all()

    def set_synthetic_target(self, x: float | None, y: float | None = None) -> bool:
        """Only the synthetic source supports this; no control affects a person."""
        with self._lock:
            source = self._source
        if source is None or not hasattr(source, "set_target"):
            return False
        if x is None:
            source.clear_target()
        else:
            source.set_target(float(x), float(y))
        return True

    def clear_callback_error(self) -> None:
        """Acknowledge a reported sink error before starting a new recording."""
        with self._condition:
            self._callback_error = None

    @staticmethod
    def _fps(times) -> float:
        return (len(times) - 1) / max(1e-9, times[-1] - times[0]) if len(times) > 1 else 0.0

    def snapshot(self, include_frame: bool = True) -> dict:
        with self._lock:
            # Sample under the lock: a worker must not publish a newer arrival
            # between sampling the clock and reading its receive timestamp.
            now = host_now_s()
            age = None if self._last_received is None else now - self._last_received
            clock_invalid = age is not None and (not math.isfinite(age) or age < 0)
            if clock_invalid:
                age = None
            result = copy.deepcopy(self._result)
            observation = copy.deepcopy(self._observation)
            state = self._state
            if result is not None and (state != "running" or age is None or age > self.max_age_s):
                reason = "invalid_host_clock" if clock_invalid else "stale_capture"
                result = self._invalid(result, reason if state == "running" else state)
                observation = None
            frame = None
            if include_frame and self._frame is not None:
                frame = replace(self._frame, image=self._frame.image.copy(),
                                metadata=copy.deepcopy(getattr(self._frame, "metadata", {})),
                                depth_m=None if getattr(self._frame, "depth_m", None) is None else self._frame.depth_m.copy())
            metrics = {**copy.deepcopy(self._engine_metrics), **self._counters,
                       "capture_fps": self._fps(self._capture_times),
                       "processing_fps": self._fps(self._process_times),
                       "host_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN,
                       "capture_age_ms": None if age is None else age * 1000,
                       "callback_error": self._callback_error}
            return {"state": state, "running": state == "running" and self._alive_locked(),
                    "alive": self._alive_locked(), "last_error": self._last_error,
                    "latest": result.as_dict() if result is not None else None,
                    "result": result, "frame": frame, "observation": observation,
                    "metrics": metrics, "camera": copy.deepcopy(self._camera)}

    def telemetry(self) -> dict:
        """JSON-only API representation; never includes image or biometric features."""
        value = self.snapshot(include_frame=False)
        return {k: value[k] for k in ("state", "running", "alive", "last_error", "latest", "metrics", "camera")}

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Loopback telemetry API with stale-result protection and optional bearer auth.

No endpoint exposes raw camera images or landmark/feature vectors. External
browser origins and non-loopback Host headers are rejected by default.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
import hmac
import json
from pathlib import Path
from threading import Lock
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from .runtime import TrackingRuntime


class GazeService:
    """Compatibility adapter for the CLI and direct embedding.

    Prefer engine_factory=... so MediaPipe is constructed in its worker thread.
    The legacy engine positional argument remains available for host-owned
    engines; service.stop never closes an engine while processing is active.
    """
    def __init__(self, engine=None, source_factory=None, record_jsonl: str | None = None,
                 *, engine_factory=None, max_age_s: float = .4):
        if source_factory is None or (engine is None and engine_factory is None):
            raise ValueError("source_factory and engine/engine_factory are required")
        self.engine = engine
        self._factory = engine_factory or (lambda: engine)
        self.record_jsonl = record_jsonl
        self._stream = None
        self._record_lock = Lock()
        self.runtime = TrackingRuntime(source_factory, self._build_engine,
                                       max_age_s=max_age_s, on_result=self._record)

    def _build_engine(self):
        self.engine = self._factory()
        return self.engine

    def _record(self, frame, observation, result):
        if not self.record_jsonl:
            return
        with self._record_lock:
            if self._stream is None:
                path = Path(self.record_jsonl)
                path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                self._stream = path.open("a", encoding="utf-8")
            self._stream.write(json.dumps(result.as_dict(), allow_nan=False) + "\n")
            self._stream.flush()

    def start(self):
        self.runtime.start()

    def stop(self, timeout: float = 3.0):
        completed = self.runtime.stop(timeout)
        # The callback owns its stream until the worker has stopped.
        if completed:
            with self._record_lock:
                if self._stream is not None:
                    self._stream.close()
                    self._stream = None
        return completed

    def snapshot(self):
        return self.runtime.telemetry()

    def metrics(self):
        return self.runtime.telemetry()["metrics"]


def _loopback_host(value: str) -> bool:
    try:
        host = urlsplit("//" + value).hostname
    except ValueError:
        return False
    return host in ("localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1")


def _origin_allowed(value: str | None) -> bool:
    if value is None:
        return True  # Python/native clients do not send a browser Origin.
    try:
        origin = urlsplit(value)
        return origin.scheme in ("http", "https") and origin.hostname in ("localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1")
    except ValueError:
        return False


def create_api(service: GazeService | TrackingRuntime, *, token: str | None = None,
               manage_lifecycle: bool = False):
    try:
        from fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket, Request
        from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
        from starlette.websockets import WebSocketDisconnect
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("API mode requires pip install 'gazespace[api]'") from exc
    if token is not None and len(token) < 16:
        raise ValueError("API bearer tokens must contain at least 16 characters")

    def snapshot():
        return service.telemetry() if isinstance(service, TrackingRuntime) else service.snapshot()

    def authorized(headers):
        if token is None:
            return True
        supplied = headers.get("authorization", "")
        if supplied.startswith("Bearer "):
            return hmac.compare_digest(supplied[7:], token)
        protocols = [p.strip() for p in headers.get("sec-websocket-protocol", "").split(",")]
        return any(p.startswith("bearer.") and hmac.compare_digest(p[7:], token) for p in protocols)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app):
        if manage_lifecycle:
            service.start()
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            if manage_lifecycle:
                service.stop()

    app = FastAPI(title="GazeSpace local telemetry", version="0.2.1", lifespan=lifespan,
                  docs_url=None, redoc_url=None, openapi_url=None)

    @app.middleware("http")
    async def protect(request: Request, call_next):
        if not _loopback_host(request.headers.get("host", "")) or not _origin_allowed(request.headers.get("origin")):
            return JSONResponse({"detail": "Local host and local origin required"}, status_code=403)
        if not authorized(request.headers):
            return JSONResponse({"detail": "Bearer token required"}, status_code=401,
                                headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"})
        response = await call_next(request)
        response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
        response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
        return response

    @app.get("/health")
    def health():
        value = snapshot()
        return {"ok": value["running"] and not value["last_error"],
                "state": value.get("state"), "error": value["last_error"]}

    @app.get("/v1/gaze")
    def gaze():
        return snapshot()

    @app.get("/v1/metrics")
    def metrics():
        return snapshot().get("metrics", {})

    # FastAPI resolves annotations through function globals; explicit annotation
    # avoids a function-local WebSocket forward-reference becoming a query arg.
    async def stream(ws):
        import asyncio
        if not _loopback_host(ws.headers.get("host", "")) or not _origin_allowed(ws.headers.get("origin")):
            await ws.close(code=1008)
            return
        if not authorized(ws.headers):
            await ws.close(code=1008)
            return
        protocols = [p.strip() for p in ws.headers.get("sec-websocket-protocol", "").split(",")]
        await ws.accept(subprotocol="gazespace" if "gazespace" in protocols else None)
        try:
            while True:
                await ws.send_json(snapshot())
                await asyncio.sleep(.05)
        except (WebSocketDisconnect, RuntimeError, OSError):
            return
    stream.__annotations__["ws"] = WebSocket
    app.websocket("/v1/stream")(stream)
    return app

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Local, opt-in gaze recordings, strict replay and portable CSV export.

Raw camera video is written only when ``record_video=True`` is explicitly
selected. Session timestamps preserve source timestamps and also add an
independent monotonic elapsed time for replay across camera reconnects.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from bisect import bisect_right
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, is_dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from threading import RLock
from typing import Callable
import csv
import json
import math
import os
import uuid
import numpy as np

from .clock import HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, host_now_s

SESSION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2


def _json_value(value):
    if is_dataclass(value):
        return _json_value(asdict(value))
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return _json_value(value.tolist())
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return _json_value(value.item())
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(k): _json_value(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_json_value(v) for v in value]
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, str, int)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, float):
        if not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError("session values must be finite; NaN/Infinity cannot be saved")
        return value
    raise TypeError(f"unsupported session value {type(value).__name__}")


def _utc_now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def _atomic_json(path: Path, content: dict):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    try:
        with tmp.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            json.dump(_json_value(content), handle, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        os.replace(tmp, path)
    finally:
        tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)


def _validate_record(record: dict) -> dict:
    data = _json_value(record)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ValueError("gaze record must be a JSON object")
    for key in ("timestamp_s", "session_time_s"):
        if key in data and (isinstance(data[key], bool) or not isinstance(data[key], (int, float))):
            raise ValueError(f"{key} must be numeric")
    if "timestamp_s" not in data:
        raise ValueError("gaze record is missing its source timestamp")
    if "valid" not in data or not isinstance(data["valid"], bool):
        raise ValueError("gaze record requires a boolean valid flag")
    for key, length in (("gaze_screen_px", 2), ("gaze_screen_normalized", 2),
                        ("raw_gaze_screen_px", 2), ("surface_xy_m", 2),
                        ("surface_xy_px", 2), ("ray_origin_world_m", 3),
                        ("ray_direction_world", 3)):
        item = data.get(key)
        if item is not None:
            if not isinstance(item, list) or len(item) != length:
                raise ValueError(f"invalid {key} shape")
            if any(isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float)) for v in item):
                raise ValueError(f"invalid {key} coordinate")
    return data


class SessionRecorder:
    """Create one unique local session with bounded-memory append operations.

    The caller owns consent/UI and chooses when recording begins. Metadata may
    include screen_size, subject_id, profile_fingerprint, camera and provenance
    (``synthetic``, ``live`` or ``video``). No model guesses physical accuracy.
    """

    def __init__(self, root: str | Path, metadata: dict | None = None, *,
                 record_gaze: bool = True, record_video: bool = False,
                 fps: float = 30.0, clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None):
        if not record_gaze and not record_video:
            raise ValueError("select gaze metadata, camera video, or both for recording")
        if not math.isfinite(fps) or not 1 <= fps <= 240:
            raise ValueError("video FPS must lie within [1,240]")
        self._clock = host_now_s if clock is None else clock
        self._start = float(self._clock())
        if not math.isfinite(self._start):
            raise ValueError("recording clock must be finite")
        self._lock = RLock()
        self._last_elapsed = 0.0
        self._closed = False
        self._video = None
        self._video_size = None
        self._video_count = 0
        self._count = 0
        self._valid = 0
        self._record_gaze = bool(record_gaze)
        self._record_video = bool(record_video)
        self._fps = float(fps)
        self._metadata = _json_value(metadata or {})
        self.session_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        name = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S") + "_" + self.session_id[:10]
        self.path = Path(root).expanduser().resolve() / name
        self.path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        self.manifest = {
            "schema_version": SESSION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            "session_time_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN if clock is None else "custom_monotonic_relative",
            "session_id": self.session_id,
            "created_utc": _utc_now(), "closed_utc": None,
            "status": "recording", "metadata": self._metadata,
            "provenance": self._metadata.get("provenance", "unclassified"),
            "physical_accuracy_verified": False,
            "record_gaze": self._record_gaze, "record_video": self._record_video,
            "records_file": "gaze.jsonl" if record_gaze else None,
            "events_file": "events.jsonl", "video_file": None,
            "video_fps": self._fps if record_video else None,
            "video_timing": "constant_fps_review_only; use gaze session_time_s for measurements" if record_video else None,
            "records": 0, "valid_records": 0, "video_frames": 0,
            "duration_s": 0.0,
            "privacy": "local_only; video opt-in; no network transfer",
        }
        self._gaze = None
        self._events = None
        try:
            if record_gaze:
                self._gaze = (self.path / "gaze.jsonl").open("x", encoding="utf-8")
            self._events = (self.path / "events.jsonl").open("x", encoding="utf-8")
            _atomic_json(self.path / "manifest.json", self.manifest)
            self.event("recording_started", {"record_gaze": record_gaze, "record_video": record_video})
        except Exception:
            if self._gaze:
                self._gaze.close()
            if self._events:
                self._events.close()
            raise

    @property
    def closed(self):
        return self._closed

    def _elapsed(self):
        value = float(self._clock()) - self._start
        if not math.isfinite(value) or value < self._last_elapsed - 1e-6:
            raise ValueError("recording clock moved backwards or became invalid")
        self._last_elapsed = max(value, self._last_elapsed, 0.0)
        return self._last_elapsed

    def append(self, result, image_bgr: np.ndarray | None = None) -> dict:
        data = result.as_dict() if hasattr(result, "as_dict") else result
        row = _validate_record(data)
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                raise RuntimeError("session is already closed")
            row["session_time_s"] = self._elapsed()
            row["record_sequence"] = self._count
            if self._record_video and image_bgr is not None:
                import cv2
                image = np.asarray(image_bgr)
                if image.ndim != 3 or image.shape[2] != 3 or image.dtype != np.uint8:
                    raise ValueError("recorded video needs BGR uint8 HxWx3 frames")
                size = (int(image.shape[1]), int(image.shape[0]))
                if min(size) < 2:
                    raise ValueError("video frame dimensions are too small")
                if self._video is None:
                    self._video = cv2.VideoWriter(str(self.path / "camera.avi"),
                                                 cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*"MJPG"), self._fps, size)
                    if not self._video.isOpened():
                        self._video.release()
                        self._video = None
                        raise OSError("MJPG encoder unavailable; disable optional video recording")
                    self._video_size = size
                    self.manifest["video_file"] = "camera.avi"
                    self.manifest["video_size"] = list(size)
                    _atomic_json(self.path / "manifest.json", self.manifest)
                if size != self._video_size:
                    raise ValueError("camera resolution changed during recording; start a new session")
                self._video.write(np.ascontiguousarray(image))
                row["video_frame"] = self._video_count
                self._video_count += 1
            elif self._record_video:
                row["video_frame"] = None
            if self._gaze is not None:
                self._gaze.write(json.dumps(row, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
                self._gaze.flush()
            self._count += 1
            self._valid += int(row["valid"])
            return deepcopy(row)

    def event(self, name: str, details: dict | None = None):
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                raise RuntimeError("session is already closed")
            row = {"event": str(name), "session_time_s": self._elapsed(),
                   "details": _json_value(details or {})}
            self._events.write(json.dumps(row, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
            self._events.flush()

    def close(self, error: str | None = None) -> Path:
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                return self.path
            try:
                self.event("recording_stopped", {"error": error})
                for handle in (self._gaze, self._events):
                    if handle:
                        handle.flush()
                        os.fsync(handle.fileno())
                        handle.close()
                if self._video is not None:
                    self._video.release()
                self.manifest.update({"status": "failed" if error else "completed",
                                      "closed_utc": _utc_now(), "error": error,
                                      "records": self._count if self._record_gaze else 0,
                                      "observations_seen": self._count,
                                      "valid_records": self._valid if self._record_gaze else 0,
                                      "video_frames": self._video_count,
                                      "duration_s": self._last_elapsed})
                _atomic_json(self.path / "manifest.json", self.manifest)
            finally:
                self._closed = True
                for handle in (self._gaze, self._events):
                    if handle and not handle.closed:
                        handle.close()
                if self._video is not None:
                    self._video.release()
            return self.path

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        self.close(str(exc) if exc else None)


@dataclass
class SessionData:
    path: Path
    manifest: dict
    records: list[dict]
    events: list[dict]


def _read_json(path: Path):
    def invalid(text):
        raise ValueError(f"nonfinite JSON number {text}")
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), parse_constant=invalid)


def _safe_child(root: Path, name: str):
    result = (root / name).resolve()
    if result.parent != root.resolve():
        raise ValueError("session manifest may only reference local immediate files")
    return result


def load_session(path: str | Path, *, strict: bool = True) -> SessionData:
    root = Path(path).expanduser().resolve()
    if root.name == "manifest.json":
        root = root.parent
    manifest = _read_json(root / "manifest.json")
    if manifest.get("schema_version") != SESSION_SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("unsupported session schema")
    records, events = [], []
    filename = manifest.get("records_file")
    if filename:
        file = _safe_child(root, filename)
        with file.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            previous = -math.inf
            for line_no, line in enumerate(handle, 1):
                if not line.strip():
                    continue
                try:
                    record = _validate_record(json.loads(line))
                    elapsed = record.get("session_time_s")
                    if elapsed is None or elapsed < 0 or elapsed < previous:
                        raise ValueError("session replay timestamps must be nonnegative and monotonic")
                    previous = elapsed
                    records.append(record)
                except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
                    if strict:
                        raise ValueError(f"invalid gaze.jsonl line {line_no}: {exc}") from exc
    event_name = manifest.get("events_file")
    if event_name:
        event_path = _safe_child(root, event_name)
        if event_path.exists():
            with event_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                for line_no, line in enumerate(handle, 1):
                    if line.strip():
                        event = _json_value(json.loads(line))
                        if not isinstance(event, dict):
                            raise ValueError(f"invalid event line {line_no}")
                        events.append(event)
    if strict and manifest.get("status") == "completed" and len(records) != manifest.get("records", 0):
        raise ValueError("session record count differs from its finalized manifest")
    return SessionData(root, manifest, records, events)


def list_sessions(root: str | Path, limit: int = 200) -> list[dict]:
    if limit < 1:
        return []
    parent = Path(root).expanduser()
    if not parent.exists():
        return []
    found = []
    for path in parent.glob("*/manifest.json"):
        try:
            manifest = _read_json(path)
            if manifest.get("schema_version") == SESSION_SCHEMA_VERSION:
                found.append({**manifest, "path": str(path.parent.resolve())})
        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
            continue
    return sorted(found, key=lambda row: row.get("created_utc", ""), reverse=True)[:limit]


CSV_COLUMNS = ("session_time_s", "timestamp_s", "record_sequence", "valid", "screen_valid", "spatial_valid",
               "reason", "quality", "source", "gaze_x_px", "gaze_y_px", "raw_gaze_x_px", "raw_gaze_y_px",
               "surface_name", "surface_x_m", "surface_y_m", "surface_x_px", "surface_y_px",
               "origin_x_m", "origin_y_m", "origin_z_m", "direction_x", "direction_y", "direction_z",
               "processing_ms", "eye_origin_source", "model_fingerprint", "warnings")


def export_csv(session_or_path: SessionData | str | Path, output: str | Path) -> Path:
    data = session_or_path if isinstance(session_or_path, SessionData) else load_session(session_or_path)
    out = Path(output)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, CSV_COLUMNS, extrasaction="ignore")
        writer.writeheader()
        for record in data.records:
            row = dict(record)
            for field, columns in (("gaze_screen_px", ("gaze_x_px", "gaze_y_px")),
                                   ("raw_gaze_screen_px", ("raw_gaze_x_px", "raw_gaze_y_px")),
                                   ("surface_xy_m", ("surface_x_m", "surface_y_m")),
                                   ("surface_xy_px", ("surface_x_px", "surface_y_px")),
                                   ("ray_origin_world_m", ("origin_x_m", "origin_y_m", "origin_z_m")),
                                   ("ray_direction_world", ("direction_x", "direction_y", "direction_z"))):
                row.update(zip(columns, record.get(field) or [None] * len(columns)))
            row["warnings"] = ";".join(str(x) for x in record.get("warnings", []))
            # Spreadsheet tools can treat leading strings as formulas. Prefix
            # only text values; do not modify legitimate numeric coordinates.
            for key, value in list(row.items()):
                if isinstance(value, str) and value[:1] in ("=", "+", "-", "@"):
                    row[key] = "'" + value
            writer.writerow(row)
    return out


class SessionReplay:
    """Deterministic metadata replay. No output is sent to input devices.

    Recorded ``session_time_s`` is already relative to recording start. Preserve
    its initial gap so slider positions, exported times and video references
    share that timeline. Only legacy source-only rows are rebased to zero.
    """

    def __init__(self, records_or_session, *, max_gap_s: float = 0.25):
        if not math.isfinite(max_gap_s) or max_gap_s <= 0:
            raise ValueError("replay stale-gap threshold must be finite and positive")
        self.max_gap_s = float(max_gap_s)
        if isinstance(records_or_session, SessionData):
            records = records_or_session.records
        elif isinstance(records_or_session, (str, Path)):
            records = load_session(records_or_session).records
        else:
            records = records_or_session
        self.records = [_validate_record(r) for r in records]
        if self.records:
            has_session_time = ["session_time_s" in r for r in self.records]
            if any(has_session_time) and not all(has_session_time):
                raise ValueError("replay records cannot mix session and source timestamp timelines")
            explicit_session_time = all(has_session_time)
            key = "session_time_s" if explicit_session_time else "timestamp_s"
            raw = [float(r[key]) for r in self.records]
            if explicit_session_time and any(t < 0 for t in raw):
                raise ValueError("session replay timestamps must be nonnegative")
            if any(b < a for a, b in zip(raw, raw[1:])):
                raise ValueError("replay records must be ordered by timestamp")
            self._times = raw if explicit_session_time else [t - raw[0] for t in raw]
        else:
            self._times = []
        self.duration_s = self._times[-1] if self._times else 0.0
        self.position_s = 0.0
        self.index = bisect_right(self._times, self.position_s) - 1

    @property
    def finished(self) -> bool:
        return not self.records or self.position_s >= self.duration_s

    def current(self) -> dict | None:
        if self.index < 0:
            return None
        result = deepcopy(self.records[self.index])
        result["replay"] = True
        result["replay_position_s"] = self.position_s
        if self.position_s - self._times[self.index] > self.max_gap_s:
            result["recorded_valid"] = result["valid"]
            result["valid"] = False
            result["screen_valid"] = result["spatial_valid"] = False
            result["reason"] = "replay_gap_no_fresh_sample"
            result["stale"] = True
            for key in ("gaze_screen_px", "gaze_screen_normalized", "raw_gaze_screen_px",
                        "surface_name", "surface_xy_m", "surface_xy_px",
                        "ray_origin_world_m", "ray_direction_world"):
                if key in result:
                    result[key] = None
        return result

    def seek(self, seconds: float) -> dict | None:
        if not math.isfinite(seconds):
            raise ValueError("seek position must be finite")
        self.position_s = max(0.0, min(float(seconds), self.duration_s))
        self.index = bisect_right(self._times, self.position_s) - 1
        return self.current()

    def advance(self, delta_s: float) -> dict | None:
        if not math.isfinite(delta_s) or delta_s < 0:
            raise ValueError("replay step must be finite and nonnegative")
        return self.seek(self.position_s + delta_s)

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Measured stereo triangulation and bounded frame timestamp pairing.

Software timestamp pairing is explicitly different from hardware exposure
synchronization. A stereo point can provide a measured eye surface position;
it does not identify a user's fixation direction without gaze calibration.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math

import cv2
import numpy as np

from .types import CameraIntrinsics, Frame


@dataclass
class PairedFrames:
    left: Frame
    right: Frame
    skew_s: float
    timestamp_basis: str


class FrameSynchronizer:
    def __init__(self, max_skew_s: float = .008, max_queue: int = 6,
                 use_hardware_clock: bool = False):
        if max_skew_s <= 0 or max_queue < 1:
            raise ValueError("Positive skew limit and queue bound required")
        self.max_skew_s, self.max_queue = float(max_skew_s), int(max_queue)
        self.use_hardware_clock = bool(use_hardware_clock)
        self.queues = {"left": deque(), "right": deque()}
        self.paired = self.dropped = 0

    def _stamp(self, frame):
        if self.use_hardware_clock:
            if not frame.metadata.get("hardware_clock_synchronized") or frame.hardware_timestamp_s is None:
                raise ValueError("Hardware clocks must be explicitly synchronized before comparing them")
            stamp = frame.hardware_timestamp_s
        else:
            stamp = frame.timestamp_s
        if not math.isfinite(stamp):
            raise ValueError("Nonfinite frame timestamp")
        return stamp

    def add(self, side: str, frame: Frame) -> PairedFrames | None:
        if side not in self.queues:
            raise ValueError("side must be left or right")
        stamp = self._stamp(frame)
        queue = self.queues[side]
        if queue and stamp <= self._stamp(queue[-1]):
            raise ValueError("Timestamps must increase on each camera")
        queue.append(frame)
        if len(queue) > self.max_queue:
            queue.popleft()
            self.dropped += 1
        left, right = self.queues["left"], self.queues["right"]
        while left and right:
            lt, rt = self._stamp(left[0]), self._stamp(right[0])
            delta = abs(lt - rt)
            if delta <= self.max_skew_s:
                lframe, rframe = left.popleft(), right.popleft()
                self.paired += 1
                return PairedFrames(lframe, rframe, delta,
                    "synchronized_hardware_clock" if self.use_hardware_clock else "host_timestamps_only")
            (left if lt < rt else right).popleft()
            self.dropped += 1
        return None


@dataclass
class TriangulationResult:
    points_camera_m: np.ndarray  # NaN rows are invalid and must not be used.
    valid_mask: np.ndarray
    reprojection_px: np.ndarray
    convergence_deg: np.ndarray

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"points_camera_m": [row.tolist() if valid else None
                                     for row, valid in zip(self.points_camera_m, self.valid_mask)],
                "valid_mask": self.valid_mask.tolist(),
                "reprojection_px": [float(x) if np.isfinite(x) else None for x in self.reprojection_px],
                "convergence_deg": [float(x) if np.isfinite(x) else None for x in self.convergence_deg]}


def triangulate_points_checked(left_xy: np.ndarray, right_xy: np.ndarray,
                               left_intr: CameraIntrinsics, right_intr: CameraIntrinsics,
                               rotation_right_from_left: np.ndarray,
                               translation_right_from_left_m: np.ndarray, *,
                               max_reprojection_px: float = 2.0,
                               min_convergence_deg: float = .1,
                               max_depth_m: float = 10.0) -> TriangulationResult:
    if left_intr.source not in ("measured", "factory") or right_intr.source not in ("measured", "factory"):
        raise ValueError("Stereo triangulation requires measured intrinsics")
    left, right = np.asarray(left_xy, float), np.asarray(right_xy, float)
    if left.ndim != 2 or left.shape[1] != 2 or right.shape != left.shape or len(left) == 0:
        raise ValueError("Matching nonempty Nx2 pixel coordinates required")
    if not np.isfinite(left).all() or not np.isfinite(right).all():
        raise ValueError("Pixel coordinates must be finite")
    R = np.asarray(rotation_right_from_left, float)
    T = np.asarray(translation_right_from_left_m, float).reshape(3)
    if R.shape != (3, 3) or not np.isfinite(R).all() or not np.isfinite(T).all():
        raise ValueError("Invalid stereo transform")
    if not np.allclose(R.T @ R, np.eye(3), atol=1e-6) or not np.isclose(np.linalg.det(R), 1, atol=1e-6):
        raise ValueError("Stereo rotation must be proper orthonormal")
    if np.linalg.norm(T) < .001:
        raise ValueError("Stereo baseline is too small for metric triangulation")
    if max_reprojection_px <= 0 or min_convergence_deg < 0 or max_depth_m <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Invalid triangulation limits")
    for xy, intr in ((left, left_intr), (right, right_intr)):
        if np.any(xy < 0) or np.any(xy[:, 0] >= intr.width) or np.any(xy[:, 1] >= intr.height):
            raise ValueError("Pixel coordinates outside calibrated images")
    lnorm = cv2.undistortPoints(left.reshape(-1, 1, 2), left_intr.camera_matrix,
                                left_intr.distortion).reshape(-1, 2)
    rnorm = cv2.undistortPoints(right.reshape(-1, 1, 2), right_intr.camera_matrix,
                                right_intr.distortion).reshape(-1, 2)
    P1 = np.column_stack((np.eye(3), np.zeros(3)))
    P2 = np.column_stack((R, T))
    homogeneous = cv2.triangulatePoints(P1, P2, lnorm.T, rnorm.T).T
    w = homogeneous[:, 3]
    candidate = np.full((len(left), 3), np.nan)
    finite = np.abs(w) > 1e-10
    candidate[finite] = homogeneous[finite, :3] / w[finite, None]
    errors, convergence = np.full(len(left), np.inf), np.zeros(len(left))
    valid = finite & np.isfinite(candidate).all(axis=1)
    for i in np.flatnonzero(valid):
        point = candidate[i]
        right_point = R @ point + T
        if not (.01 < point[2] < max_depth_m and .01 < right_point[2] < max_depth_m):
            valid[i] = False
            continue
        projected_l, _ = cv2.projectPoints(point.reshape(1, 3), np.zeros(3), np.zeros(3),
                                           left_intr.camera_matrix, left_intr.distortion)
        rvec, _ = cv2.Rodrigues(R)
        projected_r, _ = cv2.projectPoints(point.reshape(1, 3), rvec, T,
                                           right_intr.camera_matrix, right_intr.distortion)
        errors[i] = max(np.linalg.norm(projected_l.reshape(2) - left[i]),
                        np.linalg.norm(projected_r.reshape(2) - right[i]))
        camera_right_in_left = -R.T @ T
        d1, d2 = point, point - camera_right_in_left
        cosang = np.dot(d1, d2) / (np.linalg.norm(d1) * np.linalg.norm(d2))
        convergence[i] = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip(cosang, -1, 1)))
        valid[i] = errors[i] <= max_reprojection_px and convergence[i] >= min_convergence_deg
    candidate[~valid] = np.nan
    return TriangulationResult(candidate, valid, errors, convergence)

"""Typed, framework-independent API data models."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from typing import Any
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class Frame:
    """Owned image with separate source, host-receive and device timestamps.

    ``timestamp_s`` drives filtering in the source timeline (video PTS may be
    different from arrival time). ``received_monotonic_s`` uses
    ``gazespace.clock.host_now_s()`` / ``time.perf_counter()`` for freshness;
    do not supply ``time.monotonic()`` on Windows Python 3.11/3.12, wall time,
    or a device clock. ``hardware_timestamp_s`` remains in the device domain.
    """
    image: np.ndarray
    timestamp_s: float
    sequence: int = 0
    source: str = ""
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    received_monotonic_s: float | None = None
    hardware_timestamp_s: float | None = None
    depth_m: np.ndarray | None = None

@dataclass
class HeadPose:
    rotation: np.ndarray  # 3x3, head model -> camera
    translation_m: np.ndarray  # 3, from camera origin in meters; approximate face geometry
    yaw_deg: float
    pitch_deg: float
    roll_deg: float
    reprojection_px: float

@dataclass
class Observation:
    timestamp_s: float
    image_size: tuple[int, int]
    landmarks_px: dict[int, tuple[float, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    pupils_px: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    pupil_method: str = ""
    face_detected: bool = False
    eyes_open: bool = False
    blink: bool = False
    quality: float = 0.0
    features: np.ndarray | None = None
    head_pose: HeadPose | None = None
    eye_origin_camera_m: np.ndarray | None = None
    message: str = "no_face"
    context: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    quality_metrics: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    eye_origin_source: str = "unavailable"

@dataclass
class GazeResult:
    timestamp_s: float
    valid: bool
    reason: str
    source: str
    quality: float
    gaze_screen_px: tuple[float, float] | None = None
    gaze_screen_normalized: tuple[float, float] | None = None
    ray_origin_world_m: tuple[float, float, float] | None = None
    ray_direction_world: tuple[float, float, float] | None = None
    surface_name: str | None = None
    surface_xy_m: tuple[float, float] | None = None
    surface_xy_px: tuple[float, float] | None = None
    yaw_pitch_roll_deg: tuple[float, float, float] | None = None
    pupils_px: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    processing_ms: float = 0.0
    capture_timestamp_s: float = 0.0
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    raw_gaze_screen_px: tuple[float, float] | None = None
    screen_valid: bool = False
    spatial_valid: bool = False
    eye_origin_source: str = "unavailable"
    model_fingerprint: str | None = None
    tracking_context: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

@dataclass
class CameraIntrinsics:
    width: int
    height: int
    camera_matrix: np.ndarray
    distortion: np.ndarray
    reprojection_rms_px: float | None = None
    images_used: int = 0
    source: str = "measured"  # approximate is forbidden for physical calibration

    def validate(self) -> None:
        matrix = np.asarray(self.camera_matrix, dtype=float)
        distortion = np.asarray(self.distortion, dtype=float)
        if self.width < 2 or self.height < 2 or matrix.shape != (3, 3):
            raise ValueError("invalid intrinsic dimensions or camera matrix")
        if not np.isfinite(matrix).all() or not np.isfinite(distortion).all():
            raise ValueError("nonfinite camera intrinsics")
        if matrix[0, 0] <= 0 or matrix[1, 1] <= 0 or not np.allclose(matrix[2], (0, 0, 1)):
            raise ValueError("invalid focal length or homogeneous camera matrix")
        if distortion.size not in (0, 4, 5, 8, 12, 14):
            raise ValueError("unsupported distortion coefficient count")
        if self.source not in ("measured", "approximate", "factory"):
            raise ValueError("unknown intrinsic source")
        if self.reprojection_rms_px is not None and (
            not np.isfinite(self.reprojection_rms_px) or self.reprojection_rms_px < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid intrinsic reprojection RMS")

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"width": self.width, "height": self.height,
                "camera_matrix": self.camera_matrix.tolist(),
                "distortion": self.distortion.ravel().tolist(),
                "reprojection_rms_px": self.reprojection_rms_px,
                "images_used": self.images_used, "source": self.source}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "CameraIntrinsics":
        result = cls(int(d["width"]), int(d["height"]),
                   np.asarray(d["camera_matrix"], dtype=float).reshape(3, 3),
                   np.asarray(d["distortion"], dtype=float).reshape(-1, 1),
                   d.get("reprojection_rms_px"), int(d.get("images_used", 0)),
                   d.get("source", "measured"))
        result.validate()
        return result

@dataclass
class CameraExtrinsics:
    rotation_world_to_camera: np.ndarray
    translation_world_to_camera_m: np.ndarray
    reprojection_rms_px: float | None = None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        rotation = np.asarray(self.rotation_world_to_camera, dtype=float)
        translation = np.asarray(self.translation_world_to_camera_m, dtype=float)
        if rotation.shape != (3, 3) or translation.shape != (3,):
            raise ValueError("invalid extrinsic matrix dimensions")
        if not np.isfinite(rotation).all() or not np.isfinite(translation).all():
            raise ValueError("nonfinite camera extrinsics")
        if not np.allclose(rotation.T @ rotation, np.eye(3), atol=1e-5) or not np.isclose(np.linalg.det(rotation), 1, atol=1e-5):
            raise ValueError("camera rotation must be a proper orthonormal rotation")

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"rotation_world_to_camera": self.rotation_world_to_camera.tolist(),
                "translation_world_to_camera_m": self.translation_world_to_camera_m.tolist(),
                "reprojection_rms_px": self.reprojection_rms_px}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "CameraExtrinsics":
        result = cls(np.asarray(d["rotation_world_to_camera"], dtype=float).reshape(3, 3),
                   np.asarray(d["translation_world_to_camera_m"], dtype=float).reshape(3),
                   d.get("reprojection_rms_px"))
        result.validate()
        return result

@dataclass
class Surface:
    name: str
    origin_world_m: tuple[float, float, float]  # top-left origin
    u_vector: tuple[float, float, float]  # world vector pointing right
    v_vector: tuple[float, float, float]  # world vector pointing down
    width_m: float
    height_m: float
    pixel_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None
    two_sided: bool = True

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.name, str) or not self.name.strip():
            raise ValueError("surface name cannot be empty")
        vectors = np.asarray([self.origin_world_m, self.u_vector, self.v_vector], dtype=float)
        if vectors.shape != (3, 3) or not np.isfinite(vectors).all():
            raise ValueError("surface points and axes must be finite three-vectors")
        u, v = vectors[1:]
        if min(np.linalg.norm(u), np.linalg.norm(v)) < 1e-9:
            raise ValueError("invalid surface axes")
        cosine = float(u @ v / (np.linalg.norm(u) * np.linalg.norm(v)))
        if abs(cosine) > 1e-5:
            raise ValueError("surface axes must be orthogonal, not parallel or skewed")
        if not np.isfinite([self.width_m, self.height_m]).all() or min(self.width_m, self.height_m) <= 0:
            raise ValueError("surface width and height must be finite and positive")
        if self.pixel_size is not None and (len(self.pixel_size) != 2 or any(int(v) != v or v < 2 for v in self.pixel_size)):
            raise ValueError("surface pixel dimensions must be integer values >= 2")

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "Surface":
        result = cls(d["name"], tuple(d["origin_world_m"]), tuple(d["u_vector"]),
                   tuple(d["v_vector"]), float(d["width_m"]), float(d["height_m"]),
                   tuple(d["pixel_size"]) if d.get("pixel_size") else None,
                   bool(d.get("two_sided", True)))
        result.validate()
        return result

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/types.py =====

"""Guided acquisition dialogs share the same temporal collector as the SDK."""
from __future__ import annotations
import uuid
import numpy as np
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, Signal, QRectF, QPointF
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QPen, QColor, QFont, QKeyEvent

class TargetSessionDialog(QWidget):
    samples_ready = Signal(object)
    cancelled = Signal()

    def __init__(self, snapshot, targets, *, purpose='calibration', screen=None,
                 screen_size=None, collector_options=None, parent=None):
        configure_app_fonts()
        super().__init__(parent, Qt.WindowType.Window)
        self.setWindowTitle('GazeSpace · ' + purpose)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_DeleteOnClose, True)
        self.snapshot = snapshot
        self.targets = targets
        self.purpose = purpose
        self.screen = screen
        self.screen_size = screen_size
        self.options = collector_options or {}
        self.index = 0
        self.collector = None
        self.records = []
        self.context = {}
        self.session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.status_text = 'Look at the target, then press SPACE to collect'
        self.started = False
        self._finished = False
        self._active = False
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.tick)
        self.timer.start(20)
        if screen is not None:
            self.setGeometry(screen.geometry())
        self.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.StrongFocus)
        self.resize(1280,800)

    def begin_fullscreen(self):
        if self.screen is not None:
            self.setGeometry(self.screen.geometry())
        self.show()
        if self.screen is not None and self.windowHandle() is not None:
            self.windowHandle().setScreen(self.screen)
        self.showFullScreen()
        self.activateWindow()
        self.setFocus()
        if not self.screen_size:
            dpr = self.devicePixelRatioF()
            self.screen_size = (round(self.width()*dpr),round(self.height()*dpr))

    def collect(self):
        if self._active or self.index >= len(self.targets):
            return
        tid, xy = self.targets[self.index]
        self.collector = TargetCollector(tid, xy, kind='screen', **self.options)
        self.collector.start()
        self.wall_started=host_now_s()
        self._active = True
        self.status_text = 'Settling on target… keep your eyes comfortably open'
        self.update()

    def tick(self):
        if self._finished or not self._active:
            return
        if host_now_s()-self.wall_started>self.collector.max_seconds:
            self._active=False;self.status_text='No stable observations in time. Press SPACE to retry, or ESC to cancel.';self.update();return
        state = self.snapshot()
        ob = state.get('observation')
        if state.get('state') != 'running' or ob is None:
            self.status_text = 'Waiting for a current camera observation'
            self.update()
            return
        self.context = dict(getattr(ob, 'context', {}) or self.context)
        self.collector.add(ob)
        progress = self.collector.progress()
        self.status_text = (f"{progress['accepted']}/{progress['required']} accepted · "
                            f"{progress['stable_duration_s']:.1f}s stable · {progress['reason']}")
        if self.collector.complete:
            self.records.extend(self.collector.records(screen_size=self.screen_size))
            self.index += 1
            self._active = False
            self.status_text = ('Take a break or move naturally, then fixate the center and press SPACE'
                                if self.purpose == 'drift' else 'Next target ready · look at the dot and press SPACE')
            if self.index >= len(self.targets):
                self._finished = True
                self.timer.stop()
                payload = {'records':self.records, 'context':self.context,
                           'session_id':self.session_id, 'screen_size':self.screen_size,
                           'purpose':self.purpose, 'monitor':self.screen.name() if self.screen else ''}
                self.samples_ready.emit(payload)
                self.close()
                return
        elif progress['state'] in ('failed','timeout','timed_out','incompatible'):
            self._active = False
            self.status_text = 'Collection timed out. Improve tracking, then press SPACE to retry this target.'
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        p = QPainter(self)
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        p.fillRect(self.rect(),QColor('#091221'))
        p.setPen(QColor(TEAL));p.setFont(QFont(FONT_FAMILY,13,QFont.Weight.DemiBold))
        p.drawText(QRectF(30,24,self.width()-60,25),Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,'GAZESPACE  /  '+self.purpose.upper())
        p.setPen(QColor(TEXT));p.setFont(QFont(FONT_FAMILY,19,QFont.Weight.DemiBold))
        p.drawText(QRectF(30,56,self.width()-60,35),Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                   f'Target {min(self.index+1,len(self.targets))} of {len(self.targets)}')
        p.setPen(QColor(MUTED));p.setFont(QFont(FONT_FAMILY,11))
        p.drawText(QRectF(30,self.height()-80,self.width()-60,28),Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,self.status_text)
        p.drawText(QRectF(30,self.height()-45,self.width()-60,24),Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                   'SPACE  collect / retry     ·     ESC  cancel without changing calibration')
        if self.index < len(self.targets):
            _,(x,y) = self.targets[self.index]
            size=self.screen_size or (round(self.width()*self.devicePixelRatioF()),round(self.height()*self.devicePixelRatioF()))
            point = QPointF(x*(size[0]-1)/self.devicePixelRatioF(),y*(size[1]-1)/self.devicePixelRatioF())
            p.setPen(QPen(QColor('#315773'),2));p.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
            p.drawEllipse(point,29,29)
            p.setPen(QPen(QColor(TEAL),3));p.drawEllipse(point,11,11)
            p.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen);p.setBrush(QColor('#ffffff'));p.drawEllipse(point,3,3)
        if self.collector and self._active:
            fraction = self.collector.progress()['fraction']
            p.fillRect(QRectF(0,self.height()-5,self.width()*fraction,5),QColor(TEAL))

    def keyPressEvent(self,event):
        if event.key() in (Qt.Key.Key_Space,Qt.Key.Key_Return):
            self.collect()
        elif event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Escape:
            self.close()
        else:
            super().keyPressEvent(event)

    def closeEvent(self,event):
        self.timer.stop()
        if not self._finished:
            self.cancelled.emit()
        super().closeEvent(event)


class SpatialSessionDialog(QDialog):
    samples_ready = Signal(object)

    def __init__(self, snapshot, targets, extrinsics, *, purpose='spatial calibration', parent=None):
        configure_app_fonts()
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle('GazeSpace · ' + purpose)
        self.resize(820,650)
        self.snapshot, self.targets, self.extrinsics = snapshot, targets, extrinsics
        self.purpose = purpose
        self.records = []
        self.index = 0
        self.collector = None
        self.context = {}
        self.session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.heading = label('Physical target acquisition','title');layout.addWidget(self.heading)
        self.instructions = label('',wrap=True);layout.addWidget(self.instructions)
        self.video = VideoCanvas();layout.addWidget(self.video,1)
        self.progressbar = QProgressBar();layout.addWidget(self.progressbar)
        self.status = label('Fixate the physical marker at its measured coordinates, then collect.','muted',True)
        layout.addWidget(self.status)
        buttons = QHBoxLayout()
        self.button = QPushButton('Collect current target');self.button.setObjectName('primary')
        self.button.clicked.connect(self.collect)
        cancel = QPushButton('Cancel');cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        buttons.addWidget(self.button);buttons.addStretch();buttons.addWidget(cancel)
        layout.addLayout(buttons)
        self.timer = QTimer(self);self.timer.timeout.connect(self.tick);self.timer.start(30)
        self.finished.connect(lambda _: self.timer.stop())
        self.refresh_target()

    def refresh_target(self):
        t = self.targets[self.index]
        self.instructions.setText(f"Target {self.index+1}/{len(self.targets)}: {t['id']}\n"
                                  f"World coordinate (meters): {t['xyz_m']} · keep natural head posture")
        self.button.setEnabled(True)
        self.progressbar.setValue(0)

    def collect(self):
        target = self.targets[self.index]
        self.collector = TargetCollector(str(target['id']),target['xyz_m'],kind='spatial')
        self.collector.start()
        self.wall_started = host_now_s()
        self.button.setEnabled(False)

    def tick(self):
        if self.collector and host_now_s()-self.wall_started > self.collector.max_seconds:
            self.collector = None
            self.button.setEnabled(True)
            self.status.setText('No stable observations in time. Restore the camera and retry this target.')
            return
        state = self.snapshot()
        frame = state.get('frame')
        if frame is not None:
            self.video.live_state = state.get('state', 'idle')
            self.video.set_frame(frame.image,state.get('latest'),state.get('observation'))
        ob = state.get('observation')
        if not self.collector or state.get('state') != 'running' or ob is None:
            return
        if getattr(ob,'eye_origin_camera_m',None) is None:
            self.status.setText('A measured or model-based eye origin and valid face pose are required for spatial calibration.')
        self.collector.add(ob)
        progress = self.collector.progress()
        self.status.setText(f"{progress['accepted']}/{progress['required']} · {progress['reason']}")
        self.progressbar.setValue(round(100*progress['fraction']))
        if self.collector.complete:
            self.context = dict(getattr(ob,'context',{}))
            self.records.extend(self.collector.records(extrinsics=self.extrinsics))
            self.collector = None
            self.index += 1
            if self.index == len(self.targets):
                self.samples_ready.emit({'records':self.records,'context':self.context,'session_id':self.session_id,
                                         'purpose':self.purpose,'targets':self.targets})
                self.accept()
            else:
                self.refresh_target()
        elif progress['state'] in ('failed','timeout','timed_out','incompatible'):
            self.collector = None;self.button.setEnabled(True)
            self.status.setText('Collection timed out. Adjust lighting and head pose, then retry.')

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
