"""Guided calibration and validation workflows using settled target acquisition.

Camera/tracker resources are owned by the caller. Tk is only a presentation
layer; ``ScreenSession.prepare`` and ``consume_frame`` also work headlessly.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time
import uuid
import numpy as np
import cv2

from .acquisition import TargetCollector
from .clock import host_now_s
from .analytics import compare_anchors
from .capture import FrameSource
from .vision import GazeTracker
from .calibration import (GazeModel, fit_gaze_model, load_profile, update_profile,
                          new_profile, profile_geometry_hash)
from .validation import validate_gaze_records
from .geometry import world_to_camera_point
from .types import CameraExtrinsics, CameraIntrinsics, Frame


def _display_size(size):
    if len(size) != 2 or any(int(v) != v or v < 2 for v in size):
        raise ValueError("display dimensions must be integers >=2")
    return int(size[0]), int(size[1])


def grid_targets(size: tuple[int, int], cols: int = 4, rows: int | None = None):
    rows = rows or cols
    if cols < 3 or rows < 3:
        raise ValueError("calibration grid needs at least 3x3 targets")
    width, height = np.asarray(_display_size(size), float) - 1
    xs, ys = np.linspace(.09, .91, cols), np.linspace(.18, .82, rows)
    return [(f"{r}-{c}", (float(x * width), float(y * height)))
            for r, y in enumerate(ys)
            for c, x in (enumerate(xs) if r % 2 == 0 else reversed(list(enumerate(xs))))]


def offset_validation_targets(size: tuple[int, int]):
    width, height = np.asarray(_display_size(size), float) - 1
    return [(f"validation-{i}", (x * width, y * height)) for i, (x, y) in enumerate([
        (.25, .20), (.75, .20), (.50, .35), (.20, .65), (.80, .65), (.50, .80),
        (.32, .50), (.68, .50), (.50, .50)])]


def _capabilities(source):
    getter = getattr(source, "capabilities", None)
    return dict(getter() or {}) if getter else {}


def _tracking_context(profile, tracker, source, image_size=None, camera_id=None):
    """Build current context from actual inputs, not the saved model's identity."""
    context = deepcopy(tracker.context(image_size))
    caps = _capabilities(source)
    explicit_id = camera_id if camera_id is not None else (
        getattr(source, "device", None) if getattr(source, "device", None) is not None
        else caps.get("source", caps.get("serial")))
    if explicit_id is not None:
        context["camera_id"] = str(explicit_id)
    backend = caps.get("requested_backend", caps.get("backend"))
    if backend is not None:
        context["backend"] = str(backend).lower()
        context["camera_backend"] = str(backend).lower()
    context["subject_id"] = profile.get("subject_id", profile.get("context", {}).get("subject_id"))
    context["mirror"] = False  # CLI paths do not mirror acquisition frames.
    # Requested controls, when present, match GUI setting semantics. A driver
    # readback is separately reported by the source; do not pretend it is the
    # user's requested value when a backend expresses exposure differently.
    context.update({"exposure_native": None, "exposure_us": None, "gain": None, "ir_bit_depth": None})
    controls = caps.get("controls", {})
    for source_key, key in (("exposure_native", "exposure_native"),
                            ("ExposureTime", "exposure_us"),
                            ("gain_native", "gain"), ("Gain", "gain")):
        if source_key in controls and "requested" in controls[source_key]:
            context[key] = controls[source_key]["requested"]
    if hasattr(source, "ir_bit_depth"):
        context["ir_bit_depth"] = source.ir_bit_depth
    return context


def _bind_observation(observation, profile, tracker, source, camera_id=None):
    context = _tracking_context(profile, tracker, source, observation.image_size, camera_id)
    context.update(deepcopy(observation.context))
    context["image_size"] = list(observation.image_size)
    observation.context = context
    return observation


def _validate_context(model, context, profile):
    mismatch = model.context_mismatches(context, require_present=True)
    if mismatch:
        raise ValueError("calibration profile does not match current tracking configuration: " + ", ".join(mismatch))
    expected_hash = model.metadata.get("calibration_context_hash")
    if expected_hash and expected_hash != profile_geometry_hash(profile):
        raise ValueError("camera/user/world geometry changed; recalibrate before validation")


def _ensure_profile_unchanged(path, baseline):
    if not Path(path).exists():
        if baseline.get("screen_model") or baseline.get("spatial_model") or baseline.get("intrinsics"):
            raise ValueError("calibration profile was removed during acquisition")
        return
    current = load_profile(path)
    if profile_geometry_hash(current) != profile_geometry_hash(baseline):
        raise ValueError("calibration profile geometry changed during acquisition; restart the session")
    for field in ("screen_model", "spatial_model"):
        if json.dumps(current.get(field), sort_keys=True) != json.dumps(baseline.get(field), sort_keys=True):
            raise ValueError("a gaze model changed during acquisition; restart the session")


def _validation_report(model, records, kind, screen_size=None, session_id=None, *,
                       context=None, extrinsics=None):
    if kind != model.kind:
        raise ValueError("validation kind differs from the fitted model")
    actual = deepcopy(context if context is not None else records[0].get("context", {}))
    if kind == "screen" and screen_size is not None:
        actual["screen_size"] = list(_display_size(screen_size))
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, records, context=actual,
                                   session_id=session_id, extrinsics=extrinsics)
    # Retain the established CLI field names while using the same evidence gates
    # as the desktop and public SDK validator.
    report.update(timestamp_utc=report["created_utc"],
                  outside_training_envelope_samples=report["out_of_envelope_samples"],
                  outside_training_envelope_fraction=1-report["in_envelope_fraction"],
                  prediction_method="unfiltered predictions on recorded target observations",
                  target_ground_truth="user_instructed_fixation_on_measured_targets",
                  notes=("Screen pixel errors depend on the user's actual fixation; primary-display identity is not independently verified."
                         if kind == "screen" else
                         "Angular error uses the configured eye-origin estimate, which may use generic facial anatomy; validate physical surface hits independently."))
    return report


class ScreenSession:
    def __init__(self, source: FrameSource, tracker: GazeTracker, profile_path: str,
                 samples_per_target: int = 16, grid: int = 4, validate: bool = False,
                 camera_id: str | None = None, max_seconds_per_target: float = 10,
                 drift: bool = False):
        if samples_per_target < 4:
            raise ValueError("need >=4 samples per target")
        if max_seconds_per_target <= 1.2:
            raise ValueError("allow more than 1.2 seconds for settling and target acquisition")
        self.source, self.tracker, self.profile_path = source, tracker, profile_path
        self.samples_per_target, self.grid = int(samples_per_target), int(grid)
        self.validate, self.drift = bool(validate or drift), bool(drift)
        self.camera_id = camera_id
        self.max_seconds_per_target = float(max_seconds_per_target)
        self.samples = []
        self.index = 0
        self.collecting = False
        self.collect_started = 0.0
        self.accepted = 0
        self.aborted = self.completed = False
        self.last_error = ""
        self.session_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.collector: TargetCollector | None = None
        self.profile = None
        self.model = None
        self.actual_context = None
        self._observer = None

    def prepare(self, screen_size: tuple[int, int]):
        """Initialize display targets and profile checks without creating a GUI."""
        self.size = _display_size(screen_size)
        self.profile = load_profile(self.profile_path) if Path(self.profile_path).exists() else new_profile()
        if self.validate:
            if not self.profile.get("screen_model"):
                raise ValueError("first calibrate a compatible version-2 screen model")
            self.model = GazeModel.from_dict(self.profile["screen_model"])
            if tuple(self.model.metadata["screen_size"]) != self.size:
                raise ValueError("screen resolution changed; recalibrate before validation")
            preliminary = _tracking_context(self.profile, self.tracker, self.source, camera_id=self.camera_id)
            mismatch = self.model.context_mismatches(preliminary)
            if mismatch:
                raise ValueError("profile configuration mismatch: " + ", ".join(mismatch))
        center = tuple((np.asarray(self.size, float) - 1) / 2)
        self.targets = ([("anchor_initial", center), ("anchor_repeat", center)] if self.drift else
                        offset_validation_targets(self.size) if self.validate else grid_targets(self.size, self.grid))
        # Use the same depth-aware observation path as live tracking. This engine
        # is deliberately uncalibrated and does not own/close the caller's tracker.
        from .engine import GazeEngine
        self._observer = GazeEngine(self.tracker)
        return self.targets

    def run(self) -> dict | None:
        import tkinter as tk
        import sys
        if sys.platform == "win32":
            try:
                import ctypes
                ctypes.windll.user32.SetProcessDPIAware()
            except (OSError, AttributeError):
                pass
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        try:
            self.root.title("GazeSpace — " + ("drift check" if self.drift else "gaze calibration"))
            self.root.configure(bg="#09111e")
            self.root.attributes("-fullscreen", True)
            self.root.attributes("-topmost", True)
            self.root.focus_force()
            self.prepare((self.root.winfo_screenwidth(), self.root.winfo_screenheight()))
            self.canvas = tk.Canvas(self.root, highlightthickness=0, bg="#09111e")
            self.canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            self.root.bind("<space>", lambda _: self.start_target())
            self.root.bind("<Return>", lambda _: self.start_target())
            self.root.bind("<Escape>", lambda _: self.abort())
            self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.abort)
            self.draw()
            self.root.after(30, self.tick)
            self.root.mainloop()
        finally:
            # No camera/tracker close here: callers may reuse the existing app's
            # camera and are responsible for releasing their own resources.
            try:
                self.root.destroy()
            except tk.TclError:
                pass
        if self.aborted or not self.completed:
            return None
        return self.finish()

    def start_target(self):
        if self.completed or self.collecting or self.aborted:
            return
        if not hasattr(self, "targets"):
            raise RuntimeError("prepare a screen size before starting acquisition")
        target_id, target_px = self.targets[self.index]
        norm = np.asarray(target_px, float) / (np.asarray(self.size, float) - 1)
        self.collector = TargetCollector(target_id, norm, min_samples=self.samples_per_target,
                                         max_seconds=self.max_seconds_per_target,
                                         session_id=self.session_id)
        self.collector.start()
        self.collecting = True
        self.collect_started = host_now_s()
        self.accepted = 0
        self.last_error = "Allow fixation to settle before samples are collected"
        self.draw()

    def abort(self):
        self.aborted = True
        self.collecting = False
        if self.collector:
            self.collector.cancel()
        if hasattr(self, "root"):
            self.root.destroy()

    def consume_frame(self, frame: Frame | None):
        """Process one frame; return collector progress, or None when idle."""
        if self.completed or self.aborted:
            return None
        if frame is None:
            self.last_error = "Camera frame missing or source ended"
            if self.collecting and host_now_s() - self.collect_started > self.max_seconds_per_target:
                self.collector.cancel()
                self.collecting = False
                self.accepted = 0
                self.last_error = "Camera timeout; reconnect, then press SPACE to retry this target"
            return self.collector.progress() if self.collector else None
        self._observer.process_frame(frame)
        observation = self._observer.last_observation
        _bind_observation(observation, self.profile, self.tracker, self.source, self.camera_id)
        # Bind to actual frame size/method/origin even for validation. An invalid
        # detection can lack an eye-origin source; its data will be rejected by
        # acquisition rather than masquerading as a different calibrated method.
        if observation.features is not None and observation.eyes_open and not observation.blink:
            if self.model is not None:
                _validate_context(self.model, observation.context, self.profile)
            if self.actual_context is None:
                self.actual_context = deepcopy(observation.context)
            elif observation.context != self.actual_context:
                raise ValueError("tracking context changed during acquisition; restart calibration")
        if not self.collecting:
            return None
        self.collector.add(observation)
        state = self.collector.progress()
        self.accepted = state["accepted"]
        self.last_error = state["reason"].replace("_", " ")
        if self.collector.complete:
            self.samples.extend(self.collector.records(screen_size=self.size))
            self.collecting = False
            self.index += 1
            self.last_error = "Target captured; look at the next dot, then press SPACE"
            if hasattr(self, "canvas"):
                self.canvas.bell()
            if self.index >= len(self.targets):
                self.completed = True
                if hasattr(self, "root"):
                    self.root.destroy()
        elif state["state"] in ("timeout", "incompatible"):
            self.collecting = False
            self.accepted = 0
            self.last_error = "Target not saved: " + state["reason"].replace("_", " ") + "; press SPACE to retry"
        return state

    def finish(self):
        """Fit or validate after all targets; usable by headless integrations."""
        if not self.completed or not self.samples or self.actual_context is None:
            raise ValueError("all targets must be acquired before finishing")
        _ensure_profile_unchanged(self.profile_path, self.profile)
        if self.validate:
            _validate_context(self.model, self.actual_context, self.profile)
            if self.drift:
                report = evaluate_drift(self.samples, self.model, self.size)
                report.update({"session_id": self.session_id, "model_fingerprint": self.model.fingerprint,
                               "timestamp_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()})
                update_profile(self.profile_path, drift_validation=report)
            else:
                report = _validation_report(self.model, self.samples, "screen", self.size, self.session_id,
                                            context=self.actual_context)
                update_profile(self.profile_path, screen_validation=report)
            return report
        model = fit_gaze_model(self.samples, "screen", screen_size=self.size,
                               context=self.actual_context, session_id=self.session_id)
        update_profile(self.profile_path, screen_model=model.to_dict(),
                       camera_id=self.actual_context.get("camera_id", self.camera_id),
                       context=self.actual_context)
        return model.report

    def draw(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "canvas"):
            return
        self.canvas.delete("all")
        width, height = self.size
        title = "ANCHOR DRIFT CHECK" if self.drift else "INDEPENDENT VALIDATION" if self.validate else "SCREEN CALIBRATION"
        self.canvas.create_text(width / 2, 40, text="GAZESPACE · " + title,
                                fill="#eff7ff", font=("Segoe UI", 24, "bold"))
        if self.collecting:
            state = self.collector.progress()
            subtitle = f'{state["state"].title()} · {state["accepted"]}/{self.samples_per_target} samples · {state["stable_duration_s"]:.1f}s stable'
        elif self.drift and self.index == 1:
            subtitle = "Wait and move naturally between anchors, then look at the center and press SPACE"
        else:
            subtitle = "Look directly at the dot, hold head naturally, press SPACE to collect"
        self.canvas.create_text(width / 2, 84, text=f"Target {min(self.index + 1, len(self.targets))}/{len(self.targets)}  |  " + subtitle,
                                fill="#93adc5", font=("Segoe UI", 16))
        if self.index < len(self.targets):
            _, (x, y) = self.targets[self.index]
            for radius, color, stroke in ((25, "#21436a", 3), (10, "#40dfc0", 4), (3, "#ffffff", 0)):
                self.canvas.create_oval(x - radius, y - radius, x + radius, y + radius,
                                        outline=color if stroke else "", fill=color if not stroke else "", width=stroke)
        self.canvas.create_text(width / 2, height - 40, text=f"ESC: cancel  |  SPACE: sample  |  {self.last_error}",
                                fill="#f6a67a", font=("Segoe UI", 13))

    def tick(self):
        if self.aborted or self.completed:
            return
        try:
            self.consume_frame(self.source.read())
        except (ValueError, RuntimeError, OSError, cv2.error) as exc:
            self.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:130]}"
            if self.collector:
                self.collector.cancel()
            self.collecting = False
            self.accepted = 0
        if not self.completed and not self.aborted:
            self.draw()
            self.root.after(30, self.tick)


def evaluate_drift(samples: list[dict], model: GazeModel, screen_size: tuple[int, int]) -> dict:
    initial = [row for row in samples if row["target_id"] == "anchor_initial"]
    repeated = [row for row in samples if row["target_id"] == "anchor_repeat"]
    if len(initial) < 4 or len(repeated) < 4:
        raise ValueError("drift check needs >=4 samples at each repeated fixation")
    try:
        timestamps = np.asarray([row["time_s"] for row in samples], float)
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("drift check requires acquisition timestamps") from exc
    if not np.isfinite(timestamps).all() or np.any(np.diff(timestamps) <= 0):
        raise ValueError("drift timestamps must be finite, unique and increasing")
    if repeated[0]["time_s"] <= initial[-1]["time_s"]:
        raise ValueError("repeat fixation must follow the initial fixation")
    extent = np.asarray(_display_size(screen_size), float) - 1
    first = model.predict(np.asarray([r["features"] for r in initial], float)) * extent
    last = model.predict(np.asarray([r["features"] for r in repeated], float)) * extent
    # In v2 the actual center is halfway between pixel 0 and pixel width-1.
    target = extent / 2
    report = compare_anchors(first, last, target_px=target)
    # compare_anchors also supports a caller-selected diagnostic threshold. This
    # workflow has not collected any agreed threshold, so do not present its
    # library default as a selected limit or as an acceptance result.
    report["threshold_px"] = None
    report["outcome"] = "measurement_only_no_agreed_criteria"
    provenance = [getattr(model, "metadata", {}), *samples,
                  *(row.get("context", {}) for row in samples)]
    synthetic = any(isinstance(value, dict) and (
        bool(value.get("synthetic") or value.get("simulated"))
        or value.get("backend") == "synthetic"
        or value.get("eye_origin_source") == "synthetic_fixture") for value in provenance)
    report.update({"first_mean_px": report["first"]["mean_xy"],
                   "repeat_mean_px": report["repeat"]["mean_xy"],
                   "first_anchor_error_px": float(np.linalg.norm(np.mean(first, axis=0) - target)),
                   "repeat_anchor_error_px": float(np.linalg.norm(np.mean(last, axis=0) - target)),
                   # Historical field names retained, but now hold a correct
                   # RMS measure rather than std(radial distance).
                   "first_jitter_std_px": report["first"]["radial_rms"],
                   "repeat_jitter_std_px": report["repeat"]["radial_rms"],
                   "first_jitter_rms_px": report["first"]["radial_rms"],
                   "repeat_jitter_rms_px": report["repeat"]["radial_rms"],
                   "first_jitter_std_xy_px": report["first"]["std_xy"],
                   "repeat_jitter_std_xy_px": report["repeat"]["std_xy"],
                   "elapsed_s": float(repeated[-1]["time_s"] - initial[0]["time_s"]),
                   "synthetic": synthetic,
                   "measurement_provenance": "synthetic_software_fixture" if synthetic else "user_supplied_repeated_anchors",
                   "physical_accuracy_verified": False,
                   "acceptance_eligible": False,
                   "acceptance_status": "synthetic_software_only" if synthetic else "not_evaluated",
                   "acceptance_criteria": None, "acceptance_passed": None,
                   "acceptance_note": "Repeated center anchors alone do not establish global gaze accuracy; baseline error, drift and operating duration need agreed limits.",
                   "method": "two instructed center fixations; radial RMS and mean offset in calibrated pixels",
                   "warning": "Drift alone does not establish accuracy. Recalibrate if baseline target error or drift is excessive."})
    return report


def read_spatial_targets(path: str) -> list[dict]:
    payload = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if not isinstance(payload, list) or len(payload) < 9:
        raise ValueError("spatial target file must be a list of >=9 measured targets")
    keys = set()
    points = []
    for row in payload:
        if not isinstance(row, dict) or "id" not in row or "xyz_m" not in row:
            raise ValueError("each target needs id and finite xyz_m [x,y,z]")
        try:
            point = np.asarray(row["xyz_m"], float)
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            raise ValueError("each target needs finite xyz_m [x,y,z]") from exc
        if point.shape != (3,) or not np.isfinite(point).all() or not str(row["id"]).strip():
            raise ValueError("each target needs id and finite xyz_m [x,y,z]")
        if str(row["id"]) in keys:
            raise ValueError("target ids must be unique")
        keys.add(str(row["id"]))
        points.append(point)
    array = np.asarray(points)
    if len(np.unique(np.round(array, 5), axis=0)) != len(array):
        raise ValueError("target coordinates must be distinct; different labels cannot duplicate a fixation")
    singular = np.linalg.svd(array - np.mean(array, axis=0), compute_uv=False)
    if singular[1] < .005:
        raise ValueError("target geometry is collinear or has insufficient horizontal/vertical spread")
    return payload


def _check_spatial_geometry(targets, extrinsics, require_multidepth):
    world = np.asarray([row["xyz_m"] for row in targets], float)
    camera = np.asarray([world_to_camera_point(point, extrinsics) for point in world])
    if np.any(camera[:, 2] <= .02):
        raise ValueError("physical targets must lie in front of the calibrated camera")
    depth_span = float(np.ptp(camera[:, 2]))
    singular = np.linalg.svd(world - world.mean(axis=0), compute_uv=False)
    if require_multidepth and (depth_span < .02 or singular[2] < .005):
        raise ValueError("volumetric calibration needs a nonplanar target arrangement and >=20 mm depth spread in CAMERA coordinates; use single-depth mode only for planar experiments")
    return {"camera_depth_span_m": depth_span, "target_rank": int(np.count_nonzero(singular > .005)),
            "volumetric_target_coverage": bool(depth_span >= .02 and singular[2] >= .005)}


def spatial_session(source: FrameSource, tracker: GazeTracker, profile_path: str, targets_path: str,
                    samples_per_target: int = 16, validate: bool = False,
                    max_seconds: float = 15, require_multidepth: bool = True) -> dict:
    profile = load_profile(profile_path)
    if not profile.get("intrinsics") or not profile.get("extrinsics"):
        raise ValueError("need calibrated intrinsic and board-to-world extrinsic profile")
    if CameraIntrinsics.from_dict(profile["intrinsics"]).source not in ("measured", "factory"):
        raise ValueError("physical workspace calibration cannot use approximate focal length")
    extrinsics = CameraExtrinsics.from_dict(profile["extrinsics"])
    targets = read_spatial_targets(targets_path)
    geometry = _check_spatial_geometry(targets, extrinsics, require_multidepth)
    if samples_per_target < 4:
        raise ValueError("need >=4 samples per target")
    if max_seconds <= 1.2:
        raise ValueError("allow more than 1.2 seconds for each physical target")
    model = None
    if validate:
        if not profile.get("spatial_model"):
            raise ValueError("first calibrate a compatible spatial gaze model")
        model = GazeModel.from_dict(profile["spatial_model"])
        mismatch = model.context_mismatches(_tracking_context(profile, tracker, source))
        if mismatch:
            raise ValueError("spatial profile configuration mismatch: " + ", ".join(mismatch))
    from .engine import GazeEngine
    observer = GazeEngine(tracker)
    records, actual_context = [], None
    session_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    for index, target in enumerate(targets):
        print(f"\n[{index + 1}/{len(targets)}] Look at physical target {target['id']} at {target['xyz_m']} meters. "
              "Hold a steady fixation; press Enter to start or q to cancel.")
        if input("Target ready? ").strip().lower() == "q":
            raise KeyboardInterrupt("spatial calibration cancelled")
        collector = TargetCollector(str(target["id"]), target["xyz_m"], "spatial",
                                     min_samples=samples_per_target, max_seconds=max_seconds,
                                     session_id=session_id)
        collector.start()
        started = host_now_s()
        while not collector.complete and host_now_s() - started <= max_seconds:
            frame = source.read()
            if frame is None:
                if getattr(source, "eof", False):
                    raise RuntimeError("video ended during spatial target acquisition")
                time.sleep(.02)
                continue
            observer.process_frame(frame)
            observation = observer.last_observation
            _bind_observation(observation, profile, tracker, source)
            if observation.features is not None and observation.eyes_open and not observation.blink:
                if model:
                    _validate_context(model, observation.context, profile)
                if actual_context is None:
                    actual_context = deepcopy(observation.context)
                elif observation.context != actual_context:
                    raise ValueError("tracking context changed during physical target acquisition")
            collector.add(observation)
            if collector.state in ("timeout", "incompatible"):
                break
        if not collector.complete:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Target {target['id']} not saved: {collector.reason}; check pupil visibility, head stability, camera and pose")
        records.extend(collector.records(extrinsics=extrinsics))
        print(f"  Captured {len(collector.observations)} settled samples over {collector.progress()['stable_duration_s']:.2f} seconds")
    _ensure_profile_unchanged(profile_path, profile)
    if validate:
        report = _validation_report(model, records, "spatial", session_id=session_id,
                                    context=actual_context, extrinsics=extrinsics)
        report["target_geometry"] = geometry
        update_profile(profile_path, spatial_validation=report)
        return report
    model = fit_gaze_model(records, "spatial", context=actual_context, session_id=session_id)
    model.report["target_geometry"] = geometry
    update_profile(profile_path, spatial_model=model.to_dict(), context=actual_context,
                   camera_id=actual_context.get("camera_id", profile.get("camera_id")))
    return model.report

===== END FILE: src/gazespace/workflows.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/test_clock_domain.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 33354f0747297db2c9713af004cc78cf20d29dfd

"""Windows clock regressions: coarse legacy ticks and different clock epochs.

Linux commonly backs monotonic/perf_counter with the same clock. Deliberately
separating them exposes mixed-domain freshness bugs without needing a camera.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from threading import Event
from types import SimpleNamespace

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

import gazespace.clock as clock_module
from gazespace.capture import FrameSource, OpenCVSource, PylonSource, SyntheticEyeSource
from gazespace.clock import HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN, host_now_s
from gazespace.engine import GazeEngine
from gazespace.runtime import TrackingRuntime
from gazespace.sessions import SessionRecorder, load_session
from gazespace.types import Frame, GazeResult, Observation


class HostClock:
    def __init__(self, value=100_000.0, step=0.0):
        self.value, self.step = value, step

    def __call__(self):
        current = self.value
        self.value += self.step
        return current


def separate_clocks(monkeypatch, *, step=0.0):
    host = HostClock(step=step)
    # Many rapid reads share a legacy Windows tick, at a different epoch.
    monkeypatch.setattr(clock_module.time, "monotonic", lambda: 17.0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(clock_module.time, "perf_counter", host)
    return host


def fake_pylon(monkeypatch):
    class Node:
        def __init__(self, value): self.Value = value

    class Result:
        PixelType, TimeStamp = 1, 987654321
        Array = np.full((12, 16), 127, np.uint8)
        def GrabSucceeded(self): return True
        def Release(self): pass

    class Camera:
        Width, Height = Node(16), Node(12)
        def Open(self): self.open = True
        def IsOpen(self): return self.open
        def Close(self): self.open = False
        def StartGrabbing(self, *_): self.grabbing = True
        def IsGrabbing(self): return self.grabbing
        def StopGrabbing(self): self.grabbing = False
        def RetrieveResult(self, *_): return Result()

    device = SimpleNamespace(GetSerialNumber=lambda: "00017", GetModelName=lambda: "Clock fixture")
    factory = SimpleNamespace(EnumerateDevices=lambda: [device], CreateDevice=lambda value: value)
    pylon = SimpleNamespace(TlFactory=SimpleNamespace(GetInstance=lambda: factory),
                            InstantCamera=lambda _: Camera(), ImageFormatConverter=lambda: SimpleNamespace(),
                            PixelType_Mono8=1, PixelType_BGR8packed=3,
                            GrabStrategy_LatestImageOnly=1, TimeoutHandling_ThrowException=1)
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "pypylon", SimpleNamespace(pylon=pylon))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("backend", ["opencv", "pylon"])
def test_rapid_live_capture_uses_high_resolution_host_clock(monkeypatch, backend):
    host = separate_clocks(monkeypatch, step=.0001)
    if backend == "pylon":
        fake_pylon(monkeypatch)
        source = PylonSource(serial="00017")
    else:
        class Capture:
            def isOpened(self): return True
            def set(self, *_): return True
            def get(self, prop): return {cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS: 30, cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH: 16,
                                       cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT: 12}.get(prop, 0)
            def getBackendName(self): return "clock-fixture"
            def read(self): return True, np.zeros((12, 16, 3), np.uint8)
            def release(self): pass
        monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "VideoCapture", lambda *_: Capture())
        source = OpenCVSource(0)
    with source:
        frames = [source.read() for _ in range(8)]
        assert source.capabilities()["host_clock_domain"] == HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN
        if backend == "pylon":
            assert source.capabilities()["serial"] == "00017"
            assert all(f.metadata["hardware_ticks"] == 987654321 for f in frames)
        stamps = [f.timestamp_s for f in frames]
        assert all(b > a for a, b in zip(stamps, stamps[1:]))
        assert stamps[-1] - stamps[0] < .0156
        assert all(100_000 <= f.timestamp_s <= f.received_monotonic_s <= host.value for f in frames)
        assert all(f.metadata["host_clock_domain"] == HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN for f in frames)


def test_runtime_age_uses_receive_clock_and_preserves_independent_source_pts(monkeypatch):
    host = separate_clocks(monkeypatch)
    delivered, release = Event(), Event()

    class Source(FrameSource):
        def __init__(self): self.reads = 0
        def read(self):
            self.reads += 1
            if self.reads > 1:
                release.wait(2)
                return None
            return Frame(np.zeros((12, 16, 3), np.uint8), 2.5,
                         received_monotonic_s=host_now_s(),
                         metadata={"host_clock_domain": HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN})
        def request_stop(self): release.set()
        def close(self): pass

    class Engine:
        def process(self, image, stamp):
            return GazeResult(stamp, True, "fixture", "clock-test", .9,
                              gaze_screen_px=(10, 20), screen_valid=True)
        def close(self): pass

    run = TrackingRuntime(Source, Engine, max_age_s=.2,
                          on_result=lambda *_: delivered.set())
    try:
        run.start()
        assert delivered.wait(2), "Runtime rejected a correctly timed source"
        fresh = run.snapshot(False)
        assert fresh["latest"]["valid"] and fresh["latest"]["timestamp_s"] == 2.5
        assert fresh["metrics"]["capture_age_ms"] == 0
        host.value += .25
        stale = run.snapshot(False)
        assert not stale["latest"]["valid"]
        assert stale["latest"]["reason"] == "stale_capture"
        assert stale["latest"]["gaze_screen_px"] is None
        assert stale["metrics"]["capture_age_ms"] == pytest.approx(250)
    finally:
        release.set()
        assert run.stop(1)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("received", [float("nan"), float("inf"), 100_001.0])
def test_runtime_snapshot_never_clamps_wrong_clock_to_fresh(monkeypatch, received):
    separate_clocks(monkeypatch)
    run = TrackingRuntime(lambda: None, lambda: None)
    run._state, run._last_received = "running", received
    run._result = GazeResult(3, True, "fixture", "test", .9,
                             gaze_screen_px=(10, 20), screen_valid=True)
    value = run.snapshot(False)
    assert value["latest"]["reason"] == "invalid_host_clock"
    assert value["latest"]["gaze_screen_px"] is None
    assert not value["latest"]["screen_valid"]
    assert value["metrics"]["capture_age_ms"] is None


def test_engine_duration_resolves_submillisecond_work_with_coarse_legacy_clock(monkeypatch):
    host = separate_clocks(monkeypatch)
    class Tracker:
        def process(self, image, stamp):
            host.value += .0007
            return Observation(stamp, (16, 12))
        def close(self): pass
    engine = GazeEngine(Tracker())
    result = engine.process(np.zeros((12, 16, 3), np.uint8), timestamp_s=7.5)
    assert result.timestamp_s == 7.5
    assert result.processing_ms == pytest.approx(.7, abs=1e-6)


def test_default_recording_clock_preserves_relative_session_time(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    host = separate_clocks(monkeypatch)
    with SessionRecorder(tmp_path, {"provenance": "synthetic"}) as recorder:
        host.value += .003
        row = recorder.append(GazeResult(7.5, False, "fixture", "test", 0))
        assert row["session_time_s"] == pytest.approx(.003, abs=1e-8)
        assert row["timestamp_s"] == 7.5
    loaded = load_session(recorder.path)
    assert loaded.manifest["session_time_clock_domain"] == HOST_CLOCK_DOMAIN
    assert loaded.records[0]["session_time_s"] == pytest.approx(.003, abs=1e-8)


def test_generated_source_pts_is_not_replaced_by_fast_decode_arrival(monkeypatch):
    separate_clocks(monkeypatch, step=.0001)
    with SyntheticEyeSource(realtime=False, fps=20) as source:
        first, second = source.read(), source.read()
    assert second.timestamp_s - first.timestamp_s == pytest.approx(.05)
    assert second.metadata["video_pts_s"] == .05
    assert second.received_monotonic_s - first.received_monotonic_s < .001

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/test_core_v2_regressions.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Regression tests for unsafe v1 behaviors, independent of physical-camera claims."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from gazespace.calibration import (GazeModel, fit_gaze_model, load_json, load_profile,
                                  new_profile, save_json, update_profile)
from gazespace.engine import GazeEngine
from gazespace.filtering import OneEuro
from gazespace.geometry import (FACE_INDICES, FACE_TEMPLATE_M, approximate_intrinsics,
                               estimate_head_pose, ray_surface_intersection)
from gazespace.types import CameraExtrinsics, CameraIntrinsics, Frame, Observation, Surface
from gazespace.vision import GazeTracker, eye_rel_pos, observation_from_landmarks
from gazespace.pupil import detect_pupil


def calibration_rows():
    rows = []
    for i, (x, y) in enumerate((x, y) for x in np.linspace(.1, .9, 4) for y in np.linspace(.1, .9, 4)):
        for _ in range(8):
            rows.append({"features": [x, y, x, y, 0, 0, 0, .5, .5, .7],
                         "target": [x, y], "target_id": str(i)})
    return rows


def make_model(**context):
    return fit_gaze_model(calibration_rows(), screen_size=(640, 480), context=context)


class InjectedTracker:
    def __init__(self, observation):
        self.observation = observation

    def process(self, frame, timestamp):
        result = deepcopy(self.observation)
        result.timestamp_s = timestamp
        return result

    def close(self):
        pass


def center_observation(timestamp=1):
    return Observation(timestamp, (640, 480), eyes_open=True, quality=.9,
                       features=np.array([.5, .5, .5, .5, 0, 0, 0, .5, .5, .7]))


def face_landmarks(blink=False):
    intr = approximate_intrinsics(640, 480)
    xy, _ = cv2.projectPoints(FACE_TEMPLATE_M, np.zeros(3), np.array([0., 0., .65]),
                              intr.camera_matrix, intr.distortion)
    landmarks = {index: tuple(point) for index, point in zip(FACE_INDICES, xy.reshape(-1, 2))}
    # Preserve PnP outer corners and place realistic inner corners between them.
    landmarks[133] = (landmarks[33][0]+25, landmarks[33][1])
    landmarks[362] = (landmarks[263][0]-25, landmarks[263][1])
    aperture = .5 if blink else 6
    for iris, left, right, upper, lower, contour in (
        (468, 33, 133, 159, 145, (33, 160, 158, 133, 153, 144)),
        (473, 362, 263, 386, 374, (362, 385, 387, 263, 373, 380)),
    ):
        a, b = np.array(landmarks[left]), np.array(landmarks[right])
        midpoint = (a+b)/2
        landmarks[iris] = tuple(midpoint)
        landmarks[upper] = tuple(midpoint+[0, -aperture])
        landmarks[lower] = tuple(midpoint+[0, aperture])
        for j, fraction in ((1,.33), (2,.67), (4,.67), (5,.33)):
            landmarks[contour[j]] = tuple(a*(1-fraction)+b*fraction + [0, -aperture if j < 3 else aperture])
    return landmarks


def test_realistic_frontal_landmark_layout_has_neutral_pitch():
    # These manually placed image coordinates are not generated from the template.
    points = {1:(640,360), 152:(640,450), 33:(580,315), 263:(700,315),
              61:(600,400), 291:(680,400)}
    pose = estimate_head_pose(points, approximate_intrinsics(1280, 720))
    assert pose is not None
    assert abs(pose.pitch_deg) < 15
    assert abs(pose.yaw_deg) < 1
    assert abs(pose.roll_deg) < 1


def test_mirrored_eye_landmarks_do_not_emit_false_pose():
    points = face_landmarks()
    points[33], points[263] = points[263], points[33]
    assert estimate_head_pose(points, approximate_intrinsics(640, 480)) is None


def test_vertical_iris_feature_does_not_expand_when_lids_close():
    open_eye = eye_rel_pos((52,22), (20,20), (80,20), (50,10), (50,30))
    narrow_eye = eye_rel_pos((52,22), (20,20), (80,20), (50,18), (50,22))
    assert open_eye == pytest.approx(narrow_eye)


def test_ir_blink_is_not_bypassed_by_roi_fallback():
    class Detector:
        def detect(self, *args, **kwargs):
            return face_landmarks(blink=True)
        def close(self):
            pass
    tracker = GazeTracker(mode="ir", detector=Detector(),
                          roi_normalized={"right":(.2,.3,.2,.3), "left":(.6,.3,.2,.3)})
    image = np.full((480,640,3),180,np.uint8)
    cv2.circle(image,(190,210),18,(10,10,10),-1)
    cv2.circle(image,(450,210),18,(10,10,10),-1)
    observation = tracker.process(image, 1)
    assert observation.blink and observation.features is None
    assert observation.message == "blink_or_occluded_eye"


def test_ir_missing_actual_pupil_does_not_substitute_rgb_iris():
    observation = observation_from_landmarks(face_landmarks(), (640,480),1,
                                              ir_image=np.full((480,640,3),180,np.uint8))
    assert observation.features is None
    assert observation.message.startswith("ir_pupil_unavailable")


def test_nested_dark_pupil_inside_bright_sclera_is_detected():
    eye = np.full((120,180),20,np.uint8)
    cv2.ellipse(eye,(90,60),(65,35),0,0,360,190,-1)
    cv2.circle(eye,(94,60),12,15,-1)
    pupil = detect_pupil(eye,(5,5,170,110),"dark",3,25)
    assert pupil is not None
    assert pupil.xy == pytest.approx((94,60),abs=1)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", ["duplicate_targets", "collinear_targets", "constant_eye_features", "empty", "nonfinite"])
def test_unobservable_calibration_is_rejected(failure):
    rows = calibration_rows()
    if failure == "duplicate_targets":
        for row in rows: row["target"] = [.5,.5]
    elif failure == "collinear_targets":
        for row in rows: row["target"][1] = row["target"][0]
    elif failure == "constant_eye_features":
        for row in rows: row["features"] = [.5,.5,.5,.5,0,0,0,.5,.5,.7]
    elif failure == "empty":
        rows = []
    elif failure == "nonfinite":
        rows[0]["features"][0] = float("nan")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        fit_gaze_model(rows, screen_size=(640,480))


def test_mixed_tracking_context_during_calibration_is_rejected():
    rows = calibration_rows()
    for row in rows: row["context"] = {"tracking_mode":"rgb"}
    rows[-1]["context"] = {"tracking_mode":"ir-roi"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="context"):
        fit_gaze_model(rows, screen_size=(640,480))


def test_model_rejects_wrong_coefficient_shape_and_zero_scale():
    original = make_model().to_dict()
    malformed = deepcopy(original)
    malformed["coeff"] = [[0,0]]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="coefficient"):
        GazeModel.from_dict(malformed)
    malformed = deepcopy(original)
    malformed["feature_scale"][0] = 0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="normalization"):
        GazeModel.from_dict(malformed)


def test_legacy_profile_cannot_reuse_v1_gaze_weights(tmp_path):
    legacy = {"schema_version":1,"screen_model":{"legacy":"weights"},"screen_validation":{"passed":True},
              "spatial_model":None,"intrinsics":None,"extrinsics":None,"surfaces":[]}
    path = tmp_path/"legacy.json"
    save_json(path,legacy)
    loaded = load_profile(path)
    assert loaded["schema_version"] == 2
    assert loaded["screen_model"] is None
    assert "screen_validation" not in loaded
    assert loaded["migration"]["recalibration_required"]


def test_camera_change_invalidates_fit_and_stale_success_report(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path/"profile.json"
    update_profile(path,camera_id="one",screen_model=make_model().to_dict())
    update_profile(path,screen_validation={"passed":True})
    changed = update_profile(path,camera_id="two")
    assert changed["screen_model"] is None
    assert "screen_validation" not in changed


def test_camera_identity_change_cannot_reuse_another_lens_intrinsics(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path/"profile.json"
    intr = approximate_intrinsics(640,480)
    intr.source = "measured"
    update_profile(path,camera_id="first",intrinsics=intr.to_dict())
    changed = update_profile(path,camera_id="second")
    assert changed["intrinsics"] is None
    assert changed["extrinsics"] is None


def test_intrinsic_changes_invalidate_holdout_evidence_unless_replaced(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path/"profile.json"
    intr = approximate_intrinsics(640,480)
    intr.source = "measured"
    first = update_profile(path,intrinsics=intr.to_dict(),camera_calibration_evidence={"training_image_sha256":["first"]},
                           camera_validation={"passed":True})
    assert first["camera_validation"]["passed"]
    intr.camera_matrix[0,0] += 10
    changed = update_profile(path,intrinsics=intr.to_dict(),camera_calibration_evidence={"training_image_sha256":["second"]})
    assert "camera_validation" not in changed
    assert changed["camera_calibration_evidence"]["training_image_sha256"] == ["second"]


def test_new_context_and_new_fit_can_be_installed_atomically(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path/"profile.json"
    profile = update_profile(path,camera_id="new-camera",context={"tracking_mode":"ir-roi"},
                             screen_model=make_model(tracking_mode="ir-roi").to_dict())
    assert profile["screen_model"] is not None
    assert load_profile(path)["screen_model"]["metadata"]["calibration_context_hash"]


def test_manual_profile_geometry_edit_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path/"profile.json"
    update_profile(path,camera_id="correct",screen_model=make_model().to_dict())
    edited = load_json(path)
    edited["camera_id"] = "different"
    save_json(path,edited)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="context changed"):
        load_profile(path)


def test_nonfinite_json_profile_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path/"nan.json"
    path.write_text('{"schema_version":2,"value":NaN}')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="nonfinite"):
        load_profile(path)

def test_one_euro_derivative_tracks_raw_motion_not_remaining_filter_error():
    filter_ = OneEuro(beta=.5)
    filter_.update([0.],0)
    filter_.update([1.],.1)
    initial_derivative = filter_.dx.copy()
    filter_.update([1.],.2)
    assert filter_.dx[0] < initial_derivative[0]

    assert ray_surface_intersection([.5,.5,2],[0,0,-1],surface) is not None

def test_aligned_depth_sets_measured_surface_origin_and_semantic_binding():
    ob = center_observation()
    ob.pupils_px = {"right":(295.,240.),"left":(345.,240.)}
    intr = CameraIntrinsics(640,480,np.array([[500.,0,320],[0,500.,240],[0,0,1]]),np.zeros(5),source="factory")
    frame = Frame(np.full((480,640,3),128,np.uint8),1,
                  metadata={"depth_aligned_to":"color","depth_intrinsics":intr.to_dict()},
                  depth_m=np.full((480,640),.6,np.float32))
    engine = GazeEngine(InjectedTracker(ob))
    engine.process_frame(frame)
    measured = engine.last_observation
    assert measured.eye_origin_camera_m == pytest.approx([0,0,.6])
    assert measured.eye_origin_source == "aligned_depth_pupil_midpoint"
    assert measured.context["eye_origin_source"] == measured.eye_origin_source
    assert "optical_center" in measured.warnings[-1]


def test_invalid_depth_never_silently_reuses_generic_origin():
    ob = center_observation()
    ob.pupils_px = {"right":(295.,240.),"left":(345.,240.)}
    ob.eye_origin_camera_m = np.array([0,0,.6])
    ob.eye_origin_source = "generic_face_pnp"
    intr = CameraIntrinsics(640,480,np.array([[500.,0,320],[0,500.,240],[0,0,1]]),np.zeros(5),source="factory")
    frame = Frame(np.full((480,640,3),128,np.uint8),1,
                  metadata={"depth_aligned_to":"color","depth_intrinsics":intr.to_dict()},
                  depth_m=np.zeros((480,640),np.float32))
    engine = GazeEngine(InjectedTracker(ob))
    engine.process_frame(frame)
    assert engine.last_observation.eye_origin_camera_m is None
    assert engine.last_observation.eye_origin_source == "depth_unavailable"

"""Actual Qt glyph checks for the Windows/offscreen missing-font regression."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen")

import pytest
from PySide6.QtGui import QFont, QImage, QColor
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from gazespace.ui import fonts
from gazespace.ui.main_window import MainWindow


def test_bundled_fonts_render_printable_ascii_and_real_styled_widgets(tmp_path):
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    registration = fonts.configure_app_fonts(app)
    assert {item["file"] for item in registration["fonts"]} == set(fonts.FONT_FILES)
    assert all(item["bytes"] > 100_000 for item in registration["fonts"])
    assert (fonts.FONT_DIRECTORY / registration["license"]).is_file()
    window = MainWindow(workspace=tmp_path / "workspace", interactive_errors=False)
    try:
        window.show()
        app.processEvents()
        report = fonts.verify_font_rendering(window.nav_buttons[0].font(), output_png=tmp_path / "actual_qt_glyphs.png")
        assert report["ascii_glyphs_checked"] == 95
        assert report["unicode_glyphs_checked"] == 14
        assert report["distinct_probe_rasters"] == report["expected_probe_rasters"] == 12
        assert not report["missing_glyphs"]
        assert (tmp_path / "actual_qt_glyphs.png").stat().st_size > 1000
        bold = fonts.verify_font_rendering(QFont(fonts.FONT_FAMILY, 13, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        assert bold["status"] == "passed"
    finally:
        window.close()
        app.processEvents()


def test_font_gate_rejects_missing_characters(monkeypatch):
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    fonts.configure_app_fonts(app)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fonts, "REQUIRED_UNICODE", "\U0010ffff")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="missing required glyphs"):
        fonts.verify_font_rendering()


def test_font_gate_rejects_repeated_tofu_rasters(monkeypatch):
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    fonts.configure_app_fonts(app)
    def identical_box(*_):
        image = QImage(64, 64, QImage.Format.Format_ARGB32)
        image.fill(QColor("black"))
        return image
    monkeypatch.setattr(fonts, "_rasterize", identical_box)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="repeated/missing shapes"):
        fonts.verify_font_rendering()

SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a

"""Opt-in real SDK checks plus failure-evidence regressions without hardware."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import pytest


def _load_verifier():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'scripts' / 'verify_pypylon_emulator.py'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('gazespace_pylon_verifier_test', path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_emulator_opt_in_failure_keeps_json_phase_and_traceback(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    verifier = _load_verifier()
    monkeypatch.delenv('PYLON_CAMEMU', raising=False)
    evidence = tmp_path / 'failure.json'
    with pytest.raises(verifier.EmulatorVerificationError, match='opt_in'):
        verifier.verify(str(evidence))
    report = json.loads(evidence.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    assert report['status'] == 'failed'
    assert report['failure']['phase'] == 'opt_in'
    assert 'RuntimeError' in report['failure']['traceback']
    assert set(report['environment']['clocks']) == {'monotonic', 'perf_counter'}
    assert report['physical_hardware_tested'] is False


def test_duplicate_clock_failure_preserves_frames_phase_and_cleanup(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Inject duplicate ticks; never turn the strict ordering gate into >=."""
    from gazespace.types import Frame
    import gazespace.capture
    verifier = _load_verifier()
    monkeypatch.setenv('PYLON_CAMEMU', '1')
    device = SimpleNamespace(GetDeviceClass=lambda: 'BaslerCamEmu',
                             GetSerialNumber=lambda: 'EMULATOR_TEST',
                             GetModelName=lambda: 'diagnostic-test-fixture')
    pylon = SimpleNamespace(TlFactory=SimpleNamespace(GetInstance=lambda: SimpleNamespace(
        EnumerateDevices=lambda: [device])))
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, 'pypylon', SimpleNamespace(pylon=pylon))
    original_version = verifier.importlib.metadata.version
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.importlib.metadata, 'version',
                        lambda name: 'test-fixture' if name == 'pypylon' else original_version(name))
    instances = []

    class Source:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs):
            self.sequence, self.closed, self.close_count = 0, False, 0
            self.cam = SimpleNamespace(IsOpen=lambda: not self.closed, IsGrabbing=lambda: not self.closed)
            self.controls = {}
            instances.append(self)
        def capabilities(self):
            return {'sdk_emulator': True, 'test_failure_injection': 'duplicate_host_ticks'}
        def read(self):
            self.sequence += 1
            return Frame(np.zeros((8, 8, 3), np.uint8), 1.0, self.sequence,
                         'EMULATOR_TEST', {'sdk_emulator': True}, received_monotonic_s=1.0)
        def close(self):
            self.closed = True
            self.close_count += 1

    monkeypatch.setattr(gazespace.capture, 'PylonSource', Source)
    evidence = tmp_path / 'clock_failure.json'
    with pytest.raises(verifier.EmulatorVerificationError, match='strict_frame_timestamps'):
        verifier.verify(str(evidence))
    report = json.loads(evidence.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    assert report['status'] == 'failed'
    assert report['failure']['phase'] == 'Mono8/timestamp_and_sequence'
    assert 'strict_frame_timestamps' in report['failure']['traceback']
    run = report['runs'][0]
    assert run['timestamps_s'] == [1.0]*5
    assert run['timestamp_deltas_s'] == [0.0]*4
    assert run['sequences'] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    assert len(run['frames']) == 5
    assert all(frame['owns_data'] for frame in run['frames'])
    assert run['closed_and_not_grabbing'] and run['idempotent_close']
    assert instances[0].closed and instances[0].close_count == 2


def _text(value):
    return value.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(value, bytes) else value or ''


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.environ.get('GAZESPACE_TEST_PYLON_EMULATOR') != '1',
                    reason='Opt-in: set GAZESPACE_TEST_PYLON_EMULATOR=1 with pypylon installed')
def test_real_pypylon_emulator_acquisition_controls_and_buffers(tmp_path):
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    evidence = tmp_path / 'emulator.json'
    # The CI diagnostics upload already includes release_evidence/windows*.
    # Keep the subprocess receipt and both streams outside ephemeral pytest dirs.
    saved = root / 'release_evidence'
    saved.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stem = ('windows_' if os.name == 'nt' else '') + 'pypylon_pytest'
    env = dict(os.environ, PYLON_CAMEMU='1', PYTHONPATH=str(root/'src'),
               PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8', PYTHONUNBUFFERED='1')
    command = [sys.executable, str(root/'scripts'/'verify_pypylon_emulator.py'),
               '--output', str(evidence)]
    started = time.perf_counter()
    timed_out = False
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=root, env=env, text=True, encoding='utf-8',
                                errors='replace', capture_output=True, timeout=45)
        returncode, stdout, stderr = result.returncode, result.stdout, result.stderr
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
        timed_out = True
        returncode, stdout, stderr = None, _text(exc.stdout), _text(exc.stderr)
    elapsed = time.perf_counter()-started
    (saved/f'{stem}.stdout.log').write_text(stdout, encoding='utf-8')
    (saved/f'{stem}.stderr.log').write_text(stderr, encoding='utf-8')
    if evidence.exists():
        shutil.copyfile(evidence, saved/f'{stem}.json')
    process = {'command': command, 'returncode': returncode, 'timed_out': timed_out,
               'elapsed_s': elapsed, 'evidence_created': evidence.exists(),
               'stdout_log': f'{stem}.stdout.log', 'stderr_log': f'{stem}.stderr.log',
               'physical_hardware_tested': False}
    (saved/f'{stem}.process.json').write_text(json.dumps(process, indent=2)+'\n', encoding='utf-8')
    detail = (f'Real SDK verifier returncode={returncode}, timed_out={timed_out}, elapsed={elapsed:.2f}s; '
              f'preserved evidence: {saved/f"{stem}.json"}\n'
              f'STDERR:\n{stderr[-16000:]}\nSTDOUT/partial report:\n{stdout[-18000:]}')
    assert not timed_out, detail
    assert returncode == 0, detail
    assert evidence.exists(), detail
    report = json.loads(evidence.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    assert report['status'] == 'passed', report
    assert report['sdk_emulator_only']
    assert report['physical_hardware_tested'] is False
    assert report['device']['device_class'] == 'BaslerCamEmu'
    assert {r['requested_pixel_format'] for r in report['runs']} == {'Mono8', 'Mono12', 'BGR8Packed'}
    assert all(r['buffers_owned_and_stable'] and r['closed_and_not_grabbing']
               and r['timestamps_strictly_increasing'] and r['idempotent_close'] for r in report['runs']), report

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/test_runtime_v2.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: a1c676185e930ad60e2532b49061b1487c6e3039

"""Lifecycle regressions: stale gaze, bounded buffering, camera ownership and API."""
from __future__ import annotations

from threading import Event, current_thread
import time
import numpy as np
import pytest

from gazespace.capture import FrameSource, SyntheticEyeSource
from gazespace.clock import host_now_s
from gazespace.runtime import TrackingRuntime
from gazespace.types import Frame, GazeResult, Observation
from gazespace.service import GazeService, create_api


class FakeEngine:
    def __init__(self, delay=0):
        self.delay = delay
        self.last_observation = None
        self.count = 0
        self.closed_by = None
        self.created_by = current_thread().name

    def process(self, image, timestamp_s):
        if self.delay:
            time.sleep(self.delay)
        self.count += 1
        self.last_observation = Observation(timestamp_s, (image.shape[1], image.shape[0]),
                                             features=np.zeros(10), quality=.9)
        return GazeResult(timestamp_s, True, "screen_gaze_estimated", "test", .9,
                          gaze_screen_px=(100, 80), gaze_screen_normalized=(.5, .5),
                          ray_origin_world_m=(0, 0, 0), ray_direction_world=(0, 0, 1),
                          capture_timestamp_s=timestamp_s)

    def metrics(self):
        return {"frames": self.count}

    def close(self):
        self.closed_by = current_thread().name


def wait_for(predicate, timeout=2):
    limit = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        if predicate():
            return
        time.sleep(.005)
    assert predicate(), "Timed out waiting for runtime state"


def test_pause_resume_stop_clears_coordinates_and_owns_engine():
    engines = []
    def factory():
        eng = FakeEngine()
        engines.append(eng)
        return eng
    run = TrackingRuntime(lambda: SyntheticEyeSource(fps=60), factory)
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda: bool(run.snapshot(False)["latest"]))
        assert run.snapshot(False)["latest"]["valid"]
        assert engines[0].created_by == "gazespace-processing"
        run.pause()
        paused = run.snapshot(False)
        assert paused["state"] == "paused"
        assert paused["latest"]["valid"] is False
        assert paused["latest"]["gaze_screen_px"] is None
        assert paused["latest"]["ray_direction_world"] is None
        run.resume()
        wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["state"] == "running")
        assert run.snapshot(False)["latest"]["valid"]
    finally:
        assert run.stop()
    assert engines[0].closed_by == "gazespace-processing"
    assert run.snapshot(False)["latest"]["valid"] is False


def test_mailbox_drops_backlog_and_does_not_grow():
    run = TrackingRuntime(lambda: SyntheticEyeSource(fps=300), lambda: FakeEngine(.04))
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["metrics"]["processed_frames"] >= 4)
        metrics = run.snapshot(False)["metrics"]
        assert metrics["dropped_frames"] > 5
        assert metrics["received_frames"] > metrics["processed_frames"]
    finally:
        run.stop()


def test_camera_disconnect_invalidates_before_retry():
    class Disconnecting(FrameSource):
        is_live = True
        def __init__(self): self.seq = 0
        def read(self):
            self.seq += 1
            time.sleep(.02)
            if self.seq > 4:
                raise OSError("USB disconnected")
            return Frame(np.zeros((120,160,3),np.uint8),host_now_s(),self.seq)
        def close(self): pass
    run = TrackingRuntime(Disconnecting, FakeEngine, reconnect_delay_s=.3)
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["state"] == "running")
        wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["state"] == "reconnecting")
        snap = run.snapshot(False)
        assert not snap["latest"]["valid"]
        assert snap["latest"]["gaze_screen_px"] is None
        assert "USB disconnected" in snap["last_error"]
    finally:
        run.stop()


def test_stale_frame_is_invalid_even_when_read_is_blocked():
    released = Event()
    class Stalling(FrameSource):
        def __init__(self): self.seq=0
        def read(self):
            self.seq += 1
            if self.seq > 1:
                released.wait(2)
                return None
            return Frame(np.zeros((120,160,3),np.uint8),host_now_s(),1)
        def request_stop(self): released.set()
        def close(self): pass
    run=TrackingRuntime(Stalling,FakeEngine,max_age_s=.04)
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda: bool(run.snapshot(False)["latest"]))
        time.sleep(.07)
        snap=run.snapshot(False)
        assert snap["latest"]["reason"]=="stale_capture"
        assert not snap["latest"]["valid"]
        assert snap["observation"] is None
    finally:
        run.stop()


def test_blocked_stop_does_not_cross_thread_release_camera():
    entered, release=Event(), Event()
    sources=[]
    class Blocking(FrameSource):
        def __init__(self): self.closed=False;sources.append(self)
        def read(self): entered.set();release.wait(2);return None
        def close(self): self.closed=True;self.closed_by=current_thread().name
    run=TrackingRuntime(Blocking,FakeEngine)
    run.start()
    assert entered.wait(1)
    assert run.stop(timeout=.02) is False
    assert not sources[0].closed
    assert run.snapshot(False)["state"]=="stopping_blocked"
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError): run.start()
    release.set()
    assert run.stop(timeout=1)
    assert sources[0].closed
    assert sources[0].closed_by=="gazespace-acquisition"


def test_file_eof_clears_last_gaze():
    run=TrackingRuntime(lambda:SyntheticEyeSource(duration_s=.15,fps=60),FakeEngine)
    run.start()
    wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["state"]=="exhausted")
    snap=run.snapshot(False)
    assert snap["latest"] is None or not snap["latest"]["valid"]
    assert run.stop()


def test_frame_hook_handles_depth_capable_engines():
    class FrameEngine(FakeEngine):
        called=False
        def process_frame(self,frame):
            self.called=True
            return super().process(frame.image,frame.timestamp_s)
    engine=FrameEngine()
    run=TrackingRuntime(lambda:SyntheticEyeSource(),lambda:engine)
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda: engine.called)
    finally:
        run.stop()


def test_callback_errors_are_reported_without_killing_capture():
    def bad_callback(*_): raise OSError("disk full")
    run=TrackingRuntime(lambda:SyntheticEyeSource(),FakeEngine,on_result=bad_callback)
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["metrics"]["callback_error"] is not None)
        snap=run.snapshot(False)
        assert snap["state"]=="running"
        assert "disk full" in snap["metrics"]["callback_error"]
    finally:
        run.stop()


def test_api_real_http_websocket_auth_and_origin_rules():
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    run=TrackingRuntime(lambda:SyntheticEyeSource(),FakeEngine)
    token="test-only-secret-value-32-chars"
    app=create_api(run,token=token,manage_lifecycle=True)
    with TestClient(app,base_url="http://localhost") as client:
        assert client.get("/health").status_code==401
        headers={"Authorization":"Bearer "+token}
        wait_for(lambda: run.snapshot(False)["state"]=="running")
        assert client.get("/health",headers=headers).json()["ok"]
        payload=client.get("/v1/gaze",headers=headers)
        assert payload.status_code==200
        assert payload.headers["cache-control"]=="no-store"
        assert "frame" not in payload.json() and "observation" not in payload.json()
        assert client.get("/v1/gaze",headers={**headers,"Origin":"https://untrusted.example"}).status_code==403
        assert client.get("/v1/gaze",headers={**headers,"Host":"attacker.example"}).status_code==403
        with client.websocket_connect("ws://localhost/v1/stream",headers=headers) as ws:
            data=ws.receive_json()
            assert data["latest"]["valid"]
        run.pause()
        assert client.get("/v1/gaze",headers=headers).json()["latest"]["valid"] is False
    assert not run.snapshot(False)["alive"]


def test_legacy_service_recording_is_explicit_and_json_only(tmp_path):
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    path=tmp_path/"metadata.jsonl"
    service=GazeService(source_factory=lambda:SyntheticEyeSource(fps=40),
                        engine_factory=FakeEngine,record_jsonl=str(path))
    service.start()
    wait_for(lambda:path.exists() and path.stat().st_size>0)
    assert service.stop()
    import json
    rows=[json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()]
    assert rows and "gaze_screen_px" in rows[0]
    assert "image" not in rows[0]


def test_paused_finite_video_does_not_run_to_eof():
    run=TrackingRuntime(lambda:SyntheticEyeSource(duration_s=.3,fps=60),FakeEngine)
    try:
        run.start()
        wait_for(lambda:run.snapshot(False)["state"]=="running")
        run.pause()
        time.sleep(.35)
        assert run.snapshot(False)["state"]=="paused"
        run.resume()
        wait_for(lambda:run.snapshot(False)["state"]=="running")
    finally:
        run.stop()

===== END FILE: tests/test_runtime_v2.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/test_validation_evidence.py =====
SOURCE_COMMIT: 92579983a1f672c39fb742556d3c10ed74a8e62a
SOURCE_BLOB_SHA: 78cf8d8086caa6e2b5fec4640c3e7475a9e169a2

"""Evidence eligibility checks with explicit mathematical test fixtures."""
from copy import deepcopy

import numpy as np
import pytest

from gazespace.calibration import fit_gaze_model
from gazespace.types import CameraExtrinsics
from gazespace.validation import validate_gaze_records


def fixture(kind='screen', *, synthetic=False):
    context = {'subject_id': 'person-A', 'camera_id': '0',
               'backend': 'synthetic' if synthetic else 'dshow'}
    rows = []
    if kind == 'screen':
        targets = [(x, y) for y in (.2, .5, .8) for x in (.1, .5, .9)]
        context['screen_size'] = [1920, 1080]
    else:
        targets = [(x, y, z) for z in (1., 1.3) for y in (-.15, .15) for x in (-.2, 0., .2)]
    for index, target in enumerate(targets):
        for repeat in range(4):
            x, y = target[:2]
            row = {'features': [.3+.4*x, .3+.2*y, .3+.4*x, .3+.2*y, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
                   'target_id': str(index), 'session_id': 'training-session',
                   'time_s': 10+index+repeat*.04, 'quality': .9,
                   'context': deepcopy(context),
                   'acquisition': {'settle_seconds': .6, 'stable_duration_s': .12,
                                   'target_assumption': 'user_instructed_fixation'}}
            if kind == 'screen':
                row['target'] = list(target)
            else:
                origin = np.array([0., 0., .6])
                direction = np.asarray(target)-origin
                row.update(target=(direction/np.linalg.norm(direction)).tolist(),
                           target_world_m=list(target), eye_origin_camera_m=origin.tolist())
            rows.append(row)
    model = fit_gaze_model(rows, kind=kind, context=context, session_id='training-session',
                           screen_size=(1920, 1080) if kind == 'screen' else None,
                           do_group_validation=False)
    # This supplies expected static feature version/head conventions in these
    # mathematical fixtures; production callers use their observed tracker context.
    current = {**model.metadata, **context}
    fresh = deepcopy(rows)
    for index, row in enumerate(fresh):
        row['session_id'] = 'fresh-session'
        row['time_s'] += 100
        # Different acquired feature measurements, not merely renamed labels.
        row['features'][0] += (index % 4 + 1) * .0002
        row['features'][2] += (index % 4 + 1) * .0002
    return model, rows, fresh, current


def test_renamed_exact_training_arrays_are_rejected():
    model, training, _, context = fixture()
    reused = deepcopy(training)
    for row in reused:
        row['session_id'] = 'renamed-session'
        row['target_id'] = 'renamed-' + row['target_id']
        row['time_s'] += 500
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='exact model training observations'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, reused, context=context, session_id='renamed-session')


def test_spatial_training_digest_uses_same_normalization_as_fit():
    model, rows, _, context = fixture('spatial')
    for row in rows:
        row['session_id'] = 'renamed-session'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='exact model training observations'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, rows, context=context, session_id='renamed-session')


def test_conflicting_per_record_subject_is_rejected():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    fresh[-1]['context']['subject_id'] = 'person-B'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='conflicting context: subject_id'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')


def test_row_context_cannot_change_an_unbound_capture_setting():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    fresh[0]['context']['mirror'] = False
    fresh[1]['context']['mirror'] = True
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='conflicting context: mirror'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')


def test_per_record_session_cannot_disagree_with_validation_session():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    fresh[4]['session_id'] = 'training-session'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='acquisition session_id'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')


def test_one_sample_retains_metrics_but_is_diagnostic_only():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh[:1], context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['raw_metrics_computed'] is True
    assert report['screen_error']['count'] == 1
    assert report['independent_session'] is False
    assert report['validation_status'] == 'diagnostic_only'
    assert report['acceptance_eligible'] is False
    assert report['acceptance_passed'] is None
    assert 'fewer_than_nine_distinct_targets' in report['coverage']['issues']
    assert 'fewer_than_four_samples_per_target' in report['coverage']['issues']


def test_duplicate_target_geometry_cannot_gain_coverage_by_relabeling():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    for row in fresh:
        row['target'] = [.5, .5]
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['target_count'] == 9
    assert report['coverage']['distinct_target_geometry'] == 1
    assert not report['independent_session'] and not report['acceptance_eligible']


def test_complete_fresh_measurement_has_no_automatic_accuracy_pass():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['independent_session'] is True
    assert report['coverage']['sufficient'] is True
    assert report['provenance']['issues'] == []
    assert report['validation_status'] == 'independent_measurements_available'
    assert report['acceptance_eligible'] is True
    assert report['acceptance_status'] == 'pending_user_agreed_criteria'
    assert report['acceptance_criteria'] is None and report['acceptance_passed'] is None
    assert report['physical_accuracy_verified'] is False


def test_missing_acquisition_metadata_never_establishes_independence_from_id_alone():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    for row in fresh:
        for key in ('session_id', 'time_s', 'acquisition', 'context'):
            row.pop(key)
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['screen_error']['count'] == 36
    assert not report['independent_session']
    assert report['validation_status'] == 'diagnostic_only'
    assert 'per_record_acquisition_session_missing' in report['provenance']['issues']


def test_malformed_settling_metadata_keeps_diagnostic_report_json_safe():
    import json
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    fresh[0]['acquisition']['settle_seconds'] = float('nan')
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['provenance']['settled_acquisition_recorded'] is False
    assert report['validation_status'] == 'diagnostic_only'
    assert not report['acceptance_eligible']
    json.dumps(report, allow_nan=False)


def test_synthetic_provenance_survives_valid_fresh_measurement():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture(synthetic=True)
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['independent_session'] is True
    assert report['synthetic'] is True
    assert report['measurement_provenance'] == 'synthetic_software_fixture'
    assert report['acceptance_status'] == 'synthetic_software_only'
    assert not report['acceptance_eligible']
    assert report['physical_accuracy_verified'] is False


def test_duplicate_observation_timestamps_are_rejected():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    fresh[1]['time_s'] = fresh[0]['time_s']
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='unique and increasing'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')


def test_missing_training_digest_leaves_metrics_unverified():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    del model.metadata['training_digest']
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')
    assert report['screen_error']['count'] == len(fresh)
    assert not report['independent_session']
    assert report['provenance']['exact_training_digest_reused'] is None
    assert 'model_training_digest_unavailable' in report['provenance']['issues']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field,value', [('features', [float('nan')]*10), ('target', [.2, .4, .8])])
def test_malformed_numeric_records_cannot_produce_accepted_metrics(field, value):
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture()
    fresh[0][field] = value
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='feature|target|dimensions'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session')


def test_spatial_fresh_geometry_reports_ray_miss_without_claiming_depth_accuracy():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture('spatial')
    report = validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session',
                                   extrinsics=CameraExtrinsics(np.eye(3), np.zeros(3)))
    assert report['independent_session'] is True
    assert report['coverage']['volumetric_target_coverage'] is True
    assert report['spatial_geometry_complete'] is True
    assert report['target_ray_perpendicular_miss_mm']['count'] == len(fresh)
    assert report['acceptance_passed'] is None


def test_spatial_direction_cannot_disagree_with_its_physical_target():
    model, _, fresh, context = fixture('spatial')
    fresh[0]['target_world_m'][0] += .2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='direction conflicts'):
        validate_gaze_records(model, fresh, context=context, session_id='fresh-session',
                               extrinsics=CameraExtrinsics(np.eye(3), np.zeros(3)))
